 Catalog number : I00039
Uniform title: Sakalāgamasārasaṅgraha
Manuscript : IFP manuscript bundle RE22716
Description:
RE22716 - This manuscript bundle titled Sakalāgamasārasaṅgrahaḥ, is a comprehensive compilation of different topics from various Āgamas and allied texts. It encompasses nearly 70 to 75 subjects, including the Tripurasundarīkavaca (a protective hymn of Tripurasundarī), Jīrṇoddhāraṇavidhi (restoration rituals), and Pratiṣṭhotsavavidhi (consecration ceremonies) for different deities. Additionally, it includes Śāstā-mantram (mantras of Śāstā), various types of Nyāsam (ritualistic placements), and other significant practices derived from texts like the Raurava, Ajita Bhīmasaṃhitā, Suprabheda, Vīratantra, Kāraṇa, Aṃśumāntantra, Dīpta, Vātula, and even references to Āyurveda.

The bundle contains 298 folios in 350 mm x 25 mm size and there are 8-9 lines in each folio. This manuscript is a valuable resource for scholars and practitioners interested in the ritualistic, philosophical, and practical aspects of Śaivāgama traditions. It covers many topics from multiple Āgamas which makes this bundle a treasure trove of knowledge for understanding the diversity and depth of Śaiva practices.

kṛttikāvrataudyāpanam (l.1) 
athā tat pravakṣyāmi kṛttikodyāpanaṃ param । 
kārtikeyasya pūjāyāṃ phalaṃ cāpi viśeṣataḥ ॥
udyāpanavidhiṃ caiva āgamokta*vidhānataḥ ।* (l.2)
kṛttikārakṣamākhyātaṃ yeta...tamanuttamam ॥
yadā tu jāyate bhakti vrataṃ śraddhayāyutaṃ punaḥ ।
etadvacana*mālocya vratodyāpanamācaret ॥* (l.3)
udyāpanaṃ vinā naiva vratasya phalamaśnute ।
subrahmahya(ṇya)sya bhakteṣu gṛhītvā *dravyamādarāt ॥* (l.4)
vrataṃ samāpa yatnena kṛṣṇī(ṇe)naiva samāpayet ।
puṇyatīrtheṣu ya snānaṃ kartā ācāryamūrtipān ॥ (l.5)
ātmānaṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā nirāhāraṃ viśeṣataḥ ।
pratyekaṃ pratimāṃ kṛtvā pratikumbhaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
evaṃ svarṇamayaṃ devam *aṅguladvayavistaram ।* (p.12a, l.1)
samāgate tu sāyāhne snātvā śuddhodakena vai ॥
dhautavastreṇa saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) śuklamālyā vibhūṣitam । (l.2)
uddhūlanaṃ tripuṇḍraṃ ca akṣamālā samāyutam ।
udyāne bilvamūle vā ramyadeśe śivālaye ॥
taddeśasyāgrataḥ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ *caturaśrakam ।* (l.3)
daśadvādaśahastaṃ vā darpaṇodarasannibham ॥
stambhai(bhaiḥ) ṣoḍaśabhiryuktaṃ catu(tuḥ) dvādaśasaṃyutam ।
alaṅkṛtya vidhanaiśca *toraṇaiśca dhvajaistathā ॥* (l.4)
dīpaiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalaiḥ pūgaiḥ aṅkurairaṣṭamaṅgalaiḥ ।
kṛtvā navapadaṃ madhye *padamekamathāpi vā ॥* (l.5)
hastamātraṃ samutsedhaḥ ratnimātramathāpi vā ।
diśāsu caturaśrāṇi vṛttāni vidiśāsu vai ॥
diśāsu *padmakuṇḍaṃ vā vidiśā vṛttakuṇḍakam ।* (p.12b, f.9a, l.1)
indraīśānayormadhye ṣaḍaśraṃ ca pradhānakam ॥
navāgnirvātha pañcāgni ekāgnirvā vidhānataḥ । (l.2)
gomayā lepanaṃ kṛtvā śoṣaṇaṃ pācanaṃ tathā ।
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu paryagnikaraṇaṃ tathā ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayet kṛtvā *punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret ।* (l.3)
prāṇānāyamya saṃkalpya ācāryaṃ varayedvijam ॥
śrotriyāśca suśīlaṃ ca kuḍumbinam *arogiṇam ।* (l.4)
daridraṃ śāntamanasaṃ sapatnīkaṃ dṛḍau(ḍhau) vratau ॥
vedādhyayanasampannaṃ śivapūjāparāyaṇam ।
brahmāṇaṃ *tādṛśaṃ vipraṃ varayet karmako vratam ॥* (l.5)
ācāryo ṛtvijārthaṃ ca varayeddeśikottamaḥ ।
kṛttikā śubhanakṣatre nirāhāro *bhaveddivā ॥* (p.13a, l.1)
vedikopari saṅkalpya aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ ।
tadardhaṃ taṇḍailaiścaiva tadardhaiśca tilairapi ॥

sasūtrān sāpidhānāṃśca savastrān sahiraṇyakān ॥
cūtapallavasaṃyuktān *nālikerasamanvitam ।* (l.3)
ekaniṣkapramāṇena sauvarṇaṃ pratimākṛtim ॥
ādhāraśaktimārabhya skandakumbhaṃ samarcayet ।
śaktidvaya*samāyuktam aṣṭavidyeśvarānvitam ॥* (l.4)

svayaṃ svavedikāṃ pārśve sukhāsīnaparigrahaḥ ॥ (p.13b, f.10, l.1)
skanda ? pūjā prakartavyaṃ paritopasthānaṃ (de)śikaḥ ।

himatoyaṃ śuddhatoyaṃ nānāvastrasamanvitam ॥
śvetārkaṃ droṇapuṣpaṃ ca dhūrdhūraṃ girimallikā ।
jātīcamba(mpa)ka*punnāgaṃ bilvapatraṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.3)

naivedyaṃ ca *bhavettatra upacāraiḥ purassaram ॥* (p.14a, l.1)
ācāryo ṛtvijaścaiva anye brāhmaṇapuṅgavaḥ ।
saṃhitā padamantraṃ ca varṇamantraṃ kalā *japam ॥* (l.2)
tattvaṃ ca bhuvanaṃ caiva caturvedaparāyaṇam ।
pañcākṣaraṃ pañcamantraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pañcadaśākṣaram ॥
ṣaḍā(ḍa)kṣaraṃ *pradhānaṃ ca mayūradvayadevikam ।* (l.3)
dvitīyayāme saṃpūjya bilvaiḥ sahasranāmabhiḥ ॥
śatāṣṭanāmabhirvidvān yāme caiva *tṛtīyake ।* (l.4)
pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgaiśca yāme caiva caturthake ॥
raktaśāścai(ścaiva) piṣṭena dīpapātraṃ sadaṇḍakam ।
pārśvadvaye *yathāśakti sthāpayedrājyasaṃyutam ॥* (l.5)
dīpānāṃ ropayet bhaktyā sahastenāvṛtī tathā ।
pradakṣiṇyādikurvīta ṣaṭtriṃśa*saṅkhyayā tathā ॥* (p.14b, l.1)
namaskāraṃ tu tatsaṅkhyā japastotraṃ samācaret ।
yāmayāmasya pūjānte homakarmaṃ(rma) samācaret ॥
agnyādhānā[di]kaṃ *sarvam agnikāryoktamācaret ।* (l.2)
sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā pratiyāmaṃ hunetkramāt ॥
evaṃ caturṣu yāmeṣu kuryāddhomaṃ *pradhānataḥ ।* (l.3)
pūrṇāhutiṃ ca śirasā sarvadravyeṇa homayet ॥
jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛśca kramāddhunet ।
evaṃ jāka(ga)raṇaṃ *kṛtvā nṛttagītaiśca vādyakaiḥ ॥* (l.4)
purāṇairvividhai(dhaiḥ) stotrai(traiḥ) prabhāte vimale sati ।
snānaṃ kṛtvā śucībhūtvā pūrvavat *pūjayedguham ॥* (l.5)
deśikaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ sārthaṃ kṛtvā niyatamānasaḥ ।
pādau hastau ca prakṣālya vedikāyāṃ udaṅmukhaḥ ॥ (p.15a, l.1)
śuddhā.....necān saṃsthāpya namaskāraṃ vi[dhāya] ca ।
tattatpratimavāsāṃsi dhānyataṇḍulaśālibhiḥ ॥
sadakṣiṇāṃ satāmbūlaṃ pūjayitvā viśeṣataḥ । (l.2)
tebhyaḥ tebhya(yaḥ) pradātavyaṃ paścāddānaṃ samācaret ॥
deśikaiḥ brāhmaṇairvipraiḥ yajamāno'bhiṣecayet ।
śuddhavastraṃ tripuṇḍraṃ *ca rudrākṣadhāraṇaṃ param ॥* (l.3)
daśadānaṃ viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇān bhojayet kramāt ।
devatārādhanaṃ kṛtvā *...to māheśvarārcanam ॥* (l.4)
tasya ca svasvagotri ca nāmādisamudīripan ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjyā(ya) bhāvayet subrama...hyakam ॥ (l.5)
yāgopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ deśikāya pradāpayet ।
āyurārogyavijayaṃ dhanadhānyasamṛddhita(da)m ॥
evaṃ yaḥ kurute *martyā sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।* (p.15b, f.12a, l.1)
॥ iti kumāratantre kriyāpāde kṛttikānakṣatrodyāpanavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥ (l.2)

tadviśākhāya vidmahe śaktihastāya dhīmahī tanno skanda(ndaḥ) pracodayāt । itīmamāriṣṭanivṛtyartham abhīṣṭaphala(l.3)siddhaye । ta(da)dāmyarghyaṃ guhāyedaṃ ....ṇaṃ prathamā bhavet । jñānamūrtigurusvāmidevasenāni (l.4) nāyakā(ka) । idamarghya(rghyaṃ) pradātavyaṃ dvitīyaṃ saṃprakīrtitam । rogādibhayadāridraṃ pāpa.... apamṛtyu vā bhayakrodha(p.16a, l.1)manakleśaṃ idamarghyaṃ guhāya ca ॥ 3 ॥ āyurārogyavijayaṃ putrapautravivardhanam । idamarghyaṃ gṛhāṇaṃ ca devasenāpatistathā ॥ 4 ॥ (remaining portion blank, p.16b, 13a, kṛttikāvrataudyāpanam, l.1)

dā.....vidhinā kena kartavyaṃ kārtikeyavrataṃ mahat ।
udyāpanaṃ ca vidhivat tadācakṣa mamānagha ॥

tattat svanāmamantreṇa pūjayedupacāraka ॥
tato homaṃ samārabhya samidājyānnalājakaiḥ ।
saktukṣīraṃ tilaṃ mudgaṃ *kṣaudraṃ māṃsam apūpakam ॥* (l.2)
dravyāṇyekādaśaitāni brahmāṅgaiścaiva hūyate ।
aṣṭādhikaśataṃ vāpi tadardhaṃ vārdhameva vā ॥
pratyekaṃ *juhuyānmantraṃ dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hunet ।* (l.3)
diśāsvadhyānaṃ kuryānmūlamantrasamāyutam ॥
evaṃ dinaṃ prakartavyaṃ prabhāte *sthāpanaṃ kuru ।* (l.4)
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā bā(pā)likaṃ tu visarjayet ॥
uddhṛtya śayyayā de.... vastrādīni vyapohya ca ।
śailaṃ *cet snānavedyāṃ tu calaṃ garbhagehake ॥* (l.5)
praviśya mūlamantreṇa vidhivat pūjayet kramāt ।
kṣetreśaṃ sthāpayedvāme tasya *mūlamanusmaran ॥* (p.24b, f.23a, l.1)
krameṇoddhṛtya kumbhāni kuryāddhāmapradakṣiṇam ।
sthāpayet tasya pūrve tu pradhānādikrameṇa tu ॥ (l.2)
kumbhādbījaṃ samādāya pratime hṛdi vinyaset ।
tatkumbhajalenaiva sthāpayet kṣetrapālakam ॥
vardhanyāṃ *bījamādāya prakṣatiṃ hṛdi vinyaset ।* (l.3)
tenodakena tadbimbaṃ kārayedabhiṣecakam ॥
lokeśānparitassthāpya *dhāmabāhye pradarśake ।* (l.4)
dvāre vinyasedvāre kṣetrasenaṃ tu śāṅkare ॥
puruśca kukkuruṃ sthāpya tadbāhye *bi(ba)lipīṭhakam ।* (l.5)
piśācaṃ vinyasya teṣāṃ gandhādinārcayet ॥
arcayet kṣetrapālaṃ tu gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam । (p.25a, l.1)
dīpaṃ naivedyatāmbūlaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ darpaṇaṃ tathā ॥
hṛdayenaiva dātavyaṃ kṣetreśamevameva tu ।
parivārabaliṃ datvā *homaṃ kṛtvā tu nityaśaḥ ॥* (l.2)
utsavaṃ tatkuryānnityaṃ trikālaṃ vā dvikālakam ।
athavā ekakālaṃ tu tasyānte nṛttamācaret ॥ (l.3)
kṣetreśasya baliṃ datvā nityaṃ nāmāni saṃbhavam ।
tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā darbhaiścaiva paristaret ॥
apūpaṃ matsyamāṃsaṃ ca *kṣaudraṃ sakturlājakam ।* (l.4)
odanaṃ gandhasaṃyuktaṃ mūlenaiva baliṃ kṣipet ॥
daivanṛparāṣṭraṃ tu vardhano *nātra saṃśayaḥ ।* (l.5)
ekāhe vātha saptāhe kṣauradṛgbalaṃ tataḥ ॥
sthāpayeddvajadaṇḍaṃ tu pa......naṃ tu lekhayet । (p.25b, f.24a, l.1)
āropayeti saptāhe saptāhe vā viśeṣataḥ ॥
yogeśasthāpanaṃ kuryāt gandhapuṣpādinārcayet ।
homaṃ kuryāttadante tu *samidādikrameṇa tu ॥* (l.2)
samidhān sadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena hūyate ।
aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā gandhaṃ hutvā nareṇa tu ॥ (l.3)
pañcamena dadhiṃ hutvā apūpaṃ hṛdayena tu ।
kṣaudraṃ tu śirasā hutvā pratyekaṃ vyāhṛtiṃ hunet ॥ (l.4)
sāyaṃ prātaṃ(taḥ) baliṃ dadyāt hutvānte dravyasaṃyutam ।
śuddhānnaṃ ca tilānnaṃ ca hariṣā(drā)nnaṃ gulodakam ॥
mudgānnaṃ pāyasānnaṃ ca *māṃsānnaṃ ca krameṇa tu ।* (l.5)
saptānnameva dātavyaṃ tattanmantraṃ samuccaran ॥
prathamaṃ bhūtarātraṃ tu guṇarātraṃ tu dvitīyakam ।
pañcamaṃ gaṇanāthaṃ ca *ṣaṣṭhamaṃ rākṣasaṃ tathā ॥* (p.26a, l.1)
kṣetrapālaṃ tu sagaṇaṃ gaṇāḥ saptādhidevatāḥ ।
tasyānte cotsavaṃ kuryāt sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥ (l.2)
rākṣase divase caiva yuddhārambhaṃ prabhāvayet ।
cūrṇotsavaṃ tu saptāhe tīrthaṃ kuryāttadantake ॥
dhvajāvaro*haṇaṃ kuryāt snapanaṃ kārayettataḥ ।* (l.3)
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ॥
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ sa *puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।* (l.4)
kṣetreśasthāpanaṃ proktaṃ sūryasya sthāpanaṃ śru(śṛ)ṇu ॥
॥ iti bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ pratiṣṭhā(l.5)tantre kriyāpāde kṣetreśasthāpanavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥ (p.26b, f.25a)

॥ ravisthāpanam ॥ (l.1)
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa ravisthāpanamuttamam ।
sarvasiddhikaraṃ puṇyaṃ sarvalokahitāvaham ॥
āyurārogyavijayaṃ putrārthi(rthī) *dhanasaṃpadam ।* (l.2)
grāme vā nagare vāpi pattane rājadhānike ॥
kheṭake vā kuḍuve vā nadyāstīre taṭākake ।
āraṇye *ca gṛhe vāpi śāṅkare śaṅkarālaye ॥* (l.3)
grāṇāṇāṃ aindradeśe va aiśānye pāvake tathā ।
indrapāvakayo*rmadhye kalpayetsūryamandiram ॥* (l.4)
guṇabhūtābdhihastaṃ vā samamaṇḍapasaṃyutam ।
prākāraṃ bahi(hiḥ) saṃyuktaṃ gopureṇa *samanvitam ॥* (l.5)
tadardhe kalpayedvāraṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ bhavet ।
garbhagehavaśāt gehatoraṇairlakṣaṇaiḥ saha ॥
mṛṇmayaṃ *śailajaṃ vāpi dvāra(dāru)jaṃ dhātujaṃ tathā ।* (p.27a, l.1)
daśatālakaniṣṭhena prāgupta(kta)vidhinā kuru ॥
pratiṣṭhādivasātpūrve kārayedaṅkurārpaṇam । (l.2)
kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tasyordhave piṇḍikā nyaset ॥
grahāṇāṃ mantramuccārya navaratnāni vinyaset ।
raviṃ tasyopari *nyastvā susnigdhaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ kuru ॥* (l.3)
uṣā ca pratyuṣā caiva sauparṇau(rṇā)ni ca vinyaset ।
netramantreṇa dṛṣṭvākṣaṃ netra*yugmayute dine ॥* (l.4)
kartavyaṃ śilpināsārdhaṃ hemasūciprahārataḥ ।
madhusarpisavatsāṃgaṃ(gāṃ) tattanmantreṇa darśayet ॥
sūryabīja*manusmṛtya dhānyarāśiṃ pradarśayet ।* (l.5)
pañcamṛtpañcagavyena śuddhiṃ kṛtvā tato ravim ॥
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt ambhasya.....*dhipacet punaḥ ।* (p.27b, f.26a, l.1)
maṇḍapaṃ kuryāt saudhasya a....ravikaraṇe tu ।
ṣoḍaśasthūṇasaṃyuktaṃ sthālaṃ nāmasamacchrayam ॥ (l.2)
kṛtvāṣṭāṣṭapadaṃ madhye catuṣkoṣṭhena vidikā ।
ravyaṅgulyasamutsedhā nimnonnatavivarjitaḥ ॥
śreṣṭhe trikoṣṭha*madhye tu tadvikoṇaṃ caturdaśi ।* (l.3)
koṇaiṣpaikaikakuṇḍaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ kamalākṛti ॥
hastamātrapramāṇena *kuryāśi(cchi)lpivisarjanam ।* (l.4)
gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā puṇyāhaṃ vācaye(yet) tataḥ ॥
vāstuhomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuru । (l.5)
bhūsurān bhojayetpaścāt ucchiṣṭaṃ tu visarjayet ॥
lepayet gomayenaiva punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret । (p.28a, l.1)
maṇḍapaṃ maṇḍapet paścāt divyālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
sthaṇḍilaṃ vedikāmadhye kārayet ratnaśālibhiḥ ।
śayanaṃ kalpayet *tatra pūrvoktavidhinā saha ॥* (l.2)
jalāduttīrya sūryaṃ tu śuddhiṃ kṛtvāstramantrataḥ ।
lepayet kuṅkumaṃ raktacandanaṃ kurvisaṃyutam ॥ (l.3)
kūrcasamāyuktaṃ raktavastreṇa veṣṭayet । ?
rakṣāsūtraṃ ca badhvā ca śāyayecchayanordhvake ॥
droṇatoyaṃ prapūrṇaṃ *tu kumbhaḍāḍimisaṃbhramam ।* (l.4)
raktasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) kṣālayeddhṛdayena tu ॥
pūrayet gandhatoyena raktavastreṇa *veṣṭayet ।* (l.5)
māṇikkaṃ vinyasenmadhye kūrcaṃ tasye(sya) vinikṣipet ॥
śiraso dakṣiṇe bhāge sthāpaye sthāpakottamam । (p.28b, f.27a, l.1)
sūryarūpaṃ tato dhyātvā tanmūlaṃ tadghaṭe nyaset ॥
ṣāntāntaṃ vahnisaṃyuktaṃ dvitīyasvarasaṃyutam ।
bindunādasamā*yuktaṃ prathamaṃ bījamucyate ॥* (l.2)
pūṣavatsāṃ tu muddhṛtya vahnimāyāsamanvitam ।
oṃkāreṇa samāyuktaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamucyate ॥ (l.3)
ṣāntaṃ visargasaṃyuktaṃ tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ bījamucyate ।
ṣāntaṃ ṣaṣṭhasvaropetaṃ caturthaṃ bījamucyate ॥
māntadvaba(ya)samāyuktaṃ *dvitīyaṃ svarasaṃyutam ।* (l.4)
vahnibījasamāyuktaṃ pañcamaṃ bījamucyate ॥
bhāntāntaṃ ṣaṣṭhamaṃ bījaṃ ṣaḍakṣa(kṣara)mihocyate ।
ādau praṇava(l.5)saṃyuktaṃ namaskārāntasaṃyutam ॥
brahmahatyāni pāpāni japaṃ mantreṇa śaddha(ddhya)ti ।
nādādyena samāyuktaṃ brahmāṅgāni prakalpayet ॥ (p.29a, l.1)
mūlamantramidaṃ proktamuṣābījamataḥ śruṇu ।
avargāt pañcamaṃ grāhyā bindunādasamāyutam ॥
uṣābījamiti khyātaṃ ṣaḍvargāt *prathamaṃ grahet ।* (l.2)
vahnibījasamāyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam ॥
pratyuṣābījamākhyātaṃ tridaśairapi durlabham ।
droṇārdhatoyasaṃpūrṇān *kumbhau sūtrādibhau yutau ॥* (l.3)
tadaddhīrapradeśeṣu saurikumbhe nyaset kramāt ।
paritaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau *prāgādikramaśo nyaset ॥* (l.4)
somādiketuparyantaṃ pūjayettu krameṇa tu ।
tato homaṃ samārabhya agnikāryokta*mārgataḥ ॥* (l.5)
pradhānato mamācārya anyakuṇḍe tu mūrtipāt ।
deśikaḥ suprasannātmā śivadvijakulodbhavā ॥
raktoṣṇaṣottarīyaśca *raktāmbaradharaśuci ।* (p.29b, f.28a, l.1)
raktopavītasaṃyuktā ācāryo homamācaret ॥
samidājyacaruṃ lājān payodadhi*tilaṃ yavam ।* (l.2)
padmādivāmaparyantaṃ mantraiścaiva tu homayet ॥
vaikartano ca divākaram । 
ete vai dvādaśāditya *kuṇḍānāmadhidevatāḥ ॥* (l.3)
paiśācapadamadhye tu saptaviṃśadghaṭā(ṭān) nyasāt ।
saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyairnandinādi *prapūjayet ॥* (l.4)
śuklārka vātha raktārgha sarveṣāṃ samidhā smṛtāḥ ।
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hunet ॥
sūryalekādimantreṇa *homayecchatasaṅkhyayā ।* (l.5)
evaṃ rajani kartavyaṃ prabhāte sthāpanaṃ kuru ॥
pūrṇāhuti tato hutvā mūlamantramanusmaran । (p.30a, l.1)
uddhṛtyāśaya.......baṃ snānākhye vinyaseccalam ॥
acalaṃ garbhagehe tu devikaśca parinyaset ।
śaktidvayaṃ samā*yuktaṃ tanmadhye sthāpayedravim ॥* (l.2)
tattadbrahmaṣaḍaṅgāni taddehena tu vinyaset ।
ghaṭānuddhṛtya bimbāgre raktaśālyopari *nyaset ॥* (l.3)
kumbhānmantrān samādāyaṃ(ya) sūryaścandradi ? vinyaset ।
tattoyenābhiṣekaṃ tu kārayeddhṛdayena tu ॥ (l.4)
vinyastaśaktimantrau dvau dakṣiṇādikrameṇa tu ।
tattatkumbhasthatoyena tattanmantreṇa secayet ॥
gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ *ca dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca ।* (l.5)
tāmbūlādisamastādi tattadvarmeṇa dāpayet ॥
sthāpayet parivārāṇi somādīni *krameṇa tu ।* (p.30b, f.29a, l.1)
parivārabaliṃ nityaṃ homaṃ nityotsavaṃ kuru ॥
saukhyaṃ kuryāt tadante tu tattadṛ(ddṛ)ṣṭena karmaṇā । (l.2)
utsavaṃ kārayetpaścāt yāvatsaptadivāntakam ॥
navāhaṃ uttamaṃ proktaṃ saptāhaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ।
pañcāha *adhamaṃ proktaṃ utsavaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.3)
dhvajāditīrthaparyantaṃ kārayedvidhinā sahā(ha) ।
ācāryaṃ *pūjayetpaścāt dhānyavastrādikāñcanaiḥ ॥* (l.4) 
evaṃ yat kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sūryalokaṃ sa gacchati ।
ādityasthāpanaṃ *proktaṃ nāgeśasthāpanaṃ śruṇu ॥* (l.5)
॥ iti kāraṇopabhede bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ pratiṣṭhātantre kriyāpāde ādityasthāpanavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥

॥ nāgeśasthāpanam ॥ (l.1)
athā(tha) tatsaṃpravakṣyāmi nāgeśasthāpanaṃ param ।
sarvarogavināśārthaṃ rājarāṣṭravivardhanam ॥
dhā(yā)ntaṃ trayodaśāntaṃ ca *vahnibījasamāyutam ।* (l.2)
bindunādasamāyuktaṃ mūlamantramiti smṛtam ॥
yāntenaiva samāyuktaṃ brahmāṅgāni prakalpayet । (l.3)
avargā pañcamaṃ grāhyaṃ bindunādasamanvitam ॥
uragavyā tu mantraṃ syāt dāntātantaṃ bindusaṃyutam ।
nīgeśaṃ bīja*mākhyātaṃ sarvadeṣapraṇāśanam ॥* (l.4)
mantrāṇyeva samākhyātaṃ sthāpanaṃ cādhunocyate ।
grāmasya nairi(ṛ)te bhāge *vāyavye śāṅkare'thavā ॥* (l.5)
nadītīre goṣṭhe va(vā) nadyāstīre mahāpa.... ।
....vṛkṣasamīpe tu parvate kānane ॥ (p.32a, l.1)
gṛhe taṭākatīre vā śivadhāmasya naiṛte ।

prākārabhittisaṃyuktaṃ gopuradvārasaṃyutam ।
garbhagena(ha)pramāṇena pratimāṃ kārayettataḥ ॥ (l.4)
lohajaṃ śailajaṃ vāpi mṛṇmayaṃ vā nagena vā ।
dvinetra(traṃ) dvibhujaṃ śāntaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥

ā..........*uttamaṃ rudrasannidhau ।* (l.4)

sadanasya sama.......*rayettataḥ ॥* (l.2)
madhye kuṭṭimaṃ kuryāt vedāṅgulasamuśrayam ।
tatpūrve caturaśraṃ tu kuryāllakṣaṇasayu...... ॥ (l.3)

........*takṣakaṃ cāmbujaṃ tathā ॥* (p.33b, f.32a, l.1)
mahābjā śaṅkhapālaṃ ca kārkoṭagulikastathā ।
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ kumbhamadhye tu vinyaset ॥ (l.2)
homaṃ kuryāt tadante tu agnikāryoktamārgataḥ ।
samidājyacaruṃ lājān nālirodakaṃ tathā ॥
taṇḍulaṃ *śālipiṣṭena kṣīraṃ vai sarṣapaṃ tathā ।* (l.3)
homadravyaṃ samākhyātaṃ hṛdādīṃ puruṣāntakam ॥
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā pratyekaṃ *homayetkramāt ।* (l.4)

pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā [hṛda]yenaiva mantrataḥ ।
urageśaṃ samuddhṛtya garbhagehe vinyaset ॥
kumbhādbījaṃ samādāya tattat varme tu vinyaset ।
śaktibījaṃ samādāya tacchaktiṃ hṛdi vinyaset ॥ (p.34a, l.1)
prata(prātaḥ) haviṣaṃ datvā tāmbūlaṃ dāpayetpṛthak ।
paurṇamāsyamāvāsyāṃ pūjayedurageśvaram ॥
nālikerāmrasaṃyuktaṃ piṣṭadugdhasamāyutam । (l.2)
hṛdaye ...... prārthayettena mantrataḥ ॥
saptāhameva kurvīta pradakṣiṇasamanvitam ।
sarvavyādhiharaṃ tena iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ ॥ (l.3)
deśikaṃ pūjayetpaścāt vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ।
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ॥ (l.4)
nāgeśasthāpanaṃ proktaṃ bhaktānāṃ sthāpanaṃ śruṇu ।
॥ iti kāraṇopabhede bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ pratiṣṭhātantre kriyāpāde (l.5) nāgeśasthāpanavidhiḥ ॥ saptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ ॥ (p.34b, f.33a, l.1)

॥ bhaktasthāpanam ॥
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa bhaktānāṃ sthāpanaṃ param ।
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvadoṣapra......m ॥
sauvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi tāmbraṃ(mraṃ) *.....śailajaṃ tu vā ।* (l.2)
athavā mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi bhitticitrapaṭaṃ tu vā ॥
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktam aṣṭatālena veṣṭayet ।
uttamaṃ dvādaśā*ṅgulyaṃ jyeṣṭhamānāṅgulena tu ॥* (l.3)
ekaikabimbamevaṃ tu madhyamasyādhamasya tu ।
uttarāyaṇakāle tu śuklapakṣe śubhe dine ॥ (l.4)
śubhavārādibhiryukte sthāpanaṃ samyagācaret ।
aṅkuraṃ ratnavinyāsaṃ bimbaśuddhimataḥparam ॥
maṇḍapālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā *paritaḥ ......... ।* (l.5)
sthaṇḍilaṃ vedikāmadhye kṛtvā droṇāṣṭaśālinā ॥
śālyārdhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ caiva taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ tathā । (p.35a, l.1)
ambujaṃ lekhayenmadhye darbhaiścaiva paristaret ॥
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt prāgupta(kta)vidhinā saha ।
tato'kṣimokṣaṇaṃ *kuryāt pakṣākṣisaṃyute dine ॥* (l.2)
maṇḍape sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāt taraṅgeṇāvṛtaṃ bahi(hiḥ) ।
haimasūciprabhā ramyāṃ sthāpayet takṣakaiḥ saha ॥
ācārya *mantramuccārya sthapatiṃ karma kārayet ।* (l.3)
tāmbra(mra)pātraśarāveṇa kṣaudrasarpi(rpiḥ) tu pūrayet ॥
netramantramanusmṛtya darśaye tu *viśeṣataḥ ।* (l.4)
brahmajajñānamantreṇa brāhmaṇān darśayettataḥ ॥
gaurdhenuhavyamantreṇa savatsāṃ gaṃ(gāṃ) pradarśayet ।
śivamantramanusmṛtya *śivabhaktān pradarśayet ॥* (l.5)
aṣṭamṛtsalilenaiva snapayeddhṛdayena tu ।
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
jalādhi*vāsanaṃ kuryāt pūrvavaddeśikottamaḥ ।* (p.35b, f.34a, l.1)
dhāmasya purataḥ kuryāt maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam ॥
ṣaḍḍhastaṃ pañcahastaṃ vā saptahastamathāpi vā । (l.2)
tanmadhye sthāpayedviṣṇu dvārabhāgaikabhāgataḥ ॥
muṣṭimātraṃ samutsedhaṃ nimnonnatavivarjitam ।
tatpūrvādicaturdikṣu catuḥ kuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet ॥
vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā mekhalātrayasaṃyutam ।
udvāsya śilpinaṃ paścāt goma[ye]nopalepayet ॥ (l.4)
prokṣayecchivatoyena bhūsurān bhojayettataḥ ।
ucchiṣṭaṃ tu tatodvāsya punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret ॥
prāgupta(kta)vidhinā samyak *maṇḍapaṃ tu vibhūṣayet ।* (l.5)
vāstuhomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt prākkuṇḍe vedikāntare ॥
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuryāt puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ kuru । (p.36a, l.1)
vedyūrdhve sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāt aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālinā ॥
tadardhaistaṇḍulairbhūṣyā(ya) tadardhaiśca tilairapi ।
tanmadhye śayanaṃ *kalpya prokṣayenmantravāriṇā ॥* (l.2)
uṣiraṃ ve(be)ramudvāsya śuddhiṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat ।
lambakūrcaṃ samāyuktaṃ navavastreṇa *veṣṭayet ॥* (l.3)
aṅgu(ṅku)raṃ bandhayet paścāt pūrvoktavidhinā sahā(saha) ।
śayane śāyayedberaṃ prākchiraṃ cordhvavaktrakajam ॥ (l.4)
raktavastreṇa saṃveṣṭyā pūjayeddhṛdayena tu ।
madhyādīśānaparyantaṃ dvārakumbhāni vinyaset ॥
sūtrodakādibhiścaiva *āmrapallavaśobhitān ।* (l.5)
avargā tu caturthaṃ tu māyānalasamanvitam ॥
śivaviṣṇusamāyuktaṃ bhoktṛnā bījamuttamam । (p.36b, f.35a, l.1)
tasya rūpaṃ tato dhyātvā tanmantraṃ tadghaṭe nyaset ॥
pūjayedgandhapuṣpādyai naivedyaṃ dāpayet kramāt ।
indrādīśānaparyantaṃ *lokapālān prapūjayet ॥* (l.2)
tattanmantramanusmṛtya naivedyaṃ dāpayet pradhi(di)k ।
tato homaṃ samārabhya agnikāryokta*mārgataḥ ॥* (l.3)
stotraṃ japtvā mahādikṣu koṇeṣvastrajapet kramāt ।
samidājyena caruṇā hṛdayādiśikhāntakam ॥ (l.4)
pratyekaṃ ṣojaśaṃ kṛtvā dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hunet ।
evaṃ tu nirvi kartavyaṃ prabhāte sthāpanaṃ kuru ॥
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ *sārdhaṃ snānaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ ।* (l.5)
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā antaryāgamathācaret ॥
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā tasya mūlamanusmaran । (p.37a, l.1)
śayanāt bimbamudvāsya vastrādīni vyapohi ca ॥
vodyaṃ cottaradigbhāge sthāpayet phalakopari ।
utpalaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ citraṃ yajamāne*'ṣṭadeśake ॥* (l.2)
sthāpayeddhṛdayenaiva kuryāt ghṛtaśiro'rpaṇam ।
kumbhānuddhṛtya śirasā bāhye dhāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
kuryāt mūla*bimbaṃ purobhāge sthaṇḍile sthāpayet kramāt ।* (l.3)
kumbhādbimbaṃ samādāyā(ya) pradime hṛdi vinyaset ॥
tattat kubho(kumbho)dakai*ścaivā(va) * tattatsthāne'bhiṣecayet ।* (l.4)
tadghaṭe(ṭa)stodakenaiva snāpayet hṛdayena tu ॥
lokeśa*kumbhamādāya pīṭhe taddiśi vinyaset ।* (l.5)
snapanaṃ kārayedante yajedgandhādinā sahā(ha) ॥
prabhūtahaviṣaṃ datvā tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ । (p.37b, f.36a, l.1)
utsavaṃ kārayetpaścāt yathā vittānusārataḥ ॥
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ । (l.2)
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ॥
bhaktānāṃ sthāpanaṃ proktaṃ vimānasthāpanaṃ param ।
॥ iti kāraṇopabhede bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ kriyāpāde pratiṣṭhātantre bhaktasthāpanavidhi dvisaptatatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ ॥

॥ vimānasthāpanam ॥ (l.4)
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa vimānasthāpanaṃ param ।
sarvasiddhipradaṃ saukhyaṃ sarvaduḥkhanivāraṇam ॥
haimarājatatāmbrai(mrai)rvā *śilayeṣṭikathāpi vā ।* (l.5)
pādapai(pe)naiva vā kuryāt vimānaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam ॥
vimānasthāpanahīne tena haramyeṇa niṣphalam । (p.38a, l.1)
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena prāsādaṃ sthāpanaṃ kuru ॥
uttarāyaṇakāle tu pūrvapakṣe śubhe dine ।
suvāre sumuhūrte tu vidhivadaṅkurārpaṇam । (l.2)
sthāpanaṃ samyagācaret ॥ 
taddināt pūrvakāle tu vidhivadaṅkurārpaṇam ।
ṣaṇmukhaṃ savyamūrtiṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ saroruhodbhavam ॥ (l.3)
netradvayayute rikṣe kārayedakṣimokṣaṇam ।
ūrdhvādīni ca sarvāṇi pūrvoktavidhinā dṛśet ॥
toyādhi*vāsanaṃ kuryāt tattadagre prajāpate ।* (l.4)
ḍiṇḍimaṃ tu pṛthak kuryāt darbhaiścaiva paristaret ॥
pratibimbayute tatra *sthāpayet darpaṇāddhṛtā ।* (l.5)
tripādyupari kumbhāni sthāpaye tu caturdaśi ॥
tattat pṛthak suparṇena nālaṃ kṛtvā tu varjayet । (p.38b, f.37a, l.1)
tannālāt santataṃ toyaṃ sthāpayet darpaṇopari ॥
jalādhivāsanaṃ hyeva maṇḍapaṃ kārayettataḥ । 
dhāmasya purataḥ kuryāt *maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam ॥* (l.2)
ravihastaṃ rudrahastaṃ saptahastamathāpi vā ।
ṣaḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ sthaloccaṃ caturaśrakam ॥ (l.3)
paṅktitrayaṃ samāyuktaṃ paṅktimānamathocchrayam ।
dvitālanaṃ mahā nāma bhūmau hastaṃ niveśayet ॥
śaktaścaiva anantaśca pāvakaḥ *sūkṣmameva ca ।* (l.4)
yamaścaiva śivaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rākṣasaṃ caikanetrakaḥ ॥
varuṇaścaikarudraśca vāyuścaiva trimūrtikaḥ । (l.5)
rohiṇī vallabhaścaiva śrīkaṇṭho dhana eva ca ॥
śikhaṇḍī caiva vikhyātā stambhanamadhidevatāḥ ।
valayāścaturyugāścaiva alakeṣvarka*devatāḥ ॥* (p.39a, l.1)
śaṅkhāni ādhidevatyamaraṇḍapāṅgādhidevatāḥ । ?
maṇḍapaṃ śatadhā bhajya madhye viṣṇuṃ catuṣpadam ॥
tatpūrvādicaturdikṣu veda*vedāṅgakaṃ tyajet ।* (l.2)
tatpūrve yugabhāge tu tataḥ kuryāttu vedikām ॥
dakṣiṇe paścime saumye tadva(dvat) siṣyaṃ tu kārayet । (l.3)
pradhānakuṭṭimotsedhau hastamātrapramāṇataḥ ।
anyeṣāṃ dvādaśāṅgulyaṃ darpaṇodarasannibham ॥
antarvedyāścaturdikṣu catuḥ *kuṇḍāni kārayet ।* (l.4)
rasāśraṃ pūrvadigbhāge aśraṃ caiva tu dakṣiṇe ॥
paścime parvatāśraṃ tu padmākāraṃ tu saumyake । (l.5)
śāṅkare samavṛttaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ hastamātrakam ॥
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ maṇḍapaṃ bhūṣayettataḥ ।
trimekhalāsamāyuktaṃ kārayellakṣaṇā*nvitam ॥* (p.39b, l.1)
udvāsya śilpinaṃ paścāt gomayenānulepayet ।
brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ ॥ (l.2)
samaryakapaṭe samyak vāstuhomaṃ tu kārayet ।
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuryāt punaḥ puṇyāhamācaret ॥
homādisthūpiparyantaṃ *navavastreṇa veṣṭayet ।* (l.3)
darbhamālāsamāyuktaṃ tantunā veṣṭayettataḥ ॥
madhye pūrve tathā yāmye paścime cottare tathā । (l.4)
sthaṇḍilaṃ kārayedvidvān tattat vedyopari nyaset ॥
madhyame sitaśālīṃ tu raktaśālīṃ tu pūrvake ।
dakṣiṇe tu mahāśāli paścime *sthāpakaḥ suprasannātmā śivadvijakulodbhavaḥ ।* (p.40a, l.1)
navavastradhṛtoṣṇīṣaśuklavastrottarīyakaḥ pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇairyukta*gandhapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam ॥* (l.2)
madhyame suparisthāpya śivakumbhaṃ ca vardhanī ।
sa rudrau sāpidhānau ca vastrahemasamāyutau ॥ (l.3)
va ? kūrcau gandhasaṃyuktaṃ śuddhatoyena pūritau ।
sadāśivaṃ yajenmadhye vardhanyāṃ tu manonmanīm ।
parito'ṣṭa*ghaṭānyaṣṭau vastrakūrcādisaṃyutān ॥* (l.4)
caṇḍarudro mahāvego vibudho vimalottamaḥ ।
viśvarūpo *virūpākṣo bhūdharo mekha(gha)vāhanaḥ ॥* (l.5)
etān vimānapālāśca pūrvādikalaśānnyaset । (p.40b, f.39a, l.1)
pūrvavedyupari skandakumbha(bhaṃ) nyastvā svaśaktikam ॥
vinyastvā vasukumbhāni tatpūrvānyaṣṭadikṣu ca ।
skandaṃ ca va kumāraṃ ca subrahmaṇyaṃ tathaiva ca ॥ (l.2)
kārtikeyaṃ ca senānīṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ śikhivāhanam ।
senāpatiṃ ca ityetān pārvādīni kramānnyaset ॥
umāmaheśvarakumbhau *tu savye vedyopari nyaset ।* (l.3)
tatpūrvādyaṣṭakumbhe tu vidyeśāt parite(to) yajet ॥
paścādvedyopari nyastvā viṣṇukumbhaṃ *svaśaktikam ।* (l.4)
tatpūrvādyaṣṭakumbhe tu acyutādikramānnyaset ॥
adyuto nabhamūrtiśca cakrapāṇiṃ janārdanam ।
puruṣaścaiva govindo *nārāyaṇatrivikramaḥ ॥* (p.41a, l.1)
brahmakumbhaṃ tu tacchaktiṃ saumyavedyopari nyaset ।
tatpūrvādyaṣṭakumbhe tu vidhātāraṃ ........... ॥ (l.2)
prajāpatiśca dhātāraṃ sṛṣṭiṃ caiva caturmukham ।
ajaṃ hiraṇgarbhaṃ ca vinyasetu(ttu) kramānnyaset ॥
saputrān sāpidhānāṃ ca sakūrcān *sahiraṇyakān ।* (l.3)
sodakā gandhasaṃyuktān tattanmantrasamāyutam ।
dhyāyet tatra svarūpaṃ ca gandhapratimādvayet ॥ ?(l.4)
naivedyaṃ dāpayetpaścāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ ।
deśiko mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ .....ddhāmaṃ samācaret ॥
diśāsvadhyayanaṃ *kuryāt koṇe caiva japaṃ kuru ।* (p.41b, f.40a, l.1)
mayūraṃ pūrvadigbhāge vaṭavṛkṣaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ।
..............*māgniṃ tu saumyake ॥* (l.2)
śivāgniṃ śaṅkare deśe kalpayet kalpavittamaḥ ।
samidājya...........*vaṃ tilam ॥* (l.3)
sarṣapaṃ yavaṃ mudgaṃ śrīparṇaṃ ca krameṇa tu ।
tattadbrhmaṣaḍaṅgaiśca tattatkuṇḍeṣu homa...... ॥
....vā *tadardhaṃ vā tasyārdhaṃ vāpi homayet ।* (l.4)
dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā dravyaṃ pratigṛhaṃ spṛśet ॥
evaṃ rātrau.......*bhāte vimale tataḥ ।* (p.42a, l.1)
sthāpako mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ snānaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat ॥
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ...... *devatam ।* (l.2)

..........*saṃprapūjyātha pūrvoktavidhinā sahā(ha) ॥* (l.3)
kumbhān kuṇḍāni nasaṃpūjya tattanmantreṇa deśikaḥ ।
......*naṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛt cakramāvanet ? ॥* (l.4)
pūrṇātiṃ tato hutvā tattanmūlamanusmaran ।
sviṣṭa.........*yet ॥* (p.42b, f.41a, l.1)
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarva(rvā)todyasamāyutam ।

......*sumuhūrte tu sthāpanaṃ tu samārabhet ।* (l.2)

.......*padmamadhye tu vinyaset ।* (l.3)

tattat ku..........*bhiṣecayet ।* (l.4)

॥ maṇḍapasthāpanam ॥ (l.1)
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa maṇḍapasthāpanaṃ param ।
pūrvoktavidhinā.............(l.2)
...bṛjyātha puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ ।
niścityadivasātpūrve kāraye......... ॥ (l.3)
.........naṃ samyagācaret ।
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ maṇḍapaṃ tu vibhūṣayet ॥
......................................*yettataḥ ।* (l.4)
taddināt pūrvarātrau tu kārayedadhivāsanam ॥
prathamastmbhamū........................... । (broken folio, p.44a, l.1)
........devatyaṃ .......ṣāṇi rudradaivatam ॥
navavastreṇa saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) gandhapuṣpādinā ..... ।
...................................*ṇḍilaṃ kuru ॥* (l.2)
tasya madhye likhedabjaṃ darbhaiścaiva paristaret ।
brahma.............................................. ॥
....*taḥ kalaśānaṣṭau sthāpitaṃ samyagācaret ।* (l.3)
gandhapuṣpaṃ samāyuktam aṣṭa.............. ॥
..............*ni(na)rmadā ca sarasvatī ।* (l.4)
sindhurgodāvarī caiva kāverī sarayustathā ॥
tatta....................................... । (p.44b, l.1)
....dyaṃ dāpayetteṣāṃ tāmbūlaṃ tu nivedayet ।
navasaptaśareṇaiva caturhastapramāṇataḥ ॥ (l.2)
sthaṇḍilaṃ kārayettatra kalpayet vāstudaivatān ।
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya pūjakena baliṃ kṣipet ॥
tatpūrve sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā vāstuhomaṃ *tu kārayet ।* (l.3)
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuryāt punaḥ puṇyāhavācanam ॥
tena toyena saṃprokṣya pañcagavyaṃ punaḥ kuru । (l.4)
maṇḍapasya caturdikṣu ṛgā vṛddhyayanaṃ kuru ॥
brahmasūktaṃ japetkoṇe paścāddhomaṃ samācaret ।
maṇḍapasya caturdikṣu *sthaṇḍilaṃ pācakena tu ॥* (p.45a, l.1)

(p.59a, c.1) kṛṣṇan svāti । hṛṣikeśavan āyilyam । śrīdharan cittirai । kṣetrapālan mūlam । jñānaṃ tirivādirai । (c.2) bhīman hastam । bhairavan pūrāḍam । (c.3) செப்பிய kṛṣṇan svāti cītānicittirākke ஒப்பிய பிரமந்த்தம் ஒளிவந்தானமாதிலாக்கே எப்பலு செதிரபாலன இனியதொறுல-மாகும் தப்பிய ஹ்ருஷிகேஶன் தாயாயில்யமாகும். (c.4) செப்பிய க்ருஷ்ணன் ஸ்வாதி. ஸ்ரீதரன். சித்திரைக்கே ஒப்பிய பீமனத்து ஒளிர் ஞாநமஹதினாகரே. (p.59b, भद्रकालीप्रतिष्ठा, l.1)

(bhadrakālīsthāpanam -)
śruṇu vakṣye mahākālī sthāpanaṃ sarvasiddhitam ।
uttarāyaṇakāle tu śuklapakṣe śubhe dine ॥
śubha.....*dibhiryukte cararāśīṃ tu varjayet ।* (l.2)
grāme vā nagare vāpi parvate rājadhānike ॥
taṭākatīre vāraṇye nadyā*stīre śmaśānake ।* (l.3)
cautyavṛkṣasya mūle varaṃ grāme mahāpathe ? । 
grāmādīnāṃ ca saumye vā pāvake *vātha śāṅkare ॥* (l.4)
nairi(ṛ)tye vānile vāpi īśvarasyālaye'pi vā ।
sthāpayet sadanopetaṃ pūjayedvidhi*pūrvakam ॥* (l.5)
śivadvijena kartavyaṃ devatakāryamuttamam ।
tenaiva pūjanaṃ rāṣṭraṃ subhikṣaṃ roganāśanam ॥
anyajātispṛśe *caiva vyādhicorabhayaṃ bhavet ।* (p.60a, l.1)
nareṣu brāhmaṇaśreṣṭho brāhmaṇeṣu śivadvijaḥ ॥
tatra ṣaṇmuni hastaṃ vā vistāradviguṇocchrayam । (l.2)
ardhamaṇḍapasaṃyuktaṃ bhittiprākārasaṃyutam ॥
gopuradvārasaṃyuktaṃ kārayedīśvarālayam ।
pūrve vā paścime vāpi uttaradvāra*meva ca ॥* (l.3)
dhā(dā)rujaṃ śailajaṃ vāpi mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi kārayet ।
dvibhujaṃ caturbhujaṃ vāpi aṣṭahastasamanvitam ॥
hasta aṣṭādaśaṃ *caiva pretavāhanameva ca ।* (l.4)
prathamaṃ ratnavinyāsaṃ dvitīyaṃ netramokṣaṇam ॥
tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ darśanaṃ caiva caturthaṃ bimbaśiddhita(da)m । (l.5)
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
jalādhivāsanaṃ paścāt yāgapūjā tataḥ param ।
sadasyāgrataḥ kuryāt maṇḍapaṃ caturaśra..... ॥ 
.....*dhye vedikāṃ kuryāt darpaṇodarasannibham ।* (p.60b, f.74a, l.1)
tatpūrvādicaturdikṣu yonikuṇḍāni kārayet ॥
paścime cottare vāpi *kalpayet snānavedikam ।* (l.2)
gomayā lepanaṃ kṛtvā sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
śayanaṃ kalpayenmadhye pañcavastreṇa deśikam ।
jalāduttīrya *kālīṃ tu śuddhiṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.3)
raktavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya kautukaṃ bandhayettataḥ ।
triṃ(tṛṃ)śatprasthasaṃpūrṇaṃ kumbhamastreṇa *veṣṭayet ॥* (l.4)
pūrayecchuddhatoyena vastrakūrcahiraṇyakam ।
dhyātvā kālīṃ tu tanmantraṃ abhitāṣṭaghaṭān nyaset ॥
vāmādi nava*śaktiṃ syāt kālī madhyaghaṭān nyaset ।* (l.5)
parivāraghaṭe ceva śaktijālasamanvitam ॥
gandhādyairarcayitvā tu naivedyāntaṃ pṛthak pṛthak । (p.61a, l.1)
homakarma samārabhya vahnimārgoktamārgataḥ ॥
palāśaṃ pūrvadigbhāge khādiraṃ dakṣiṇe tathā ।
śamī paścime kuṇḍe tu *audumbaraṃ tu saumyake ॥* (l.2)
pradhānayonikuṇḍe tu akṣau samidhā hunet ।
samit sapyodanaṃ lājān sarṣapaṃ ca yavaṃ tilam ॥ (l.3)
māṣāpūpaṃ gulāpūpaṃ tilāpūpaṃ tathaiva ca ।
sadyādi cāstraparyantaṃ mantraiścaiva tu homayet ॥ (l.4)
pratyekaṃ tu śataṃ hutvā dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hunet ।
dvisandhipūjanaṃ homaṃ nṛttagītasamanvitam ॥
caturdvāre caturvedaṃ *śaivamantraṃ japetkramāt ।* (l.5)
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ snānadvayamathācaret ॥
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā sviṣṭamagneti mantrataḥ । (p.61b, f.77a, l.1)
uddhṛtya śayanāṃ kālīṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
lohaṃ cet snānavedyāṃ tu śailajaṃ cet gṛhaṃ viśet ।
ratnanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā *aṣṭabandhanamārabhet ॥* (l.2)
pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgairvā adhvanyāsakrameṇa tu ।
pradhānaṃ ghaṭamuddhṛtya aṣṭaśaktisamanvitam ॥
kumbhānmantrān samādāya *bimbasya hṛdi vinyaset ।* (l.3)
tadghaṭastodakenaiva  mahākālī tu secayet ॥
sarvālaṅkārasaṃktaṃ(yuktaṃ) gandhapuṣpādinārcayet । (l.4)
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ aṣṭakumbhābhiṣecanam ॥
mantreṇa sthāpayenmantrī gandhapuṣpādinārcayet ।
pādyācamanaṃ cārghyā nālikera*phalairyutam ॥* (l.5)
gu(ku)ru (dhū)padīpanaivedyaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ ikṣudaṇḍakam ।
raktavastraṃ raktapuṣpaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥ (p.62a, l.1)
abhaktaṃ ca viśvaṃ ? proktaṃ ikṣyaṃ pañcavidhaṃ japet ।
vyajanaṃ cāmaraṃ chatraṃ darpaṇaṃ pādukaṃ tathā ॥ (l.2)
parivārabaliṃ datvā ajacchodanameva ? ca ।
nityāgnikāryaṃ pūjānte nityotsavaṃ tataḥ param ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścāt daśa*dānādikaṃ kramāt ।* (l.3)
nṛttagītasamāyuktaṃ paścādutsavamācaret ॥
mahādhvajaṃ sthāpya pūrvaṃ paiśāca......tam । (l.4)
yāgapūjā yāgahomaṃ rakṣābandhanameva ca ॥

ācāryaṃ pūjaye*ttatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ॥* (p.62b, f.78a, l.1)
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।

yugalacaraṇavāmaṃ padmapīḍho(ṭho)paristham itarakaratriśūlaṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣyam ।
amaravarasupūjyaṃ dakṣiṇe *hastalambaṃ jaya śivakālirūpaṃ santataṃ cintayāmi ॥* (l.2)

	bhuvi cakrāniladiśormadhye nityavat śivasūryam abhyarcya । (l.3) visṛjyaiva samācamya । kṛtasakalīkaraṇa(ṇam) । sāmānyārghyaṃ saṃkalpya । astreṇa saṃprokṣya । pūrve oṃ hāṃ śānti(l.4)toraṇāya namaḥ । oṃ ri(ṛ)gvedāya namaḥ । kṛtayugāya namaḥ । triśūle – gaṅgāyai namaḥ । mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । ni(na)rmadāyai namaḥ । (l.5) avargāya namaḥ । kavargāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇottaraśākhayoḥ nandine namaḥ । kundalīśaktaye namaḥ । mahākālāya namaḥ । pīṭa(ṭha)nīśaktaye (p.66a, l.1) namaḥ । madhye - śāntikalāyai namaḥ । umāyai namaḥ । dehinīśaktaye namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – saṃ somāyai namaḥ । buṃ budhāyai namaḥ । (l.2) ye(e)te gandhākṣatapuṣpadīpaṃ datvā । aṃ agnaye namaḥ । svāhāśaktaye namaḥ । dakṣiṇe – bhūtitoraṇāya namaḥ । vidyākalādvārāyai namaḥ । (l.3) yajurvedāyai namaḥ । tredā(tā)yugāya namaḥ । triśūle – yamunāyai namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ । sindhuve namaḥ । dakṣiṇottaraśākhayoḥ - (l.4) bhṛṅgiṇe namaḥ । hryādinīśaktaye namaḥ । vināyakāya namaḥ । bhṛṅgiṇīśaktaye namaḥ । madhye – vidyākalāyai namaḥ । yamāya namaḥ । (l.5) kālakaṇṭhyai namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – bṛhaspataye namaḥ । bhārgavāya namaḥ । cavargāya namaḥ । gaṅgāyai namaḥ । ete gandhākṣatadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । (p.66b, f.82a, l.1) niri(ṛṛ)taye namaḥ । dehinīśaktaye namaḥ । viṣṇave namaḥ । mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । vāstuvā(vāstva)dhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । paścime - (l.2) balatoraṇāya namaḥ । nivṛttikalādvārāya namaḥ । dvāparayugāya namaḥ । sāmavedāya namaḥ । triśūle – godāvaryai namaḥ । umāyai namaḥ । (l.3) kāveryai namaḥ । dakṣiṇottaraśākhayoḥ – vṛṣabhāya namaḥ । bhadrādevyai namaḥ । skandāya namaḥ । śaśisutāya namaḥ । mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । (l.4) śāśvaye ? hitavargāya namaḥ । pavargāya namaḥ । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । vuyu(ya)ve namaḥ । ghoraśaktai namaḥ । gaṇeśvarāya namaḥ । (l.5) vallabhāyai namaḥ । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । uttare – ārogyatoraṇāya namaḥ । pratiṣṭhākalādvārāya namaḥ । kaliyugāya namaḥ । (p.67a, l.1) atharvaṇavedāya namaḥ । triśūle – śroṇabhadrāyi namaḥ । bhāgīrathī namaḥ । tuṅgabhadrāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇottaraśākhayoḥ – devyai namaḥ । (l.2) caṇḍāyai namaḥ । rūpiṇyai namaḥ । madhye – pratiṣṭhākalāyai namaḥ । kuberāya namaḥ । gambhinīśaktaye namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – rāhave (l.3) namaḥ । ketu(ta)ve namaḥ । ya/tha ? vargāya namaḥ । śavargāya namaḥ । astravardhanīṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । īśānye – īśānāya namaḥ । maharṣa(rṣi)ṇīśaktaye namaḥ । (l.4) brahmaṇe namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ । sadāśivagurave namaḥ । anantagurave namaḥ । śrīkaṇṭhagurave namaḥ । ambikāgurave namaḥ । (l.5) guhagurave namaḥ । kuṣṇagurave namaḥ । dhā/yā ? tāgurave namaḥ । iti saptagurūn saṃpūjyā(jya) । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । paścime - (p.67b, f.83a, l.1) nityavat dvāraṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । pūrvādi-dikpatākāsu – khaḍgāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । madhye – oghoraśaktaye namaḥ । pūrvādidhvajeṣu – kumudāya namaḥ । (l.3) kumudākṣāya namaḥ । puṇḍarīkākṣāya namaḥ । vāmanāya namaḥ । śaṅkukarṇāya namaḥ । sannetrāya namaḥ । sumukhāya namaḥ । (l.4) supratiṣṭhāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । pūrvādidikṣu – oṃ hāṃ hetukāya namaḥ । tripuraghnāya namaḥ । vahnaye namaḥ । yamajihvāya namaḥ । (l.5) kālāya namaḥ । karāline namaḥ । ekahṛdayāya namaḥ । bhīmāya namaḥ ।
dhyānam –
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanā sarpayajñopavītanā ।
kāmsyakhaḍga*samāyuktā hetukādyaṃ prakīrtitāḥ ॥* (p.68a, l.1)

   iti dhyātvā । kṣetrapālaṃ saṃpūjya । santuṣṭān paribhāvyā(vya) । maṇḍalordhvasthite (l.2) kampādiṣu – pṛthivītattvāya namaḥ । sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ । tṛṇeṣu – appu(p)tattvāya namaḥ । vāmadevaguhyāya namaḥ । bandheṣu - (l.3) tejastattvāya namaḥ । oghorahṛdayāya namaḥ । vaṃśeṣu – vāyutatvāya namaḥ । tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । sthūṇāsu – ākāśa(l.4)tattvāya namaḥ । īśānamūrdhnā(rdhā)ya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । śaktimaṇḍapaṃ vibhāvyā(vya) । taddṛṣṭyāvalokya । śeṣadvāreṇa (l.5) saṃkha(gha)ṭṭayan saptaviṃśatighaṭān tiryadvāraghayān saṃsthāpya । pūrvadvārasyottarakumbhe – 
caturvaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ *kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ mahābalam ।* (p.68b, f.84a, l.1)
dhvajābhayākṣamālābjaṃ śāntīṃ sarpopavītinīm ॥
   oṃ hāṃ śāntikalādvārāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇa(l.2)dvārasya pūrvapadakumbhe – 
śaktyābhayākṣamālābjaṃ vidyat(dyut)jvalanasannibhām ।
caturvaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ mahābalam ॥ (l.3)
   oṃ hāṃ vidyākalādvārāya namaḥ । paścimadvārasya dakṣiṇapadakumbhe – 
taptā(pta)cāmīkarābhāsaṃ caturvaktrendubhūṣitam । (l.4)
vajrābhayākṣamālābjaṃ nivṛttiṃ parikīrtidam(tam) ॥
   nivṛttikalādvārāya namaḥ । uttaradvārasya paścimapadakumbhe – 
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ *dvivaktraṃ ca caturbhujam ।* (l.5)
pāśābhayākṣamālābjaṃ pratiṣṭhāratnabhūṣitam ॥
   oṃ hāṃ pratiṣṭhākalādvārāya namaḥ । vidhivat saṃpūjyā(jya) । (p.69a, l.1) pūrvadvārasya uttarapadakumbhe – ambikāyai namaḥ ।
dhyānam –
ekāsyaṃ dvibhujaṃ caiva sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ।
jaṭāmakuṭa*saṃyuktaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ suyauvanam ॥* (l.2)
dakṣiṇe – oṃ hāṃ gaṇāmbikāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇadvārasya pūrve – gaṅgāyai namaḥ । paścime (l.3) gauryai namaḥ । paścimadvārasya dakṣiṇe – umāyai namaḥ । uttare – aiśvaryai namaḥ । uttaradvārasya paścime – manonmanyai (l.4) namaḥ । pūrave – dhāriṇyai namaḥ । pūrvādicaturdikṣu –
pītavarṇāmbarābhūṣā haṃsavāhanameva ca ।
caturbhujaṃ *triṇetrāṃ ca caturvaktraṃ tathaiva ca ॥* (l.5)
abhayaṃ varadaṃ caiva akṣamālāṃ ca kuṇḍikā ।
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam ॥
   iti (p.69b, f.85a, l.1) dhyātvā । brāhmai namaḥ ।
triṇetraṃ caturbhujaṃ caiva śuddhavarṇasamāyutam ।
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ ekapatnyā samanvitam ॥
abhayaṃ varadaṃ caiva *mṛgaṭaṅkasya vāriṇam ।* (l.2)
vāhanaṃ prabhaṃ kṛtvā māheśvarīṃ tathaiva ca ॥
   māheśvarī namaḥ । 
ekānanaṃ dvinayanaṃ karaṇḍamakuṭānvitam । (l.3)
vajraśaktidharaṃ caiva sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
   kaumārī namaḥ । 
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ caiva kanakābharaṇānvitam ।
garuḍāsanamevoktaṃ *kamalāsanasamanvitam ॥* (l.4)
   oṃ hāṃ vaiṣṇavī namaḥ ।
śūlapāśābhayavarān karaṇḍamakuṭānvitam ।
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ *māhiṣārūḍhameva ca ॥* (l.5)
   oṃ hāṃ vārāhī namaḥ ।
vajraśaktidharaṃ caiva gajavāhanameva ca ।
akṣamālāsamāyuktaṃ sarvāvayavasundaram ॥ (p.70a, l.1)
oṃ hāṃ māheśī namaḥ । 
triṇetraṃ ūrdhvakeśaṃ ca ḍamarupāśaṃ tathaiva ca ।
kapālaśūlahastaṃ ca pretavāhanameva ca ॥
   oṃ hāṃ (l.2) cāmuṇḍi namaḥ । 
ekavaktraṃ caturdorbhi(rbhiḥ) nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam ।
raktavastradharaṃ caiva śūlapāśasamanvitam ॥

	oṃ hāṃ durgāyai namaḥ । (l.3) mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । paścime nityavat dvārapālān saṃpūjya । gaṅgāya namaḥ । yoginyai namaḥ । (l.4) ḍhā(ḍā)kinyai namaḥ । yamunāyai namaḥ । mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ । ni(na)rmadāyai namaḥ । pada(ta)ṅgāyai namaḥ । (l.5) bhuvaṅgāyai namaḥ । vimalāyai namaḥ । ......ve namaḥ । siṃhā(sihmā)yai namaḥ । astrara(?)dvārapālāya namaḥ । vāstupānidhi(p.70b, f.86a, l.1)patibrahmaṇe namaḥ । iti gandhapuṣpādīn saṃpūjyā(jya) । divyāntarikṣabhūmiṣṭhān vighnānussā(tsā)ryā(rya) । ........astramabhyarcya (l.2) praviśya । pradakṣiṇakramāt gatvā । naiṛte vāstunāthaṃ saṃpūjya । vedidakṣiṇataḥ uttarābhimukhaṃ praviśyā(śya) । bhūtaśuddhi (l.3)antaryāgam । sthānaśuddhi । viśeṣārghyadravyaśuddhi । mantraśodhanam । pañcagavyasādhanāni pūrvavat kṛtvā । sva sva pūjāṃ (l.4) vidhāya । svasmin lalāṭakaṇḍa(ṇṭha)pādāntā bhāge । śivatattvāya namaḥ । śivatattvādhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । vidyātattvāya namaḥ । vidyā(l.5)tattvādhipataye oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । ātmatattvāya namaḥ । ātmatattvādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । iti vinyasya । sāsanaṃ sabrahmāṅgaṃ (p.71a, l.1) śivatattvavyāpakatvena śivaṃ vinyasya । śivahastamāropya । ātmānaṃ svamūrtipaṃ savastramakuṭābhiralaṅkṛtyā(tya) । śivohamiti bhāvya । (l.2) jñānakhaḍgaṃ gṛhītvā । vikirān abhimantryā(trya) । nairi(ṛ)te sthitvā । nirīkṣaṇādisamskṛtyā(tya) । vikirān nikṣipya । kuśakūrco(l.3)pasaṃhṛtya । aiśānyāṃ vardhanyāsanaṃ datvā । vāstuṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । paścimadvāre pūrvavat lakṣmīṃ saṃpūjayet । tataḥ śiva(l.4)kumbhāstravardhanyau calāsanaṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । sa(śa)ktyādilokapālān sva sva dikṣu saṃpūjyā(jya) । ........jñāyā । (l.5) śivājñāṃ śrāvayet । kuṇḍāgragāminīṃ makhamaṇḍitadhārāṃ vardhanīṃ astradurgamanusmaran । melena bhrāmya । pūrvakumbhaṃ paścāt vardhanīṃ (p.71b, f.87a, l.1) saṃsthāpya । sthirāsane kumbhavardhanyāṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । bhagaliṅgasamāyoganijayāgajanāyaka । ....... kṣamasva bhaktānukampayā (l.2) iti vijñāpya । jñānakhaṭgaṃ arghyaṃ nirodhārghyaṃ saṃsthāpya । naivedyatāmbūlaṃ datvā । paścāt vedikoṇeṣu – (l.3) dharmādipadmāntaṃ parāśaktaye namaḥ । 

dhyānam –
vadanamekacaturbhujasavyaṃ karakuśūlasupāśadharaṃ tathā ।
ḍamarukātharaka ? *kapālakarau mañjayaśivamaha(hā)kāli namaste ॥* (l.4)

	ityāvāhya । ....... tārāyai namaḥ - mūrdhni । vidyāyai namaḥ – vaktre । mūrtyai namaḥ – hṛdaye । śuddhāyai namaḥ – gale । rajāyai namaḥ – vāmabāhu । medhāyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇabāhu । oṃ hrīṃ (p.72a, l.1) svadhāyai namaḥ – jaḍha(ṭha)re । svadhātyai namaḥ – nābhau । varmaṇāyai namaḥ – yonau । kapāline namaḥ – dakṣiṇoru । pālinyai namaḥ – vāmoru । (l.2) tṛṣṇāyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇajānu । dhṛtyai namaḥ – vāmajānu । .......yai namaḥ – dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām । matyai namaḥ – vāmajaṅghāyām । prajāyai (l.3) namaḥ – dakṣiṇasphici । śāntāyai namaḥ – vāmasphici । mūrtyai namaḥ – vāmapārśve । oṃ hrīṃ kriyāyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇapārśve । śāntyai (l.4) namaḥ – vāmapādau । oṃ hrīṃ dāntyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇapādau । dayāyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇapāṇau । śraddhāyai namaḥ – vāmapāṇau । (l.5) dvitīyai namaḥ – dakṣiṇastanau । bandhāyai namaḥ – vāmastanau । rātryai namaḥ ityārādhyā(dhya) । prathamāvaraṇe nyaset । 
īśāna*sphaṭikavarṇāya varadābhayahastāya nṛttarūpāya śāntaguṇāya oṃ hrīṃ umāyai namaḥ ।* (p.72b, f.88a, l.1)
oṃ tatpuruṣapītavarṇāya nāgahastāya paramarūpāya rudraguṇāya hrīṃ gauryai namaḥ ॥ 2 ॥
oṃ aghoraañjanavarṇāya dhanurdharāya mahābhayaṅkarāya khaṭgakheṭakahastāya (l.3) hrīṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ । 3 ॥
oṃ vāmadevāya raktavarṇāya sukhāsanāya pāśāṅkuśahastāya krūrarūpāya hrīṃ ambikāyai (l.4) namaḥ । 4 ॥
oṃ sadyojātāya śvetavarṇāya śūlapāśāṅkuśahastāya ugrarūpāya hrīṃ gaṇāmbikāyai namaḥ । 5 ॥
oṃ hṛdayāya (l.5) agnitejāya jñānasvarūpāya abhayavaradahastāya hrīṃ vāminyai namaḥ । 6 ॥
oṃ śirasāya īśānatejāya pītivarṇāya (p.73a, l.1) trilocanāya śūlakapālāya varadahastāyā(ya) oṃ hrīṃ kriyāyai namaḥ । 7 ॥
oṃ kavacāya raktavarṇāya vāyudevāya triṇe(l.2)trāya śūlakapālavarada(dā)bhayāya hrīṃ khaṭvāṅgine namaḥ । 8 ॥
	astrāya kṛṣṇāṅgāya trilocanāya śūlakapālāya varadābhayāya (l.3) hrīṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ । 

dvitīyāvaraṇam – vāmāyai namaḥ । jyeṣṭhāyai namaḥ । raudri(dryai) namaḥ । kālyai namaḥ । kalavikaraṇyai (l.4) namaḥ । balavikaraṇyai namaḥ । balaprathama(matha)nyai namaḥ । karvabhūtadamanyai namaḥ । manonmanyai namaḥ । (l.5)

tri(tṛ)tīyāvaraṇam – ambikāyai namaḥ । gaṇāmbikāyai namaḥ । gaṅgāyai namaḥ । gauryai namaḥ । umāyai namaḥ । devyai namaḥ । īśvaryai namaḥ । (p.73b, f.89a, l.1) manonmanyai namaḥ ।

caturthāvaraṇam – brāhmai namaḥ । māheśvaryai namaḥ । kāmārī namaḥ । vaiṣṇavyai namaḥ । vārāhī namaḥ । (l.2) māhendryai namaḥ । cāmuṇḍī namaḥ । durgāyai namaḥ । mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ ।

pañcāvaraṇam – khaḍgāya namaḥ । (l.3) pāśāya namaḥ । aṅkuśāya namaḥ । ḍamarukāya namaḥ । kapālāya namaḥ । dhvajapaṭāya namaḥ । vetālāya namaḥ । (l.4) kṣurikāya namaḥ । śūlāya namaḥ । gatā(dā)ya namaḥ । darpaṇāya namaḥ । pūrṇakumbhāya namaḥ । siṃhā(sihmā)ya namaḥ । yugmācarāya namaḥ । śrīvatsāya (l.5) namaḥ । svastikāya namaḥ । śaṅkha...........namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । rambhāyai namaḥ । tilottamāyai namaḥ । (p.74a) sumukhi(khī) namaḥ । sundarī namaḥ । durmukhi(khī) namaḥ । kāma-vardhanī namaḥ । pūjanī namaḥ । śobhāyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pūrvādicaturdikṣu (l.2) padmakuṇḍam īśānacakrayormadhye yonikuṇḍe agnikāryamācaret । oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ hūṃ bhāṃ bhīṃ (l.3) indrāyai namaḥ । kālī namo namaḥ । oṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ hoṃ bhadrāya namo kālī namo namaḥ । cakṣuśrotrajihvā(l.4)ghrāṇavākpādapāṇipāyūrupasthahuravat prāṇaprakṛṣya bhadranālyai namaḥ ।

(savyamūrtisthāpanam -)
dhyānam  - (p.74b, f.90a, l.1)
atha vakṣye viśeṣeṇa sthāpanaṃ savyamūrtinam । 
bhuktidaṃ sarvadānāya sarvayajñaphalapradam ॥
yajanaṃ jñānadaṃ ......*rdhanaṃ ripunāśanam ।* (l.2)
tridaśānāṃ divākāle sitapakṣe śubhe dine ॥
śubhavārādibhiryukte sthāpanaṃ samārabhet । (l.3)
pratiṣṭhādivasātpūrve kārayedaṅkurārpaṇam ॥
vedayāgre maṇṭape ramye śālibhi(bhiḥ) sthaṇḍilaṃ kuru । (l.4)
tadūrdhve vinyasetkūṭaṃ dvāratiṣṭhe samāyutam ॥
pūrvoktavidhinā dhīmān ratnanyāsaṃ tu kārayet ।
ratnasthāpana*naṣṭe ṛṣīṇāṃ tu prajāpate ॥* (l.5)
pakṣinetrayeti ṛkṣe kāraye(yet) netramokṣaṇam ।
sthaṇḍilaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā *beraṃ tasyopari nyaset ॥* (p.75a, l.1)
dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇe nyasya kauśikādiri(ṛ)ṣīn kramāt ।
deśikaḥ sūkṣmakairvācaṃ svarṇasūciprahārataḥ ॥ (l.2)
tato'kṣimokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt netramantraṃ samāyutam ।
tattat kuryā(ryāt) ri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ tu tattannetramanusmaran ॥
takṣakaṃ pūjayet paścāt *vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ।* (l.3)
vyapohya ? takṣakaṃ paścāt tatpūrve sthaṇḍilaṃ kuru ॥
devaṃ tasyopari sthāpya dakṣiṇe *dakṣiṇe ri(ṛ)ṣīn ।* (l.4)
tattanmanujamantreṇa prokṣayecchuddhavāriṇā ॥
prohaimanakhairapi ?
madhunā ca yutenaiva tarpayettu krameṇa tu ।
pūrvoktapātraṃ saṃgṛhya pratyekaṃ prasthapūritam ॥
ghṛtena madhunā caiva *pūrayetpūrvavatsudhi(dhīḥ) ।* (p.75b, f.91a, l.1)
tattatsūtraṃ samuccārya darśayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
..........ṅgaṃ ca viprāśca dhānyarāśikrameṇa tu ।
........nā *caiva deveśasya pradarśayet ॥* (l.2)
ri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ darśayet paścāt aṣṭamṛtsalilena tu ।
snāpayet pañcamenaiva *ri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ tu tathaiva ca ॥* (l.3)
lambakūrcasamāyuktaṃ bimbaṃ vastreṇa veṣṭayet ।
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ tiryaggrāma*pradakṣiṇam ॥* (l.4)
praviśya toyatīraṃ tu sthāpayet sthaṇḍilopari ।
toyamadhye prāpāṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye sthāpayet śivam ॥ (l.5)
ri(ṛ)ṣīṃ tasyottare savye tattanmantreṇa śāyayet ।
saudhāgre maṇḍapaṃ kuryāt prāgupta(kta)vidhinā saha ॥
tanmadhye kuṭṭimaṃ *kuryāt bhuktimātrasamaṃ śivam ।* (p.76a, l.1)
mahāṇe cāni kuṇḍāni kṛtvā tu hṛdiṣu kramāt ॥
vedāśraṃ caiva pādāśraṃ suṣirapyaṃ ? ca vartulam । (l.2)
mahāśāsu prakalpyātha koṇeṣvevaṃ kuśeśayam ॥
indraīśānayormadhye suvṛttaṃ tu pradhānakam ।
liṅgodbhavapratiṣṭhokta*mārgeṇa snānavedikam ॥* (l.3)
kuryāttakṣakamudvāsya lepayedgomayādibhiḥ ।
vācayenmantrapuṇyāhaṃ bhūsurān *tatra bhojayet ॥* (l.4)
ucchiṣṭaṃ tu vyapohyātha prokṣayetpañcagavyakaiḥ ।
maṇḍapaṃ pūjayetpaścāt *pūrvoktavidhinā saha ॥* (l.5)
vāstuhomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuru ।
sthaṇḍilaṃ vedikāmadhye kṛtvā saroruhaṃ likhet ॥ (p.76b, f.92a, l.1)
śayanaṃ kalpayettatra gandhapuṣpādinārcayet ।
jalāduttīrya berāṇi snānākhye vinyaset kramāt ॥
kūpādīni vyapohyātha pūrvavat *śuddhimācaret ।* (l.2)
pādyamācamanārghyaṃ tattat sadyena dāpayet ॥
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya tattadīśānamantrataḥ ।
padmamudrāṃ pradarśyātha *maheśākhyaṃ pradarśayet ॥* (l.3)
......kādiri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ tu surabhīṃ tu pradarśayet ।
lambakūrcasamāyuktaṃ pṛthak vaktreṇa *khaṇḍayet ॥* (l.4)
tattaddhṛdayamantreṇa kautukaṃ bandhayottataḥ ।
śayane śāyayeddevaṃ prākpaściraṃ cordhvavaktrakam ॥
devasya dakṣiṇe vāme *śāyayettu ri(ṛ)ṣi(ṣiṃ) kramāt ।* (l.5)
pratyekaṃ raktavastreṇa veṣṭayeddhṛdayena tu ॥
śirase dakṣiṇe bhāge śivakumbhaṃ tu vinyaset । (p.77a, l.1)
tasyaiva vāmapāṇe tu ............. ॥
......tu śirobhāge tattat kumbhe tu vinyaset ।
lokapālā *kumbhāśca parita(taḥ) sthāpayet kramāt ॥* (l.2)
sakūrcān sāpidhānāśca ganadhatoyaprapitān ।
sasūtrān pallavopetān pratyekaṃ *vastraveṣṭayet ॥* (l.3)
.......pañcaratnāni vardhanyāṃ hemapaṅkajam ।
ri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ hemakhaṇḍaṃ tu tattanmantreṇa nikṣipet ॥ (l.4)
dhyāyenmaheśarūpaṃ tu tattanmantraṃ kalaśe nyaset ।
gaurīrūpaṃ tato dhṛtvā mantraṃ kumbhe tu vinyaset ॥
mukulaṃ *padmamudrāṃ ca darśayorubhorapi ।* (l.5)
gandhapuṣpādinā pūjyā(ya) naivedyaṃ dāpayettataḥ ॥
tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettasmi(smai) mukhavāsa*samanvitam ।* (p.77b, f.93a, l.1)
vinyaset ṛṣibījāni tattatkumbheṣu suvratā(ta) ॥
visargāt prathamaṃ grāhya dīpāntena samāyutam ।
rasavargaṃ catuḥ *pañcapakṣasvarasamanvitam ॥* (l.2)
śivadūrvā dvayopetāṃ bhāradvārasya suvrata ।
dvitīyasya tṛtīyaṃ tu saptasvarasamāyutam ॥
śivaviṣṇu*samāyuktaṃ ....................... ।* (l.3)
mūlamantrasamāyuktaṃ tasmin aṅgāni kalpayet ॥
tattaddhyānasamāyuktaṃ tattatkumbheṣu *vinyaset ।* (l.4)
lokeśaṃ svasva bījāni nyasettattadghaṭeṣu ca ॥
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya naivedyāntaṃ pṛthak pṛthak । (l.5)
tato homaṃ samārabhya ācāryo mūrtipaissahā(ha) ॥
diśāsvadhyayanaṃ kuryāt koṇe mantrajapaṃ kuru ।
dikdikpadma *madhyadeśeṣu stotrāṇi sadā japet ॥* (p.78a, l.1)
agnyādhānādikaṃ sarvaṃ agnikāryoktamācaret ।
prāgādipañcakuṇḍeṣu *śivāgniṃ parikalpayet ॥* (l.2)
pāvake kauśikāgnistu kāśyapāgniṃ tu rākṣase ।
bhāradvājasya vāyavye śāṅkare gautamasya tu । (l.3)
pūrvoktavidhinā caiva tattanmantraiśca homayet ॥
evaṃ kṛtvā tathā rātau prabhāte vimale tataḥ ।
deśiko *mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ vidhivat snānamācaret ॥* (l.4)
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pūjayedātmadevatā ।
navāmbaradharoṣṇīṣaṃ *sitavastrottarīyakam ॥* (l.5)
pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇairyuktā śuklacandanamakṣatam ।
bimbānuddhṛtya śayanāt vastrādīni vyapohya ca ॥ (p.78b, l.1)
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya tattanmantreṇa deśikam(kaḥ) ।
kuṇḍān kumbhāni saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādinā saha ॥
jayādīni tato hutvā hṛdā pūrṇāhutiṃ *hunet ।* (l.2)
dakhyeti ca mantrāṇi hutvāgniṃ tu visarjayet ॥
pūjayeddeśikādīni pūrvoktavidhinā sahā(ha) । (l.3)
bimbānuddhṛtya śayanāt ....nākhye vinyasettataḥ ॥
madhyame tu śivaṃ sthāpya dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇe ri(ṛ)ṣīn ।
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ *kārayet śakti......... ॥* (l.4)
akārādikṣakārāntaṃ ri(ṛ)ṣīṇāṃ vinyasettataḥ ।
pūjayet svasva sadyena kuryādghṛtaśirorpaṇam ॥ (l.5)
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvātodyasamāyutam ।
uddhṛtya kalaśān pūjānyakrameṇa śirasā vahe ॥
grahapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt berāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ *nyaset ।* (p.79a, l.1)
kumbhādbījaṃ samādāya pratime hṛdi vinyaset ॥
tadghaṭasthodakenaiva snāpayet savyamūrtinam ।
vardhanyāṃ(nyāt) bījamādāya *ambuje madhyame nyaset ॥* (l.2)
tatrābhiṣecanaṃ kuryāt tattatkumbhe(bha)jalena tu ।
purandarādīni bījāni prāgādī(di)ṣvambuje nyaset ॥ (l.3)
tadvastrasthodakenaiva tattatsthānābhiṣecayet ।
ṛṣīṇā(ṇāṃ) mantramādāya tattadbere kramānnyaset ॥
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya *tattadīśānamantrataḥ ।* (l.4)
snapanaṃ kārayetpaścāt pratyekaṃ vidhinā vaham ॥
paścādabhyarcayet bimbaṃ naivedyāntā....*pṛthakh ।* (l.5)
utsavaṃ kārayetpaścāt vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
praviśya bhavanaṃ paścāt prāpayeddakṣiṇānanam ।
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe vāme *kauśikādikramānnyaset ॥* (p.79b, l.1)
sthāpayeddevadeveśaṃ geyāruddhaṃ ca suvratā(ta) ।
sāṅgamevaṃ samākhyātaṃ svaprayātamathaśruṇu ॥ (l.2)
grāmasya dakṣiṇe bhāge daśadaṇḍaṃ vayapohya ca ।
nadyāstīre taṭāke vā anyadeśe manorame ॥
kūṭaṃ vā maṇḍapaṃ vāpi prāsādaṃ *vāpi kārayet ।* (l.3)
tanmadhye sthāpayetsavye ....tat svapradhānakam ॥
nityapūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāt śāstradṛṣṭe(ṣṭena) karmaṇā ।
bhuktyarthaṃ *vātha ....rtha jñānārthaṃ paravaṃśaya ॥* (l.4)
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।
dakṣiṇāmūrtimeva syāt kālāgnisthāpanaṃ śruṇu ॥ (l.5)
॥ iti kāraṇopabhede bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ pratiṣṭhātantre kriyāpāde svaya(savya)mūrtisthāpa(pana)vidhisaptacatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ ॥ (p.80a blank, p.80b, jīrṇoddhāraṇam, l.1)

(jīrṇoddhāraṇam-)
jīrṇoddhravidhiṃ vakṣye sarvādhiṣṭavināśanam ।
liṅgasya piṇḍikāyāṃ ca pratimādyālayasyatu ॥
taruṇā yasya liṅgasya parivārā*la(laya)sya tu ।* (l.2)
prākārā(ra)syaiva anyeṣāṃ uddhāraṃ cottarakriyām ॥
sphuṭite khaṇḍite bhinne nāśite aśanināśane ।
unmane *corakalabhe nāśite śatrubhistu vā ॥* (l.3)
....rik srotakaribhyāṃ vā jīrṇe kāle'pi paryayā ।
kṣatrajīrṇe yathā kṣete*'nyadvānyat pratipadyate ॥* (l.4)
tato jīrṇāni vastrāṇi sadyo muñjati devatāḥ ।
taddṛṣṭvā bhūtavetālaṃ piśācā *brahmarākṣasāḥ ॥* (l.5)
pretālpasatyaślanyadvādāśrayanti na saṃśayaḥ ।
rāṣṭragrāmanṛpāṇāṃ ca sthāpakasyaiva mantriṇam ॥
kurvanti te *bhavatyugraṃ durbhikṣamaraṇaṃ dhṛvam ।* (p.81a, l.1)
arcitaṃ tveti ha(bha)tyā(ktyā)śunihatyośca na pūjitam ॥
tasmāduddharaṇaṃ cāśu vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇām ।
ādiśaiva*kulebha(jā)taḥ prāgupta(kta)lakṣaṇairyutam ॥* (l.2)
susnātaṃ sakalīkṛtyā(tya) gandhapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam ।
navavastradharoṣṇīṣaṃ hemapu(pa)ñcāṅga*bhūṣitam ॥* (l.3)
liṅgāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā hastamātreṇa deśikaḥ ।
abhyarcya devadeveśaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt ॥
jīrṇoddhāraṃ tu *viprajña pramāṇajñamanūjñayā ।* (l.4)
agnyādhānādikaṃ sarvaṃ agnikāryoktamācaret ॥
mūlena juhuyāddhīmān samidhaṃ brahmavṛkṣakām । (l.5)
ghṛtamimikṣeti mantreṇa ghṛtaṃ hutvā viśeṣataḥ ॥
hṛdayena caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ vāmena homayet ।
sarṣapaṃ netramantreṇa kṣīraṃ *................... ॥* (p.81b, first half of the folio broken, l.1) 
........pravate'pi ? pratyaṅgānimiti smṛtam । 
a.......aṅgo yogarāhīna............... ॥* (l.2)
........hīnaṃ cet tyājya bimbaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।
liṅge sarvasamutpannaṃ layaṃ layaṃ tatrai...... ॥ * (l.3)
..............................liṅganāmamihotyate ।
akhaṇḍaṃ sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ ...ṇḍaṃ tatpratimaṃ *....... ॥* (l.4)
.................ṇḍaliṅgamiti smṛtam ।
mṛṇmayadārujaṃ vāpi lohajaṃ vā śilāmayam ।
chinnaṃ vā *................................... ॥* (l.5)
pūjayedyati(di) mohena rājarāṣṭraṃ vinaśyati ।
tasmādberaṃ samuddhṛtya jale *.................. ॥* (p.82a, l.1)
nābikāmanupādau nābhyaśca meḍhrāntare śaṅkham ।
cakrakirīṭabāhūvi................. ॥
.......*berayāmasahamaṇḍapameva cā(ca) ।* (l.3)
pīṭhagopuraśālapurātanaiḥ sthānasaṃ*........ ॥ * (l.4)
................................ghrevamuddharet ।
.....nadīvṛṣagajaroṣṭraiḥ hastacoraiḥ pramādaiḥ ॥
patita*........................... ।* (l.5)
dinamanupe..................supramāṇai(ṇaiḥ) ॥
vicitrai(traiḥ) punarapi sakalāṅgaṃ pūjayet ।
kalpa.........................*sadyena homayet ॥* (p.82b, l.1)
ekaikaṃ juhuyāddhīmān aṣṭottaraśataṃ hunet ।
dravyānte caiva dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyāttataḥ ॥ (l.2)
jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛśca hunetkramāt ।
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā sviṣṭamagneti mantrataḥ ॥
uddhārayettato liṅgaṃ vṛṣabhe *hṛdinā budhaḥ ।* (l.3)
gajenoddhāraṇaṃ cettu turago carmakṛtarāmmunā ॥
piṇḍikāṃ ghānayettatra kundālyāṃ hemaṃ tu ..... । (l.4)
pādaśailādiśailā prakīrṇāṃ ca lakṣaṇānvitāḥ ॥
ba(pa)ṅktyantyasthāpanoktena sthāpayennavam ।
jīrṇarūpaṃ *samuddhṛtya śibikāyāṃ rathe'pi vā ॥* (l.5)
āropya nṛttavādyaiśca sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ।
tato bhaktajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ stotramaṅgala*vācakaiḥ ॥* (p.83b, l.1)
samudre nikṣipecchailaṃ namaskṛtyā(tya) mahājanāḥ ।
aśoṣya tu taṭāke tu nikṣipedathavā punaḥ ॥ (l.2)
nikṣipenmṛṇmayaṃ toye kṣipedaknau tu dārujam ।
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra gobhūmikāñcanādibhiḥ ॥
aśraśre devaṃ bhinne ........ *vārivihīnake ।* (l.3)
a....ṣāṃ jīrṇakāle tu ........piṇḍikāṃ punaḥ ॥
tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā sikataiḥ *....gaiḥ śubhaiḥ ।* (l.4)
ādaraṃ na ca kartavyaṃ agnikāryoktamārgataḥ ॥
samidhā sada........ṇa hṛdayena ghṛtaṃ hunet ।
aghoreṇa caruṇā vā lājaṃ vai śikhayā hunet ॥
talimastreṇa mantreṇa sarṣapaṃ kavacena tu ।
taṇḍulaṃ vāmadevena *pratyekaṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ॥* (p.83b, l.1)
kundālyāṃ khātayeddhīmān vṛṣeṇoddharaṇaṃ vinā ।
piṇḍikāsthāpanoktena sthāpayennava*piṇḍikām ॥* (l.2)

vīratantre – 
liṅgaṃ doṣaṃ yadi bhinnaṃ pīṭhaṃ pīṭhaṃ tyajet pūrvavadeva kuryāt ।
pīṭhaṃ na doṣa yadi *bhinnaliṅgaṃ ādhāraśailādyapi varjanīyam ॥* (l.3)
mānuṣe liṅgapīṭhe ca dagdhe ca sphoṭanādike ।
athāṅgulasya *śilāṅgaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ pūjayennavam ॥* (l.4)
pīṭhe ca tryaṅgulaṃ hīnaṃ tatparaṃ varjayennavam ।
pūrvaliṅgaṃ ca pīṭhaṃ ca sthāpaye *samṛddhidam ॥* (l.5)
viparītamanarthāya bhavet satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।

dīpte –

....hāṃ gamaṅgapratyaṅgaṃpīṭhotsedhaṃ tridhā bhavet ॥

....mekavaraṃ caiva jagatīpādukaṃ tathā ।

aṅge rasāṅgulaṃ grāhyaṃ aṅge *dviguṇaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.3)
pratyaṅge dviguṇaṃ grāhyaṃ pramāṇāṅgāsikaṃ tyajet ।

dīpte –

atha ......gulaṃ grāhyā tadūrdhve parivarjayet । (l.5)
....rajīrṇā ।

ūrūjānudvayaṃ caiva pratyaṅgamiti kīrtitam ॥

ubhaiva putramān śrīmān antye sāyujyamāpnuyāt ॥ 
॥ iti *kriyādīpike jīrṇoddhāraṇavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥* (p.88a l.1)

kāmike –  jīrṇoddhāraviśeṣam । tataḥ snānādikaṃ nivṛtya kṛtanityāhnika(l.2)dvayo yāgamaṇḍapaṃ gomayālepitam । vitāna-darbhamālātoraṇapatākādhvajakadalīpūgādyairbhūṣitamāsādya (l.3) śilpivisarjanaṃ kṛtvā । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । sakalīkṛtya । sāmānyārghyaṃ sāda(dha)yitvā । huṃphaḍantāstreṇābhimantrayet । (l.4) pūrvādicatustoraṇe pūjya । dvārādikalaśe yajet । pūrvadvāratoraṇe - astre(streṇa) saṃprokṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । (l.5) oṃ hrīṃ kumbhinītoraṇāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ hāṃ śāntikalādvārāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe – oṃ hrīṃ nikumbhinī(p.88b, f.104a, l.1)toraṇāya namaḥ । paścime - oṃ hrīṃ jṛmbhiṇītoraṇāya namaḥ । uttare – oṃ hrīṃ maṅgaṇītoraṇāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । śāntikumbhadvārakāya (l.2) namaḥ । iti pūrvam । vidyākalādvārāya namaḥ । iti dakṣiṇam । oṃ hrīṃ nivṛttikalādvārāya namaḥ । iti paścimam । pratiṣṭhākalādvārāya namaḥ । (l.3) uttaram । iti saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇavāmakrameṇa oṃ hrīṃ kālarātrī namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ mahākālyai namaḥ । iti pūrvam । oṃ hrīṃ śaṅkhine (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ vijayāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇam । oṃ hrīṃ vāruṇī namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ sihmavaktrī namaḥ । iti paścimam । oṃ hrīṃ dākṣāyiṇī namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ (l.5) bhahalādhārī namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pādyādinābhyarcya । iti pūrvā-dīśānāntaṃ lokapālāsthānāṣṭaśaktiṃ samabhyarcya । oṃ hrīṃ koṭarāya(yai) (p.89a, l.1) namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ viśālākṣi namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ muktakeśī namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ subhīṣiṇī namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ harivakrī(ktrī) namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ hayagrīvi namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ ......... (l.2) nīlalocanī namaḥ । iti dhvajāṣṭakaṃ saṃpūjya । maṇḍapaṃ nirīkṣyāntasthā bhava tuṣṭaye । dharmādīnarcayet । tadbāhyadvādaśa-stambheṣu । (l.3) dvādaśādityān saṃpūjya । kambye(mbe)ṣu - oṃ hāṃ pṛthvītattvāya namaḥ । tṛṇeṣu – oṃ hāṃ jalatattvāya namaḥ । bandheṣu – oṃ hāṃ agnitattvāya namaḥ । (l.4) vaṃśeṣu - oṃ hāṃ vāyutattvāya namaḥ । aṅghriṣu – oṃ hāṃ ākāśa-tattvāya namaḥ । vitāne – candrāya namaḥ । taraṅgeṣu – oṃ hāṃ jaleśāya (l.5) namaḥ । darbhamālāyām – vāsukhine namaḥ । iti pādyādīnabhyarcya । aṣṭamaṅgalam – pūrvādipūjayet । darpaṇe – caṇḍikarudrāyai namaḥ । pūrṇakumbhe – (p.89b, f.105a, l.1) oṃ aparṇarudrāyai namaḥ । vṛṣabhe – mantramālāyai namaḥ । yugmacāmare – vajramiśaktyāyai namaḥ । śrīvatse – oṃ umādevyai namaḥ । svastike – (l.2) oṃ mantraśaktyai namaḥ । śaṅkhe – oṃ trayambakāyai namaḥ । dīpe – oṃ śriyai namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । daśāyudhaṃ pūrvādipūjayet । (l.3) oṃ vajrāya prasannātmanī namaḥ । śaktyāya kopātmanī namaḥ । daṇḍāya guṇātmanī namaḥ । khaḍgāya komalātmanī namaḥ । pāśāya (l.4) viṣātmanī namaḥ । aṅkuśāya vimalātmanī namaḥ । gadāya vikṛtātmanī namaḥ । triśūlāya mahātmanī namaḥ । cakrāya śriyānmanī namaḥ । (l.5) guttā(ptā)ya maṅgalātmanī namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । maṇḍapaṃ māyāśaktiṃ vibhāvya । devīsamīpaṃ gatvā । prāṇāyāmaṃ karaśuddhiṃ karaṇa.....(p.90a, l.1) kṛtvā pañcagavyaṃ pūrvavat sthāpayitvā । vedimadhye vardhanīṃ saṃpūjya । (remaining place blank, p.90b, f.106a, śivapratiṣṭhāmuhūrtam, l.1)

(ā)layasya naidhanasthamane grahān sarvāśca varjayet । 
dvādaśyekādaśasyaiva pañcamī ca dvitīyakā ॥
ṣaṣṭhyāṣṭamī ca navamī *sūryādibhiḥ kramānvitā ।* (l.2)
dagdhavārā iti jñeyā sthāpanādiṣu dhīyatā ॥
pūṣā ca saptamī yuktā cāśvanī ca dvitīyyayā ।
ṣaṣṭhe *vaiṣṇavasaṃyuktā sitavārottareṇa tu ॥* (l.3)
saumyena guruvārastu uttarāṣāḍasaṃyutā ।
somavāre tu *hastenāśvanī budhavārakā ॥* (l.4)
dagdhayogā iti jñeyā sthāpanādi varjayet ।
etat śubhanā proktā kuryāt *prāṇavināśanam ॥* (l.5)
pāpagrahasamāyuktaṃ kāṅkṣitārthaṃ karaṃ ruhat ।
khaṇḍastaṇaṃ vyatīpādaṃ ṣaṣṭhakāṇaṃ ca sambhramā ॥ 
kaṇame *brahmadaṇḍaṃ ca ulkāpātaṃ ca vṛddhikam ।* (p.91a, l.1) 
grahaṇaṃ pathanakṣatraṃ śūlaṃ ca bhūmikampanam ॥
dhvajaṃ caiva viśeṣeṇa varjayettu *prayatnataḥ ।* (l.2)

evaṃ parīkṣya bahudhā liṅgasthāpanamārabhet । (l.3)

dhanamapi yato sā sadā kraśaye mukhyaiśaktyai daivadine sati ।
vinā caitreṇa māghādau pratiṣṭhā māsapañcake ॥ (l.2)
guruśukrodaye kāryā prathame kāraṇatraye ।
śuklapakṣe viśeṣeṇa kṛṣṇe vā pañcamaṃ dinam ॥
caturthī navamī ṣaṣṭhī *varjayitvā caturdaśi ।* (l.3)
śobhanā sthitiḥ śeṣāṃ krūravāravarjitāḥ ॥
tārayogaviśeṣaṃ ca jānīyyā jyotiṣāgame । (l.4)
.......dhaniṣṭhā bhādrā ye tantrayottaram ॥
rohiṇyāṃ śravaṇā ceti sthirārambhe mahodayam ।
lagnaṃ ca kumbhasahmāla*tulā........ṣṭarālitām ॥* (l.5)
............kathitaṃ piṅgalādikaiḥ ।
puṣyā hastottarāṣāḍhā pūrvāṣāḍhā ca *rohiṇī ॥* (p.94a, l.1)
ri(ṛ)kṣādyetāni viprāḥ praśayādyathājananī । punaḥ pratiṣṭhāvidhikāle dharmucyate । sāmpradaṃ(taṃ) mayāta(tha) ।
uttarāyaṇakāle *tvamākha(gha)māsavivarjite ।* (l.2)
śuklapakṣe dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā pañcamī tathā ॥
ṣaṣṭhī cā(ca) saptamī caiva daśamī ca trayodaśī ।
etāntu *tithāyāṃ proktā(kta)dine pūrvārdhamiṣyate ॥* (l.3)
śreṣṭhavāragurujñenduśukre'tha parikīrtitāḥ ।
pakṣagatadrāṃ ca *pakṣeṇa rikte caiva vivarjayet ॥* (l.4)
koṇāgāradineśānamuparāgaṃ ca varjayet ।
prāptyendutiṣyāśca makhāsvātipunarvasu ॥ (l.5)
aśvanī revatī hastāradrage cottaratrayaḥ ।
maitravaiṣṇavasaṃyuktaṃ nakṣatraṃ śubhadaṃ jayā ॥
andhaṃ kāṇaṃ ca varjaṃ *syāt karṇa....ṇameva cā(ca) ।* (p.94b, l.1)
pāpagatā yathā dṛṣṭaṃ bhaktaṃ kāṅkṣitatameva cā(ca) ॥
sa vyatīpātapatraiśca dhanuṣā śubhamanya(ha)bham । (l.2)
drekkāṇaśatruhorāśca darśanaṃ śu......dagraham ।
hitvā somodayaṃ teṣu devasthāpanakarmaṇi ॥
triṣaḍāya sthitaḥ pāṃ*śubhadarśana(naṃ) vidadhyāt ।* (l.3)
lagnāṅgāḥ praṇavaṃ dehaṃ yajamānasya kṛtavrate ॥
pāpadṛṣṭā śāśivarjaṃ sthiraścāpi viśeṣatā । (l.4)
ṣaṭsaptadaśamastho'pi śukre varjamudāhṛdaḥ(taḥ) ॥
dvitīyā pañcamo hitvā candrāḥ śeṣāśvaśobhanaḥ ।
saptadvīpitaṃ ca tavarmaṃ *pūjyānyatra tathā guruḥ ॥* (l.5)
so'pi lagnagataḥ kuryāt cakhaśca sukhadaṃ padam ।
sūryaṃ śubhakaro yuktā pauṣṇe hastaṃ triṣūttare ॥ (p.95a, l.1)
mandaśca śubhadaḥ proktā prājāpatyānurikṣayuk ।
aśvati hastasaṃyuktā budhavāre tu śobhanaḥ ॥
āryamaṇāścandramābhaśca guruvāreṇa *varjayet ।* (l.2)
vaiṣṇavaṃ puṣyagaṃ caiva śubhavāreṇa yojayet ॥
vaiśvadeyutaḥ somaṃ dvitīyā buddhisaṃyutam ।
guruvārayutā ṣaṣṭhī *tapanaśacaikādaśīṃ tathā ॥* (l.3)
saptamī......ṣṇayuktā ca varjanīyyā(yā)t prayatnataḥ ।
utpāte durdine cāpi niṣprāte nāma *beśrite ॥* (l.4)
nindite divasaṃ proktaṃ mṛte dinapadeśvare ।
evamādiṣu sarveṣu na kuryālliṅgasaṃsthitam ॥
evaṃ parīkṣya *niśātya ? pratiṣṭhākālamuttamam ।* (l.5)

ajite –
athāta(taḥ) saṃpravakṣyāmi liṅgasthāpanamuttamam ।
ayane śubhanakṣatre tithivārāṃ*śarākapaḥ ? ॥* (p.95b, l.1)
muhūrtakramaṃ vīkṣya sthāpanaṃ ca samācaret ।
puṣyamāsādiṣāṇmāsaṃ devānāṃ tu divā smṛtā ॥
tatrāṣāḍādiṣaṇmāsaṃ *devānāṃ tu niśā smṛtāḥ ।* (l.2)
dīkṣākāle prakartavyaṃ liṅgasthāpanamuttamam ॥
site pakṣe dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā pañcamī tathā । (l.3)
ṣaṣṭhī cā(ca) saptamī caiva daśama ca trayodaśī ॥
ekādaśī patidhayaḥ pratipat paurṇamāsyāpi ।
prājāpatyaṃ ca *......śaivamādityasaumyake ॥* (l.4)
pauṣṇako bṛhaspatī caiva cinnā vaiṣṇavanairi(ṛ)tam ।
vāyavyāṃ *cāśvayuk tvaṣṭā paitṛkaṃ cottaratrayam ॥* (l.5)
yeṣu nakṣatrayogeṣu pratiṣṭhāṃ ca samārabhet ।
meṣakarkaṭakaṃ taulyam *aṅkuraṃ ca vivarjayet ॥* (p.96a, l.1)
śeṣeṣu sthāpayelliṅgaṃ dhanurrāśiṃ ca varjayet ।
gurubhārgavasaumyānāṃ vārā somasya so(śo)bhanā ॥
eṣā *drekkāṇahorāyām aṃśānāṃ darśanaṃ śubham ।* (l.2)
eṣāmevodayaḥ śastaṃ varjaṃ ....modayaṃ sadā ॥
bhānumandārasomaśca truṣaḍāya *gatāḥ śubhāḥ ।* (l.3)
sarve grahavivarjyāsu aṣṭamasthā viśeṣatam(taḥ) ॥
ṣaṭsaptadaśamaṃ hitvā śukraścānyaśca śobhanam ।
uttarāṣāḍha*saṃyuktā somavāramaśobhanam ॥* (l.4)
hastā....śvarajāpahne budhavāreṇa varjayet ।
mṛgaśīrṣottarā cobhāvānya*vāreṇa vārayet ॥* (l.5)
vaiṣṇavaṃ caiva tiṣyaṃ ca śukravāreṇa varjayet ।
budhayuktāmiti yā ca ṣaṣṭhī yuktā bṛhaspatiḥ ॥
candra ekāśī*ryuktaṃ prāṇaṃ ca diśi kārayet ।* (p.96b, f.112, l.1)
saptamī pauṣṇasaṃyuktā vahni taddahate prajām ॥
andhasaṃsthaṃ ca nakṣatraṃ avarjam asthānakarmaṇi । (l.2)
pāṃsulohitavarṣeṣu maghānāma sanniteṣu ॥
ullākapātāṃ diśā dāhe grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ।
ṣaḍaśītimukhe *caiva sthāpanaṃ samācaret ॥* (l.3)
mārgaśīrṣamāṣāḍhaṃ mākha(gha)māsaṃ tathaiva ca ।
apraśastāni māsāni tasmānya(da)nyāni vratā ॥
uktamāseṣu *varṣeṣu pūrvapakṣe subha(pa)kṣake ।* (l.4)
suvāre sumuhūrteṣu sthāpanaṃ tu samārabhet ॥

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
rohiṇī *ca punarvasu puṣye mṛgaśirastathā ।* (l.5)
hastaṃ ca revatī naiva svātī caiva kurūttarā ॥
sthāpaye sthiranāśaṃ tu aṣṭame grahavarjitā । (p.97a, l.1)

vīratantre –
ayane dakṣiṇe naṣṭaḥ praśastaṃ cottarāyaṇe ।
śuklapakṣe caturthī ca saptamī daśamī tathā ॥
sthirarāśau *ca kartavyam ubhaye ravi kārayet ।* (l.2)
aṣṭamasthā grahā(hāḥ) sarve na kurvīta kadācana ॥
caturthī caiva ṣaṣṭhī ca aṣṭamī ca caturdaśi ।
navamī *pratipaścaiva pañcadaśyāṃ śubhā smṛtāḥ ॥* (l.3)
yamāṃ meṣāṇi śūrpāṇi śraviṣṭāḥ sarpavāruṇam ।
jyeṣṭhā ca trīṇi ca *pūrvāṇi tena ritaṃ caiva varjayet ॥* (l.4)
śeṣāśca tithayogāśca śāstusthāpanakarmaṇi ।
rāśikeṣu dhanuścaiva ca *tasya cararāśayaḥ ॥* (l.5)
apraśastā iti khyātā śeṣāstu śubhadā matāḥ ।
aśubhā sūryaśaurārā śubhayogaṃ ca śubhapradā ॥ (p.97b, 113a, l.1)
rohiṇyā nivasaṃ .....śanivārastu śobhanam ।
pūṣā sāvitrisaṃyuktā sūryavārau varasmṛtāḥ ॥
viśvedevāśca niyuktā kujavārau *smṛtaḥ ।* (l.2)
śuktāmbhorakānāṃ ca drekkāṇaṃ darśane ॥
punaśvāśastathā citradhaniṣṭhā śravaṇānvitam ।
bījastu revatī bhadrā *vārtha....tva...yaśvani ॥* (l.3)
śūdrasya tu makhā śreṣṭhā svātī pūrvā ca phalguni ।
etānyapi viśeṣeṇa dṛśyate pārame vare ॥ (l.4)
..................
uttarāyaṇakāle tu tato māsavivarjite ।
pūrvapakṣe tu puṇyakṣe(rkṣe) sulagne saṅgahe kṣite ॥
ādityahaste *saptamyāṃ kāle kanyodaye'thavā ।* (l.5)
sthāpayedbhāskaraṃ mantraṃ vipradevajñameva ca ॥
sthāpakaḥ śivaviprasya gṛhasthaṃ priyadarśinā । (p.98a, l.1)
śivaśāstrārthatattvajño vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ ॥
devāgnigurubhartā(ktā)stu saṃpūrṇāṃ gotrasa(saṃ)prati ।
kulino nipuṇaḥ śilpivāstu*devāśca pārakā ॥* (l.2)
sahāyādvā ....balaṃ caiva daivajñe lagnavit sudhi ।
vedavedāṅgatajño viprāstu mudrapāṭhakāḥ ॥
muhūrtamīkṣya *devajño gurave sannive(veśa)yet ।* (l.3)

kāraṇe – 
puṣyamāsādiṣāṇmāsaṃ di(de)vānāṃ tu divā bhavet ।
āṣāḍādi ca ṣāṇmāsaṃ rātri *ityavi(bhi)dhīyate ॥* (l.4)
divā māsaṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt rātrimāsaṃ vivarjayet ।
prājāpatyaṃ ca vatsaumyahastapauṣṇatriruttarā ॥
maitrāditya*maghāsvātiārdrāśravaṇāḥ śubhāḥ ।* (l.5)
eṣāmeva dinaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dhanadhānyavivardhanam ॥
dvitīyyāyāṃ tri(tṛ)tīyyāyāṃ pañcamī ṣaṣṭhi saptami । (p.98b, f.114a, l.1)
daśamyekādaśī vāpi śuklapakṣe trayodaśi ॥
pratipat paurṇamāsyāṃ vā kārayettu śivadvijaḥ ।
rāśayaṃ ca carāvarjayā(rjyā) dhānya*dhanuraśonā ॥* (l.2)
somabhārgavasaumyāyāṃ vārā śreṣṭatamā smṛtaḥ ।
eṣāmeṣodayaḥ śastāmatra somodadha(ya?)śvinā । (l.3)
aṣṭamasthāgrahāḥ sarve ...yaṃ .........sthitam ॥
khaṇḍastraṇāṃ tu nakṣatraṃ gulikāviṣya sṛjet ।
graheṇa viṣuve *caiva ayane vyatipā......ka(ke) ॥* (l.4)
.....llaśitramākhe(ghe) vāpi kṛtaṃ kāryaṃ vinaśyati ।
evaṃ parīkṣya kartavyaṃ sarvasampatkarāya ca । (l.5)

vīratantre –
devapratiṣṭhāṃ kathapā(yā)mi samyak śivasya viṣṇośca । 
tathā pareṣāṃ saumyāya daivaguravau ca śukre ॥
dṛśyamāne (p.99a, l.1) parivārakāraṇam । mādhe(ghe) tapasye tapasī pratiṣṭhā dhanāyurārogyakarā ca kartaiḥ caitre māhārugbhayadā ca śukre samāya (l.2)....putradhanaprasāda.... । āṣāḍhamāsādicatuṣṭaye ca kalatrasantānavināśana(naḥ) syāt । ūje ipaḥ kartuṃ nidhanapradā drāk samye sauveṣe (l.3) khalu duḥkhadāsāya .....śubhadaḥ samaste sadavai tatrādyaparaṃ vihāya । 
anyatra bhāgaṃ parimahṛtya kṛṣṇapakṣe viśastaḥ (l.4) khalapakṣayoścā(śca) । 
riktāpamānadiṣṭa.....gi....ṣu nindyā yogeṣu vai nāśikavarjiteṣu । 
dine mahādoṣavivarjite ca । śaśāṅkatā(l.5)balasaṃyuto'pi ॥
devasya yasyo sunidhipraśaste saṃsthāne karmaṇi vāsaraśca । 
karturdineśasya balaṃ sadaiva grāsipāgrāmi balaṃ vicārya ॥ (p.99b, f.115a, l.1) 
māsādyādipasaṃriktāmamāvāsyāṃ tithikṣayam ।
dhātrītanayā pāraṃ ca sthāpane parivarjayet ॥
mūle vā hi tripūrvāgni vidvevapi trayāmyaham । (l.2)
ārdrāṃ tyaktvānyaviṣṇeṣu surāṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ śubham ॥
hastratraye mitraharitraye ca pauṣṇadvayādityasurosyabheṣu ? ।
citrottarādharaśaśāṃ*.....ṣurvādyarasthāpanamuttamasya vā ॥* (l.3)
kīrtipradatve makaraṃ kṛśānobhayapradam ।
vṛddhikaraṃ narāṇāṃ lakṣmīkaramasthiramatvanādivāreṣu (l.4)
saṃsthāpanamāmananti śu...kartuṃ sūryabalopete ca bhū tārābalānvite ।
pañcāṅgaśuddhidivase pūrvāhne cāṣṭamodaye sūryendu*kujarāhvārkaketavo* (l.5)
lagnatātvitikartumitru(tra)pradā saumyā....śrīputrapautradā ॥
dvitīyye bhaktadāḥ saumyā ca candrā teṣu mā pare ।
tri(tṛ)tīyasthā gatā *saumyagrahā śrīputrasaukhyadām ॥* (p.100a, l.1)
caturthe lābhadāḥ saumyāḥ krūrāścandraśca duḥkhadāḥ ।
pañcame vyādhitāḥ krūrā śubhāṃ putradhanāditāḥ ॥ (l.2)
kṛtastatra pūrṇendukṣīrenduḥ putranāśataḥ । 
śatrūgaṃstatpadā saumyakrūrāśatruvināśanam ॥
pūrṇākṣiṇo'pi vā candraṣaṣṭe *ripugayapradā ।* (l.3)
saptame śatra(tru)dā krūrāḥ śubhāśca lābhadā samaḥ ॥
mṛtyusthānagatāḥ sarve grahāṃ śatruvināśadā ।
śrīpradā sendapaḥ saumyā *dharme tu rāgatapradāḥ ॥* (l.4)
sacandragā karmagā sa saumyā kīrtidā kleśadāḥ pare ।
lābhasthānagatāḥ sarve grahāḥ śrīputralābhadā ।
vyayasthāna*gatāḥ sarve bahuvyayakaraṃ nṛṇām ॥* (l.5)
bhaktyarthaṃ hīne kartāraṃ mantrahīne tu ri(ṛ)tvijam ।
striyaṃ lakṣaṇahīnena pratiṣṭhāsamo rupuḥ ॥ (p.100b, f.106, रत्नविन्यासम्, l.1)
॥ iti saṃhitāratnākare sarvakāryasammate devapratiṣṭhākālavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥


sthāpiteṣu bhavet satsthāpanaṃ dvidhā *bhavet ।* (l.2)
sthāpanaṃ ca pratiṣṭhāṃ ca sthāpanaṃ śilpisaṃyutāḥ ॥
pratiṣṭhā tadvihīnaṃ syāt mantreṇa sahi(ha) saṃbhavet ।
mantrarūpī mahādeva *śivaḥ paramakāraṇam ॥* (l.3)
mantra(ntraḥ) syāt paramajñānaṃ mantraṃ vācya sadāśivaḥ ।
vācyavācakasaṃbanadho mantrā yasyā ca dṛśyate ॥ (l.4)
vācyasya devadevasya lo(lokā)nugrahasya ca ।
liṅgacchinnaṃ śarīraṃ ca kasya te'tra janārdanā(na) ॥ 
śarīraṃ mantramevaṃ syāt śabdabrahmeti saṃjñitam । (l.5)
śarīraṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā vyaktāvyaktobhayātmakam ॥
trividhaṃ tat suraśreṣṭhaṃ pūjārthaṃ tasya nityaśaḥ ।
pratiliṅgasthitāmātraṃ pratiṣṭheti prakīrtitam ॥ (p.101a, रत्नविन्यासम्, l.1)

ratnavinyāsam –
ajite –
prathamaṃ ratnavinyāsaṃ dvitīyaṃ netramokṣaṇam ।
tṛtīyaṃ pratimāśuddhi caturthaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
jalādhivāsanaṃ *pañca ṣaṣṭhaṃ vai maṇḍapaṃ tataḥ ।* (l.2)
saptamaṃ śayanaṃ caiva aṣṭamaṃ homameva ca ॥
navamaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt daśamaṃ sthāpanaṃ bhavet । (l.3)
daśakarmamidaṃ proktaṃ teṣāṃ bhedaṃ śruṇuṣvathā(tha) ॥

vīratantre –
sthaṇḍilaṃ maṇḍapamadhye kārayedambujaṃ likhet । (l.4)
tanmadhye vinyaset pīṭhaṃ navakoṣṭhasamāyutam ॥
cakranīlādiratnāni pūrvavat vinyaseddhṛdā ।
skandaṃ tasyopari nyastvā susnigdhaṃ *sudṛḍhaṃ kuru ॥* (l.5)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
ratnanyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ tasya tasya pade'thavā ।
śālīnāṃ vikare tatra taṇḍulān tala vinyaset ॥
nava*vastrasamāyuktaṃ pratimāṃ tatra vinyaset ।* (p.101b, f.117a, गर्भविन्यासम्, चित्रविधि, l.1)
māṇikkaṃ madhyame sthāpya parita(taḥ) sthāpayet kramāt ॥
indranīlaṃ ca vaiḍūryaṃ sphaṭikaṃ mara*takaṃ(kadaṃ) tu vā ।* (l.2)
padmarāgaṃ pravālaṃ ca vajramauktikamucyate ॥
pūrvādīni nyaset ye(e)tān pañcabrahma samuccaran ।

vīratantre – (l.3)
citraṃ ca trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ ste(te)ṣāṃ bhedaṃ śruṇuṣvathā(tha) ।
śilāpradi(ti)maviprendrā citrakarmaṃ tu kārayet ॥
mṛṇmayaṃ śūlasaṃyuktaṃ bhikti*citre paṭe'pi ca ।* (l.4)
ete trividhamucyante sthāpanaṃ tu śruṇuṣvatha ॥
citrasya sannidhau caiva pratiṣṭhāmaṇḍapaṃ kuru । (l.5)

vīratantre –
vajraṃ maratakaṃ(kadaṃ) caiva vaiḍūryaṃ ca pravālakam ।
sphaṭikaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca puṣayarāgaṃ tathaiva ca ।
indranīlaṃ viprendra pūrvādi *vinyaset kramāt ॥* (p.102a, l.1)
māṇikyaṃ madhyame sthāpya padmasyopari saṃsthitam ।
navaratnaṃ pañcaratnaṃ vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ ॥
ratnānāmapi lābhe(mapyalābhe) *tu svarṇādīni ca kārayet ।* (l.2)

vīratantre –
prāsādaṃ madhyabhāgaṃ tu saptadhā parikalpayet ।
madhyamabrahmabhāgaṃ tu devikaṃ tadanantaram ॥ (l.3)
tadante mānuṣaṃ jñeyaṃ paiśācaṃ bhittimāśra(śri)taḥ ।
tatra madhye sthitaṃ samyak pādaśailā nyasettataḥ ॥
ratnanyāsādisaṃyojyā(jya) *susnigdhaṃ kārayettataḥ ।* (l.4)

suprabhede –
acalasya śilāgrābhyāṃ śruṇudhvaṃ sthāpanāvidhi ।
ratnanyāsaṃ jalāvāsaṃ śayanaṃ tu *pradakṣiṇam ॥* (l.5)
citrabhāse tu varjaṃ na hi ratnaje susamācaret ।
sakalānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ samame sa dvidhau matam ॥

jalādhivāsanaṃ sthānamevameva samācaret ॥

suprabhede –

bhīsaṃhitāyām –

māṇikkaṃ caiva vaiḍūryaṃ nīlaṃ maratakaṃ(gadaṃ) tathā ॥
mauktikaṃ caiva gomedhaṃ vajraṃ puṣyaṃ pravālakam ।
etāni *hṛdayenaiva madhyādīśāta(śāntaṃ) vinyaset ॥* (l.2)
tilānīvāramudgaṃ ca yavaṃ vai sarṣapaṃ tathā ।
śyāmamāḍhakulutthaṃ ca priyaṅguta(na)vabījakam ॥ (l.3)
suvarṇenakṛtānīhā(ha) bījāni kavacena tu ।
aja(ñja)naṃ ga?ritāraṃ ca śyāmaṃ caiva manaśśilā ॥

karavīraṃ padmapuṣpaṃ punnāgaṃ raktapañcakam ॥
