cambukaṃ ca danaṃ caiva *droṇapuṣpaṃ ca yūthikā ॥* (l.3)
evaṃ me(ma)teṣu sarveṣu nyastvā dravyāṇi mantravit ।

vīratantre –
padmapatrotpalaṃ paścāt *pāṭaladampakaṃ tathā ।* (l.4)
punnāgaṃ kurakhā dadyāt nandyāvartaṃ ca mallikā ॥
śvetākaṃ vakulairyuktaṃ puṣpatoyamiti smṛtam । (l.5)

kāmike –
phalodakam –
nāraṅgalikucā.....phalairiktaphalairyutam ।

ajite –
panaso nālikeraṃ ca mātuluṅga*dvayaṃ tathā ।* (p.112, l.1)
nāraṅgadvayaraktamā.......bhradhanyuktarasmiphalodakam ॥

kāmike – pañcagavyam –
prasthapādaṃ ghṛtaṃ caiva dvipādaṃ dadhirucyate । (l.2)
tripādaṃ kṣīramityuktaṃ caturthaṃ caiva gomayam ॥
ṣaṣṭhaṃ caiva tu gomūtraṃ pañcagavyamiti smṛtam ।

suprabhede –
etadvitri[catuḥ]*prasthaṃ pañcagavyādimānakam ।* (l.3) – iti kāmike dravyaparimāṇavidhiḥ । ?

ekapādaṃ bhavetsarpa(rpaṃ) dvipādaṃ dadhirucyate ।
tripādaṃ *kṣīramityuktaṃ prasthaṃ gomayasārakam ॥* (l.4)
sārdhaprasthaṃ tu gomūtraṃ ekadvitriguṇaṃ tu vā ।
kuśodbhave tu ṣaṭprasthaṃ *samaṃ sthāpayet samam ॥* (l.5)

kāraṇe –
kṣīraṃ tu pañcatriphalaṃ dadhiṃ syāt phaladvayam ।
gojalaṃ ṣaṭkamityuktaṃ phalārdhamātraṃ khalu (p.112b, f.128a, l.1) gomayaṃ syāt phalaṃ kuśā.....ti pañca..... ।

vātule –
kṣīraṃ tu madhyame sthāpya gomūtraṃ pūrvakoṣṭhake ।
gomayaṃ *dakṣiṇe sthāpya paścime dadhi vinyaset ॥* (l.2)
uttare ghṛta(taṃ) saṃsthāpya vahnau tu .....ravinyaset ।
nairi(ṛ)te phalasāraṃ ca vāyavye gula*sārakam ॥* (l.3)
aiśānyāṃ nālikeraṃ ca evameva tu vinyaset ।
kṣīraṃ tatra prasthasaṃyuktaṃ gomūtraṃ kuṅkuma?dvayam ॥
gomayaṃ kuḍupaṃ *caiva dadhivatkuḍupadvayam ।* (l.4)
ājyaṃ kuḍupamityuktaṃ payaśca kuḍupadvayam ॥
kuḍubantamayaproktaṃ phalasāraṃ tataḥ dvayam । (l.5)
gulasāraṃ tataḥ prasthaṃ nālikeraṃ tathā bhavet ॥
navadravyaṃ ca sakhyātāṃ adhidevavāṃ tata(taḥ) śruṇu ।
agastyaṃ ca pulastyaṃ ca viśvāmitraṃ *ca bha(bhā)rgavāt ॥* (p.113a, l.1)
gārgapaṃ tumburuṃ caiva bha(ga)vādīnāṃ ca mudgalam ।
ri(ṛ)ṣibrahmapaṭaṃ caiva kalaśaṃ pāṇisaṃbi(jñi)tam ॥
puṣpaiḥ kūrcaiḥ *samabhyarcya svasvamantreṇa deśikāḥ ।* (l.2)
dhūpadīpaṃ ca yatkṛtvā liṅgaṃ śarabhi(surabhi)mudrayā ॥
tattanmantreṇa saṃpūjya darśamudrāṃ pradarśayet ।
pradhānaṃ *kalaśe madhye sarva....yojayedbudhaḥ ॥* (l.3)
sapidhānaṃ ca sarvatra astramantreṇa buddhimān ।

kāraṇe –
saṃsthāpitatadra(ddra)vyā *tailādīnyabhiṣecayet ।* (l.4)
piṣṭāmalakacūrṇāśca rajanīṃ gharṣayetpunaḥ ॥
pañcagavyena saṃsthāpya pañcāmṛtaṃ tadantake । (l.5)
tadante phalasāraṃ ca gavyodakaṃ tadantake ॥
nālikerodakenaiva sthāpayet parameśvaram ।
śītalaṃ tajjalaṃ kṛtvā yatheṣṭa*mabhiṣecayet ॥* (p.113b, f.129a, l.1)

kāraṇe –
pañcagavyābhiṣekaṃ tu pañcabrahmasamuccaran ।
pañcāmṛtaṃ sagandhodaṃ ṣaḍaṅgairabhiṣecayet ॥ (l.2)
bhogyapatraphalekasmin kiñcidanyaṃ nya(na) vidyate ।
pā(vā)lumūledyagaṅgādyādeva yajña samantragān ॥
saṃsthitāḥ sarva tasmāgodehaje ṣaḍbhi*mudrādyairocanāntakam ।* (l.3)
śivapriyai viśeṣeṇa śivapūjāṃ samācaret ॥
pañcagavyavidhānena pañcagavyaṃ pṛthi(tha)k *pṛthi(tha)k ।* (l.4)
pūrvāhne caiva madhyāhne liṅge kṛtvābhiṣecayet ॥
gorocanāmathābhyarcya candanaṃ kṣodamiśrakān । (l.5)
śivaliṅgaṃ tu yo bhaktyā sakṛdvāpi samarcayet ॥
so''nandaphalamāpnoti śaivasaṅgamamiṣyate ।
jalaṃ payo dadhyapi *sarpimanyaṃ liṅge sakṛdvāravidhiprayuktam ।* (p.114a, उत्सवम्, l.1)
evaṃ hi puṃsā kramaśarjitādhyeśatottaraṃ saṃhari(ra)tīti vṛddhiḥ । 
yaḥ pañcagavye sakṛtkaroti *liṅge niṣekaṃ śucirapramattaḥ ।* (l.2)
sa saptajanmaprabhavaiśca pāpairvicacate nātra hi saṃśri(śa)yo'sti ॥
tasmācca godehabhajaiśca *ṣaḍbhirgatyā ...yane ।* (l.3)
śivaṃ sakṛtvā samarcya yojyo vidhipadmaśuṣyāstasyāpi ca sā phaladatu janmā । 
idaṃ *sarveṣu kartavyaṃ sakṛdvāpi viyannarai(raiḥ) ।* (l.4)
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇaparvaṇi cottarān ।
ajite – iti pratiṣṭhādīpikāyāṃ (l.5) pratimāśuddhipaṭalaḥ ॥

(utsavam -)
utsavārambhakāle tu kiñcidasti śruṇuṣva... ।
snapanaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ caiva kartavyaṃ tu sudeśikaḥ ॥ (p.114b, f.130a, l.1)
sthāpanaṃ vidhisaṃproktaṃ calaṃ caivācalaṃ tathā ।
calasthāpanamevoktaṃ kartavyaṃ tat prajāya te ॥
calaṃ tu na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ *kartṛnāśanam ।* (l.2)

vidveṣaṇasaṃhitāyām –
utsavādiṣu kāle tu pratiṣṭhādyaṃ na kārayet ।
vidhāya śāntihomaṃ tu *pratiṣṭhādyantu vā nayet ॥* (l.3)

kāmike –
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ vidhināśodhanaṃ pūrve mārjanaṃ cāpi kārayet । 
śuddhatoyena *saṃprāptā ....ktaiḥ sarvakuśeśayet ॥* (l.4)
grahaśeṣaṃ ca sarvatra badhnyādvidhinā dhyavām । 
sahasā sthāneṣu vidhīnāṃ badhniyā*dvṛṣabhadhvajam ॥* (l.5)
pūrva(pūrṇa)kummabhaṃ pratidvāraṃ maṅgalaṃ kuru saṃyutam । 
upayukta(ktaṃ) samāyojya darbhamālāvalambitām ॥
rathe vāśibikāyāṃ *vā pratimaṃ bandhakautukam ।* (p.115a, l.1)
sarva(rvā)bharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ vigrahādyāmśukāṃ tathā ॥
mālāpuṣpaprabhāyuktāṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam । (l.2)
nānāchatrasamopetaṃ pañcacāmarasaṃyutam ॥
tālabṛndasamopetaṃ dhūpadīpasamanvitam ।
gītanṛttasamāyuktaṃ grāma...yeḥ śanaiḥ ॥ (l.3)

ajite –
vidhināma śodhanaṃ pūrvaṃ mārjanādiṃ ca kārayet ।
śuddhatoyena sarvatra vidhi(vīthī) pūrvatra seca[yet] ॥
dhvajaiśca *vividhaiścaiva patākādyaistathaiva ca ।* (l.4)
kadalī caiva saphalaiḥ puṣpaiśca pūrṇakumbhakaiḥ ॥
muktādāmaiśca *dīpaiśca bhuvanaṃ prati pūṣayet ।* (l.5)

kāmike –
tadagre ghoṣayet samyk sarva(rvā)todyakrameṇa tu ।
paṭahairmardalaiścaiva *bherīphaṇasaḍuṇḍubhiḥ ॥* (p.115b, f.131a, l.1)
...(jha)llarī  jhajharī motte(mūrte)ṣṭaguṇa...stathā ।
madhyataḥ pṛṣṭhataścaiva ghoṣayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥ (l.2)
sarve vīṇādante tu pra(vra)jeyuḥ kramaśastataḥ ।
evaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ grāmaṃ vā nagaṃ(garaṃ) tu vā ॥

vātule –
sarvābharaṇasaṃkte(yuktaṃ) *chatrapaṭṭāṃśukaṃ tathā ।* (l.3)
mālāpuṣpaprabhāyuktaṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
chatracāmarasaṃyuktaṃ piñcadhvajasamāyutam । (l.4)

suprabhede –
grāmādividhayaḥ(vīthayaḥ) śodhyā nimnonnatavivarjitam ।
vālukāstaraṇaṃ kuryāt dvidhayaścaivāpi vidhayaḥ ॥ (l.5)
vidhānaiśca ....je chatre kramukairnālikeradīpamālaiḥ phalai(laiḥ) puṣpaiḥ bhavanaṃ prati bhūṣayet । (l.6)
kautukaiḥ beradānānte kuryāt brapradakṣiṇam ।
sarvābharaṇasaṃyu(yuktam) aṅkurairabhiśobhitam ॥
nānāpuṣpasamāyuktaṃ *nānāgandhānulepanam ।* (p.116a, l.1)
nānāberasamāyuktaṃ mā vrateyuḥ samagrataḥ ॥
sarvato samāyuktaṃ nṛttageyasamākulam ।
tadante *rudragaṇikā sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥* (l.2)
tadante gāyakāḥ proktā tadante nṛttakā vrajet ।
nānāpiñchadhvajaiśchatrai(traiḥ) nānā*cāmarasaṃyutam ॥* (l.3)
nānā......samāyuktaṃ pādukairupaśobhitam ।
tadante ghoṣaye nṛttā sa[rvāto]dyakrameṇa tu ॥
sarvataḥ *pṛṣṭhato ......e(ye) daṇḍebhya.....laṃ vrajet ।* (l.4)
evaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt praviśedālayaṃ bhavet ॥

dīpte –
grāma*pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt jalai(le) caivādhivāsanam ।* (l.5)

vīratantre –
lambakūrcasamāyuktaṃ brahmaghoṣaiḥ samāyutam ।
vitāna*dhvajasaṃyuktaṃ muktādāmairalaṅkṛtam ॥* (l.6)
nānābhaktajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ kuryā(ryād) grāmapradakṣiṇam ।
nadyāṃ vāpi taṭāke vā tattīre sthaṇḍilaṃ *kuru ॥* (p.116b, f.132a, l.1)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā jalādhivāsanaṃ kuru ।
॥ cintye pratiṣṭhātantre grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ pañcamapaṭalaḥ ॥ (l.2)

raṅge vā śibikāyāṃ vā ārohya pratimaṃ tataḥ ।
gītavādyasamopetaṃ śaṅkhakāhalasaṃyutam ॥
nānādhvajasamāyuktaṃ nānāpiñchasamanvitam । (l.3)
nānābhaktajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
nadyādīn praviśettatra jalādhivāsanaṃ kuru ।
nadī taṭākatīre vā hṛde *gatvā viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.4)
tīre samatalaṃ kṛtvā gomayenānulepayet ।
śālinā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā pratimā(māṃ) tatra vinyaset ॥ (l.5)
apanīya samālya pratimaṃ snapayettataḥ ।
navavastreṇa saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) kūrcenaiva samanvitam ॥
pratimāṃ caiva vipreṇa *jale caiva nipātane ।* (l.6)
caturgātrasamāyuktaṃ prapāṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ ।
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ kalaśānya(na)ṣṭa vinyaset । (p.117a, māda(sa)மழை(varṣā) jalādhivāsam, l.1)
savastrān sā[pidhān] kṛtvā ......parasamanvitam ॥
lokapā ca படில நாசங்கடுந்துஸிகட்டுச்சிமலுபடிலெங்குமழை(l.2)யுண்டாம் ...லவெடிச்சிலம்பு பொதுபுறக்குடே(மே) சங்கிறமநெரசுருங்குமென்றே நிகழ்த்து. 
phalabaliṃ dadyāt dinaṃprati viśeṣatā । (l.3) 
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā saptarātramathāpi vā ॥
dīpahīnaṃ bhavedyāpi tasmāddīpaṃ samācaret । 
vāstuhomaṃ divā *kuryāt lakṣaṇena samanvitam ॥* (l.4)
dinānte caiva vipreṇa jalāduttīrya deśikaḥ ।
ālayaṃ praviśettatra puṣpadantapade *sthitaḥ ॥* (l.5)
snāpaye(yet) pañcagavyena vyomavyāpi samuccaran ।

vīratantre –
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā jale caivādhivāsayet । (l.6)
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ kalaśānaṣṭa vinyaset ॥
savastrān sahiraṇyāṃśca vidyeśvarasamanvitān ।
jalāduttīrya pūrvedyu*rgavyaṃ prakṣālayettataḥ ॥* (p.117b, f.133a, l.1)

vīratantre – 
nadītaṭākatīre vā gomayenānulepayet ।
pīṭhaṃ tatraiva nikṣipya gaurī *tatropavinyaset ॥* (l.2)
snāpayetpañcagavyena jalādhivāsanaṃ kuru ।
navavastreṇa saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) kūrcenaiva samanvitam ॥
jalamadhye *nyaseddevīṃ gaurībījena deśikaḥ ।* (l.3)
pratimāṃ kaṇṭhalagnena jale śailādhivāsayet ॥
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā pañca*rātramathāpi vā ।* (l.4)

vīratantre -
citrapratiṣṭhāṃ ki devā ? brūhi me parameśvaram(ra) ।
īśvarauvāca –
jalādhivāsanam *uktaṃ śayane śāyayettataḥ ।* (l.5)
tantramantreṇa saṃpratyaśayanaṃ ? pañca kalpayet ॥
darpaṇaṃ śāyayettatra pracchannapaṭasaṃyutam ।

vīratantre – (l.6)
gauṇapakṣekarātraṃ vā vaidayāmamathāpi vā ।
jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryāt rātrau caiva ma(a)nāraye(bhe)t ।
saudhe *paṭṭe tathā citre noktaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇam ॥* (p.118a, citram sideheading, l.1) 
jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryāt teṣāmagre prajāpate ।
śālibhistaṇḍulaṃ kṛtvā *pūrvaṃ nalinaṃ likhet ॥* (l.2)
darbhaiścaiva paristīrya puṣpairjālaiśca śobhitam ।
aṣṭadroṇajalaiḥ pūrṇabhāṇḍaṃ veṣṭana*pūjayet ॥* (l.3)
kṣālayedastramantreṇa sthāpaye(yet) sthaṇḍilopari ।
vastreṇa dviratadvastraṃ tamūrdhve tarpaṇaṃ nyaset ॥
pratibimba*yutaṃ tatra yajñayeddeśikottamaḥ ।* (l.4)
tanmūrdhve lambakūrcaṃ tu vinyaseddhṛdayena tu ॥
catuṣpādīn nyasettatra tanmūrdhve'ri *nyasedghaṭam ।* (l.5)
ūrdhvakumbhopakumbhau ca catuṣpādīn tathaiva ca ॥
śītakumbhasya vidhivannkāralakārānvitam ।
jalādhivāsanaṃ *hyevaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kārayettataḥ ॥* (l.6)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
ekarātraṃ tadardhaṃ vā jale caivādhivāsayet ।
paścādutthāpya *liṅgaṃ tu snānākhye vinyaseddhṛdaḥ ॥* (p.118b, f.134a, l.1)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām – 
kuryājjalādhivāsaṃ tu nadyāṃ vāpi taṭākake ।
bhūme samatalaṃ *kṛtvā śuddhiṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat ॥* (l.2)
mūrdhādipādaparyantaṃ vastrairdarbhaiśca veṣṭayet ।
śāyayejjalamadhye tu *prākśirāṃskahṛdāmbudhaḥ ॥* (l.3)
ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ vā trirātraṃ vādhivāsayet ।

dīpte –
nadyāṃ vāpi taṭāke vā kūpe *vāpi caturvidhe ।* (l.4)
kūpāddaśaguṇaṃ vāpi snānaṃ vāpi vidhīyate ॥
vāpi śataguṇaṃ jñeyaṃ taṭākaṃ tu yathākramam । (l.5)
taṭākāt sahasraguṇitaṃ nadyāṃ vā snānamuttamam ॥
koṭitaṭaguṇaṃ proktaṃ samudrasaṅgame bhavet ।
dīpte jalādhivāsanaṃ *kuryāttadvidhirucyate ।* (l.6)
jalatīre samādāya pratimāṃ prāṅmukho sudhīḥ ॥
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu tajjalairabhiṣecayet । (p.119a, l.1)
anyaṃ tu uktivinyasya praṇavaṃ samudāharat ॥
saṃveṣṭya kūrcavastrābhyāṃ pratimāṃ hṛdayāṇunā ।
pūrvavajjalamadhye tu śāyaye*tphalakopari ॥* (l.2)
tatrottamapītakālaṃ nītvā deśikottamaṃ pūjite ।
jaladroṇitripādūrdhve darpaṇe tu nidhāpayet ॥ (l.3)
tadghaṭastvodakenaiva abhiṣekaṃ samācaret ।
arcanoktaṃ samabhyarcya prabhūtahaviṣaṃ dadet ॥

raurave – (l.4)
ekarātraṃ tu yāmaṃ vā dvā(dvi)yāmā vā jale vaset ।

raurave –
nadyāṃ vāpi taṭāke vā prapāṃ kṛtvābhisundaram । (l.5)
tattīre pratimāṃ sthāpya pavamānena secare(ye)t ॥
vastreṇa veṣṭayet sarvān kuśaiśca pariveṣṭayet ।
jalamadhye *caturgātraṃ kṛtvā tatre(trai)va śāyayet ॥* (l.6)
prākchiraścordhvavakraṃ(ktraṃ) tu garṇamagre jale budhaḥ ।
pañcarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā *ekalātramathāpi vā ॥* (p.119b f.135, l.1)

dīpte –
rathādau devamāropya sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ।
paraṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtya kuryājjalā*dhivāsanam ॥* (l.2)
trirātramekarātraṃ vā nadyāṃ vāpi taṭākake ।

kāraṇe –
mṛṇmajaṃ(yaṃ) śailaṃ caivā(va) kramaśo lohamucyate । (l.3)
mṛṇmaye tu daśā noktam adhivāsajalena tu ॥
samā mānamanyat sarveṣāṃ yathāśakti vidhīyyate ।

suprabhede – (l.4)
lohañcera(cedra)tnavināyāsaṃ kṛtvā nayanamokṣaṇam ।
jalādhivāsanaṃ bimbaṃ śailaje tu viśeṣataḥ ॥

suprabhede –
patraṃ caruṃ *carannityaṃ śravanti niśi nimnakam ।* (l.5)
tasmā(smāt) snānaṃ ca pānaṃ ca na kurvīta kadācana ॥
nadyaḥ samudragāścaiva taṭākāśca danaṃ ? tathā ।
pallavā pavamāścaiva *saṅgamaṃ puṇyatīrthakam ॥* (l.6)

snānamaṇḍapam –
uktakālastu tatraiva nītvā *tasmājjalāśaye(yā)t ।* (l.2)
snānamaṇḍapamānīyaṃ madhyame saṃniveda(śa)yet ।
prāṅmukhaṃ pratimāṃ tatra tatra vastrādikaṃ nyaset ॥
tato gandhāmbunā devam *abhiṣicya śayet ।* (l.3)
gātryā?bhyarcanaṃ cāpi puṇyāhaṃ tatra kārayete ॥
badhvā pratisaraṃ homaṃ savyahaste hṛda(dā) tadā । (l.4) 
taśca(cca) kūrcavastrābhyāṃ veṣṭayet pratimāṃ sudhī ॥

ajite –
jalādānīya bimbaṃ tu snānavedyāṃ niśā? bhavet । (l.5)
gandhaiśca sodakaiścaiva astrai(straiḥ) caivābhiṣecayet ॥
gandhādyairarcayedvidvān badhvā pratisaraṃ tataḥ ।
lambakūrca*samāyuktaṃ navavastreṇa veṣṭayet ॥* (l.6)
śayane śāyayet paścāt prākchiraścordhvavaktrakam ।

raurave – (p.129b, f.136a, l.1)
jalā(lād)gaurī samānīyā snānaśvabhre nidhāpayet ।
vyapohya vastrakūrcādīn gavyaiḥ kṛtvābhiṣecanam ॥
śuddhāmbubhiḥ snāpayet *paścāt gauryāstatra mudāharan ।* (l.2)
gandhādibhirhṛdābhyarcya navavastre dharottamam ॥
badhvā pratisaraṃ paścāt kūrcamālambamūrdhni । (l.3)
śayane śāyayetpaścāt prāṅmukhaścordhvavaktrakam ॥

raurave -
mantranyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam ।
hastābhyāṃ *sparśayettatra kādipādāntameva ca ॥* (l.4)
aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntaṃ pañcabrahmakramānya(nnya)set ।
madhyāditarjanībhyāntaṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vinyaset kramāt ॥ (l.5)
astaṃ karatale caiva vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ ।
prākṛtaṃ bhāvamutsṛjya śivo'hamiti bhāvayet ॥

svarṇasūtraṃ tu saṃyuktaṃ kautukaṃ bandhayettataḥ ॥
phalatrayaṃ ca karpūraṃ mukure(ṭe) candanena tu sālosya? ca । (p.121a, l.1)
..........lepayettu samantataḥ ॥

pratimaṃ navavastraiśca vidhāya ca sakalaṃ tataḥ ॥ (l.2)

vīratantre –
sthaṇḍilaṃ śālibhiḥ kuryāt aṣṭadroṇapramāṇakaiḥ । (l.3)

toyādhivāsanaṃ bimbam ānīyyā snānamaṇḍape ।
pañcagavya*kaṣāyodapañcāmṛtasamanvitaiḥ ॥* (l.4)
bhrū....ttāyai phalapuṣpādyairgandhatoyaiḥ kuśodakaiḥ ।
snānayogyai*rathānyaiśca snāpayitvārcayet kuru ॥* (l.5)
suvarṇaṃ varṇasaṃbhūtaṃ kautukaṃ bandhayet karaiḥ । (p.121b, l.1)
puṇyāhaṃ vācanaṃ ghoraiḥ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
pradakṣiṇamathānīya prāsādaṃ vā sunādikṣa? ।
śayene śāyayitvā tu *raktavastreṇa veṣṭayet ॥* (l.2)
saṃpūjya mūlamantraiśca navamantraiśca tattvadam ।

kāraṇe –
jalāduttīrya bimbāśca snānavedyupari *nyaset ।* (l.3)

havirdanvādhivā ?  sāntatsamāpyevaṃ puroktavat ॥

samidhā sadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena homayet ।
aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu ॥
śaktumīśena juhuyāt *tilāyai hṛdayena tu ।* (l.3)
siddhārthaṃ śirasā hutvā śikhāyāṃ tu phalāhuti(tiḥ) ॥
apūpaṃ kavacenaiva mudgān netreṇa *homayet ।* (l.4)
madhumastreṇa juhuyāt kṣīraṃ mūlena homayet ॥
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā pratyekāhutimācaret ।
dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā astreṇa sparśāhutiḥ ॥

raurave –
agnyādhānādikaṃ karma agnikāryoktamācaret ।
samidājyacaruṃ kṣīraṃ *tilaṃ sarṣapamāṣakaiḥ ॥* (p.129b, f.146a, l.1)
priyaṅgamuga(dma)gulaṃ caiva phanasasya phalairapi ।
....gāstrai(straiḥ) śataṃ hutvā devadevośca *kuṇḍayoḥ ॥* (l.2)
mūlena ājyacaruṃ hutvā rātriśeṣaṃ vyapohya ca ।

raurave –
kuryādadhyayanaṃ dikṣu japanmantraṃ vidu(di)kṣu ca । (l.3)
japedagnidiśi pūrvāyāṃ yajurvedaṃ tu ॥ ?
dakṣiṇe sāmavedaṃ saumyāyāṃ paścime'tharvameva ca । (l.4)
prāsādamīśakoṇeṣu āghāraṃ [ādhāraṃ] vahnigocare ॥
nairi(ṛ)tyāṃ vahnibījaṃ tu umābījaṃ tu vāyave ।
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ (l.5)
samidhājyacaruṃ lājān satilārthakamāṣakā ।
yavanīvāraśrīpatratilaiḥ vaikādaśā ime ॥
aśvatthodumbaralpa(pla)kṣavaṭā pūrvādidikṣu ca । (p.130a, l.1)
śamīkhādiraśrīvṛkṣaṃ māyūrāśca vidikṣu ca ॥
pradhānaṃ tu palāśaṃ syāt sarveṣāṃ idhya(dhma) ucyate ।
svadisthā mūrtipāḥ *sarve tviṣṭasambhārasammatāḥ ॥* (l.2)
śivāgnimānayetpūrvam ācāryo vidhinā sahā(ha) ।
soṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyaṃ ca divyagandhānulepanam ॥ (l.3)
kuṇḍakuśo'nvayet pūrvaṃ kṣālanaṃ tadanantaram ।
tṛtīyaśoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā caturthamācamanaṃ bhavet ॥
ullekhanaṃ purāstreṇa *kavacenābhyukṣaṇaṃ smṛtam ।* (l.4)
pūṣaṃ mukhāstisrarekhā brahmarudrākhyavaiṣṇavaḥ ॥
uttarābhimukho'nye kasva(śya)po tu na *saṃśayaḥ ।* (l.5)
gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ pūṣā śivāgnimānayetdguruḥ ॥
mathitvā vahnimādāya śrotryāṅgārajaṃ tu vā ।
śarāve tāmbra*pātrāṇi nikṣipyāgniṃ parigrahet ॥* (p.130b, f.147a, l.1)
astramantreṇa saṃśodhya kavacenāvakuṇḍa(kuṇṭha)yet ।
bījamukhyena mantreṇa *amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuru ॥* (l.2)
agnibījenābhyarcya gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt ।
viṣṭaraṃ kuṇḍamadhye tu hṛdayena *prakalpayet ॥* (l.3)
sthāpyā(pya) vāgīśvarīṃ tatra tu snātvā viśeṣataḥ ।
bhrāmaye tu tridhā vahniṃ yonimārgeṇa nikṣipet ॥ (l.4)
sthāpya sadyena mantreṇa vratānte ...stri(tri)dhā smaran ।
garbhādhānamidaṃ proktaṃ tataḥ puṃsavanaṃ śruṇu ॥
vāmadevena *śikhāyā gandhairabhyarcya deśikam ।* (l.5)
bhavet puṃsavanaṃ hyevaṃ sīmantaṃ tu tataḥ śruṇu ॥
puṣpaṃ dadyādviśeṣeṇa *śirasā bahurūpiṇā ।* (p.131a, l.1)
vaktreṇa rasavarmayuktena ? arghyaṃ dadyāt sujātakam ॥
īśānena kayuktena dhūpadīpaṃ *pradāpayet ।* (l.2)
nāmakarma iti proktaṃ pañcasamskāramucyate ॥
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam ।
hṛdayenai *mantreṇa dadyāt sarvaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.3)
śivāgnenāgnimādāya agnikuṇḍe tu nikṣipet ।
prāgagrai*ruda(daga)gre ca kuṇḍadarbhaiḥ paristaret ॥* (l.4)
ājyasthālīṃ praṇītāṃ ca prokṣaṇī sṛksṛvau tathā ।
carusthālīṃ tu *pañcaite vasurūpeṇa sādhayet ॥* (l.5)
prokṣaṇīpātramādāya pūrayet prokṣakena tu ।
tiraḥ pavitreṇa *pūrvaṃ syāt kuśāgraṃ tatra nikṣipet ॥* (p.131b, f.148a, l.1)
pātrān saṃprokṣayet tūṣṇī(ṣṇīm) utthānāni ca sarvāṇi ।
brāhma(brahmā)ṇaṃ dakṣiṇe *sādhya kūrcaṃ tatraiva nikṣipet ॥* (l.2)
viṣṇuṃ vai vāmata(taḥ) sthāpya praṇītā tatra nikṣipet ।
pūrvapaścimakuṇḍe tu uttarā*bhimukhasthitau ॥* (l.3)
anyeṣu prāṅmukhobhūtvā homaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ ।
ājyaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paryagni*plavanādibhiḥ ।* (l.4)
sṛ(sru)vasamskāramevaṃ tu sṛksamskārameva ca ॥
tistupari? dharpa(rbha) sthāpya paścime dakṣiṇottare ।
agnīśānadiśa(śe) *sthāpya vāmāghoramidhā? vubhau ॥* (l.5)
aditenādimantreṇa pariṣecanamārabhet ।
āghāramājyabhāgaṃ ca vidikṣu *juhuyāt ॥* (p.132a, l.1)
puruṣāghorasadyaiśca vāmetaravādadiśi? ।
īśaṃ namacyato (na madhyato) hutvā vahnibījaṃ daśaṃ hunet ॥ (l.2)
kṣurikenotsave dva(va)ktramastreṇaiva tu niṣkṛtam(niṣkataḥ) ।
śivāgniṃ janito hyevā(va) paścānnikṣipya viṣṭaram ॥
āvāhanādiyatkarmaṃ *pūrvoktavidhinā kuru ।* (l.3)
śivāgnau śivamāvāhyaṃ pañcāvaraṇasaṃyutam ॥
bhavaśca pūrvadigbhāge āgneyāmabhrameva ca । (l.4)
sarvaṃ(vaḥ) ca dakṣiṇe kuṇḍe vāmadevastu naiṛte ।
rudraśca paścime bhāge bhīmo vai vāyugocare ॥
paśupati*ścottare kuṇḍe īśānyāmiti yeva ca ।* (l.5)
triṇetrāścaturbhujāścaiva jagāma kṛtadhāriṇam ॥
kuṇḍamadhye nyase*detān tadbāhye ca ....... ।* (p.132b, f.149a, l.1)
tadbāhye mārutāścaiva lokapālāśca tadbahi(hiḥ) ।
lokapālavihīnā(nāḥ) syu(syuḥ) madhyame *homakarmaṇi ॥* (l.2)
marutopacaro hīnakanyase homakarmaṇi ।
kuṇḍādhidevatāḥ sarvā vinyaset kuṇḍamadhyame ॥ (l.3)
pādyādidīpaparyantamarcayettu vidhānataḥ ।
śivamantra(ntraṃ) samuccārya śivāgnau juhuyācchatam ॥
anyeṣāṃ devatānaṃ(nāṃ) ca *tattanntrairdaśāhutiḥ ।* (l.4)
brahmāṅgaiśca tato hutvā pratyekaṃ tu śata(tā)hutiḥ ॥
triyambakenaiva mantreṇa samidhāhutimācaret । (l.5)
sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhārdhamathāpi vā ॥
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vāpi pratyekaṃ juhuyāt kramāt ।
ājyaṃ hutvā hudā *mantrī pūrvasaṅkhyākrameṇa tu ॥* (p.133a, जपम्, l.1)
ghṛtamimikṣeti mantreṇa hutvā tatsaṅkhyayā ghṛtam ।
caruṃ hutvā tu sadyena davasyatvā *caruṃ hunet ॥* (l.2)
śirasā vā juhutvā tu yemāṣiśeti mantrataḥ ।
saktuṃ vāmeti mantreṇa āporājeti mantrataḥ ॥
siddhārthān *śikhayā hutvā yo rudrastadantare ।* (l.3)
mānuṣīṃ hutvā hyaghoreṇa yeṣatate rudramantrataḥ ॥
kavacena yavaṃ hutvā triyambakenaiva *homayet ।* (l.4)
ticāraṃ puruṣeṇaiva kuryā ye sveti mantrataḥ ॥
astreṇa juhuyāt patraṃ(tra)mapsu somo bravīmiti ।
tilān *hutvena mantreṇa yatteyamiti mantrataḥ ॥* (l.5)
dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā spṛśelliṅgaṃ samastakam ।

dīpte –
kadrudramācamanaṃ ca pūrva*bhāge japedbudhaḥ ।* (p.133b, f.150a, l.1)
śrīsūktaṃ pavamānaṃ ca puruṣasūktaṃ triyambakam ।
śāntidvayaṃ japeddhīmān dakṣiṇe tu viśeṣataḥ ॥
kalpa*sūktaṃ śayanavargaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ tu vāruṇam ॥* (l.2)
paścime tu yajeddhīmān pavamānaṃ hiraṇyakam ।
nayanāgniṃ virūpākṣam *uttare tu japedbudhaḥ ॥* (l.3)
śrīsūktapavamānaṃ ca narasūktaṃ triyambakam ।
śāndidvayaṃ japeddhīmān dakṣiṇe tu *viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.4)
kalpasūktaṃ śayanavargaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ ca vāruṇam ।
paścime tu japeddhīmān pavamānaṃ hiraṇyakam ॥ (l.5)
devavrataṃ puruṣavrataṃ jyeṣṭhā sāmaṃ tathaiva ca ।
kayānaścitrakaṃ caiva rūpākṣaṃ cottare japet ॥
ācāryaṃ mūrtipaiḥ *sārdhaṃ tato homaṃ samācaret ।* (p.134a, l.1)

kāraṇe –
ṛgādidhya(dyadhya)yanaṃ cā(śā)straṃ japamīśānako caret ।

ajite – (l.2)
diśāsudhyayanaṃ(diśāsvadhyayanaṃ) kuryāt catvārastu dvijottamaḥ ।
vidiśāsu jape(pet) stotrān cakvaramarcantu śaivakā ॥

raurave – (l.3)
caturdikṣu caturvedai(daiḥ) kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ ।
vyomaprāsādamantraṃ ca brahmāṅgaṃ ca tathaiva ca ॥
dikṣu te *brāhmamaiścaiva caturdikṣu japettataḥ ।* (l.4)

vīratantre –
kuryādadhyayanaṃ dikṣu japenmantra(ntraṃ) vidikṣu ca ।
ṛgvedaṃ diśi *pūrvāyāṃ yajurvedaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥* (l.5)
sāmamuttaradeśe tu paścime'tharvameva ca ।
prāsādamīśakoṇeṣu *aghoraṃ vahnigocare ॥* (p.134b, f.151a, l.1)
nairityāṃ vahnibījaṃ tu umābījaṃ tu vāyave ।
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ homaṃ *kuryādviśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.2)

dīpte –
uktasthāneṣu sarvāṃśca brāhmaṇairjapamācaret ।
viparītakriyā tāścet tatkṛtyaṃ *niṣphalaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.3)
॥ vātule pratiṣṭhādīpikāyāṃ homavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥

(dakṣiṇā -)
kriyāśarīrai(mi)tyuktaṃ *dakṣiṇo jīva ucyate ।* (l.4)
kriyā dakṣiṇayā hīnā nirjīvā niṣphalaṃ bhavet ॥
kriyāsaphalatāmicche dakṣiṇāṃ *dāpayettataḥ ।* (l.5)
ārtho'pi dakṣiṇāṃ datvā kṣubhraṃ hastaṃ darirbhuvām ॥
saupyannye sāmakaṃ caiva yatho phalamavāpnuyāt । (p.135a, l.1)
athavā pañcaniṣkādyā ācāryāya tu dakṣiṇaḥ ॥
triguṇā madhyamā proktā ṣaḍguṇā cottamaṃ bhavet ।
tasmāddaśāṃśa*hotrāṇāṃ madhyā guṇāṃ tadartha(rdha)kaḥ ॥* (l.2)
japānāṃ hotṛvaktasya daivajña triguṇā bhavet ।
sakalasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ *saṃprakīrtitā ॥* (l.3)
niṣkalasya pratiṣṭhāṃ tu tatasmā triguṇo bhavet ।

ajite –
ācāryaṃ pūjayetpūrvaṃ vastrā*ṅgulīyakādibhiḥ ।* (l.4)
pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇā dadyāt ācāryasya viśeṣataḥ ॥
adhamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ ca madhyamaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavet ।
uttamaṃ triguṇaṃ *proktamācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥* (l.5)
daśabhāgaikabhāge tu mūrtipānāṃ tu dakṣiṇā ।
stotrakādhyayanādīnāṃ tadardhya(rdha)*dakṣiṇā dadet ॥* (p.135b, f.152a, l.1)
gobhūhiraṇyavastrādyaiḥ hastyaśvaśayanāsanaiḥ ।
dāsīdāsagrahārāmairativistīrṇalakṣaṇam ॥ (l.2)
dakṣiṇāṃ gurave paścāt sādhakaṃ sādimūrtipaḥ ।
yadi vā putrakāsteṣāṃ pādaṃ pādārdhameva hi ॥
deśikā dakṣiṇā *proktā daivajñādyai trajāpinām ।* (l.3)
pādaṃ cāpi tadardhaṃ ca tadardhaṃ ca kramā bhavet ॥

ajite - 
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ snānaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ । (l.4)
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ soṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyakam ॥
pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇairyuktaṃ vastrāṅgulīyabhūṣitam । (l.5)
haimakairyaṅgulīsūtrai(traiḥ) paṅkajaistakaḥ ॥
adhamaṃ viṃśatiniṣkaṃ dviguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ।
uttamaṃ triguṇaṃ *proktam ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥* (p.136a, l.1)
piṇḍikāsthāpakasyārdham aṣṭāṃśairmūrtipaisya tu ।
dviguṇaṃ vāstuhomaṃ syāt *tadardhaṃ madhyamasya tu ॥* (l.2)
devajña tathaivasya tasyārdhaṃ cāstrajā vinā ।
bhaktānāṃ paricārāṇāṃ yathāśraddhānudakṣiṇā ॥ (l.3)

dīpte –
ācāryadakṣiṇai datvā yathā śāstroktamārgataḥ ।
navavastreṇa saṃyuktaṃ soṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyakam ॥
pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇaṃ *dadyāt śikhe karṇe gale tathā ।* (l.4)
hastābhyā pādayoścaiva ete pañcāṅgamucyate ॥
adhamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ syāt madhyamaṃ dviguṇaṃ *bhavet ।* (l.5)
uttamaṃ triguṇaṃ proktam ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥
tadardhamupadeṣṭā ca japahomaṃ tadardhakam ।
mūrtipānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ *tadardhaṃ dakṣiṇā dadet ॥* (p.136b, f.153a, niṣkalakṣaṇam, l.1)
daivajñaṃ dviguṇaṃ tasya dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettataḥ ।
adhye ca mantratalpānāṃ mūrtipānāṃ tu dakṣiṇā ॥
pariṣṭi?*parivārāṇāṃ yathāśaktistu dakṣiṇā ।* (l.2)
dīkṣitā śivayogīṃ ca māyāmaṃ? caiva pūjayet ॥

vīratantre –
vrīhyāścatuṣṭayaṃ *guñjaṃ dviguñjaṃ māṣasaṃjñakam ।* (l.3)
pañcamāṣaṃ tu pādaṃ syāt catuṣpādaṃ niṣkasaṃjñakam ॥
niṣkadvādaśavijñeyaṃ phalaṃ teṣā(ṣāṃ) *śataṃ phalam ।* (l.4)

āyurvede –
deśikaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ।
dakṣiṇā dāpayettasya vittaśāṭhyā *vivarjitam ॥* (l.5)
uttamaṃ śataniṣkaṃ tu daśaniṣkaṃ tu madhyamam ।
bhūtaniṣkaṃ tu vijñeya dakṣiṇā deśikasya tu ॥
vasubhāgaikabhāgaṃ tu *mūrtipānāṃ tu dakṣiṇā ।* (p.137a, l.1)
...(sa)daiva kupyata daive hattadardhatṛṇān tadardhakam ॥
japānāṃ tu tadardhaṃ syādbhaktāśca paricārakān । (l.2)
dīnā nādha(tha)śca kṛpaṇānyadhā(thā) śraddhā tu dakṣiṇā ॥

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ । (l.3)
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayet paścāt navaniṣkā tu co'dhamaḥ ॥
madhyamaṃ rājaniṣkā tu naiṣkā tu cottamaḥ kathye ।
trimūrtidhārāṃśca *bhaktaśca paricārakān ॥* (l.4) ?
pratyekaṃ dāpayenniṣkaṃ devayajñastadeva hi ।

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
ācāryaṃ pūjayeddevāṃ *vastrāṅgulīyakādibhiḥ ।* (l.5)
hemapuṣpopavītaṃ ca kuṇḍalaṃ kaṭakaṃ tathā ॥
dāsī dāsaśca gaṇṭhāmimā(māmā)cāryāya pradāpayet ।
kanyā(nya)sā*dabhiniṣkaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ tathā ॥* (p.137b, f.154a, l.1)
dakṣiṇā kanyasādīni deśika...vidhīyate ।
daivajña vāstuhotāra(raṃ) mūrtipaṃ triguṇena ca ॥ (l.2)
mūrtipasya tribhāgaikaṃ madhye traṃśca ? pṛthak pṛthak ।
teṣāṃ hemāṅgulīyaṃ ca vastrayugmaṃ ca dāpayet ।
bhaktānāṃ paricārāṇāṃ *dāpayitvā tu śaktitaḥ ॥* (l.3)
dīnanārakṛpāṇāṃ tu annapānādi dāpayet ।
ācāryaṃ dāpayettatra gobhūmikāñcanādibhiḥ ॥ (l.4)
mūrtipān pūjayettatra daivajñaṃ pūjayettataḥ ।

raurave –
ācāryaṃ pūjayetpaścāt vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ।
upa*yuktamiha bhravya? snapanaṃ yāgamaṇḍape ॥* (l.5)
ācāryāya pradātavyaṃ nānyabhogāya kalpayet ।
yāgaṃ viśiṣṭayaddravyaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ maṇḍapasya tu ॥ (p.138a, l.1)
sthaṇḍilaṃ ca svarṇaṃ kumbhaṃ vai kalaśādikam ।
toraṇasṛksṛvāvaṣṭamaṅgalaṃ navaratnakam ॥
taddhomacarunaivedyaṃ viśeṣaṃ *maṇḍapasya tu ।* (l.2)
nālikeraphalādyaṃ ca yadanyadupayogikam ॥
ācāryāya pradeyaṃ syāt pañcagocarapatite ।
niṣkādidaśaniṣkāntaṃ *deśikasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥* (l.3)
niṣkāṣṭamāṃśahāni(niḥ) syā(syāt) aṣṭamāṃśaṃ tadardhakam ।
navadhā dakṣiṇā kṣubhraṃ kriyāyāṃ hīnavittake ॥ (l.4)

kāmike -
ācāryāddakṣiṇe hīne tadyāga(gaṃ) niṣphalaṃ bhavet ।
mūrtihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ....naiva śataṃ japet ॥
prārthayeddeśikaṃ sadya*dviguṇaṃ dāpayettataḥ ।* (l.5)
yāgopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ mā(ā)dada(dadet) deśikasya tu ॥
putrayāgaphalaṃ sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavati dhṛvam ।
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā *havyaṃ tasya pradāpayet ॥* (p.138b, f.155a, second half of the folio broken, l.1)
mūrtihomaṃ diśāhoma(maṃ) śāntihoma..... ॥ (l.2)
anuktaṃ yadbhavet kiñcit śāntihomena śāmyati ।
ācāryaṃ.......................................... ॥
............*....dakṣiṇā jīva ucyate ।* (l.3)
kriyā pūjanayā hīne tajjīvuṃ ni...... ॥ (l.4)

ācāryadakṣiṇā hīne sthānāśaṃ dhanakṣayam ।
................................................... ॥ (l.5)

sarvābharaṇasaṃ................................... ॥ (p.139a, l.1)
................................sarva pradarśayet ।
ācāryadakṣiṇāścaiva dāpayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥
............................................ ।
.........*niṣkaṃ tu utsavaṃ tu dviniṣkakam ॥* (l.2)

..................................................... ॥
.....(kṛ)tvā *tu dviguṇaṃ paścāt deśikaṃ tatra yojayet ।* (l.4)

kiraṇe – 
....................................... । (l.5)

triguṇaṃ cottamaṃ ................................. ।
........................*pañcāṅgaṃ bhūṣaṇaiḥ sahā(ha) ॥* (p.139b, f.156a, l.1)
adhye tatra mupāgai(upāṅgaiśca) pūjayet pṛthak pṛthak ।

kiraṇe -
ācāryaṃ pūjayetpaścāt *vastrābharaṇakuṇḍalaiḥ ।* (l.2)
svarṇayajñopavītaṃ ca taptahemāṅgulīyakam ॥
śayyāvāhanadāsaṃ ca aratnāni vividhāni ca । (l.3)
supuṣpāṃ vasudhāṃ caiva savatsāṅgī tathaiva ca ॥
etāni gurave dadyāt pañcāsvamapi? yojayet ।
adhamaṃ pañca*niṣkaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.4)
triguṇamuttamaṃ proktam ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā ।

dīpte –
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ । (l.5)
kanyasā daśaniṣkaṃ syāt dviguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ॥
triguṇaṃ cottamaṃ proktaṃ deśikasya tu dakṣiṇā ।
evaṃ vai *dāpayettasya pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇaiḥ saha ॥* (p.140a, l.1)
adhyetṛśatipāśca pūjayetpūrvavadbudhaḥ । (l.2)

kāraṇe –
aṅkurārpaṇakāścaiva dhvajārohaṇakālake ।
yāgādhivāsane home prārambhe vasiyodviṣayoḥ? ॥
athavā *ekasavyāpaprārambhe vāvasānake ।* (l.3)
nṛttamūrtyotsave tīrthaśuddhasnapanakāla(lake) ।
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpat(payet) paścāt trividhā ca sapatma(dma)kāḥ ॥ (l.4)
athavā pañcaniṣkasya dviguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ।
triguṇāya varā jñeyā deśikasya tu dakṣiṇā ॥
tadardhasaṅkhyayā proktā dakṣiṇā *kanyasaṃ kramāt ।* (l.5)
śreṣṭhaṃ tadviguṇaṃ proktaṃ tvanyathā vinigadyate ॥
niṣkaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā pratyahaṃ dakṣiṇā mataḥ ।
kāmike – (p.140b, f.157a, l.1)
ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ।
svarṇaṃ ca śā...vastraṃ dhānyopakaraṇāni ca ॥
deśikāya pradātavyaṃ bhaktāśca *paricārakān ।* (l.2)
dīnāṃ nānāyāṃ tatkāle pūjayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥
॥ iti pratiṣṭhādīpikāyām ācāryadakṣiṇāvidhipaṭalaḥ ॥ (l.3)

sthāpanam -
evaṃ jāgaraṇaṃ rātrau prabhāte sumuhūrtake ।
śayanāddevamuddhṛtya sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
harmya(rmyaṃ) pradakṣiṇīkṛtya garbhagehaṃ *samāviśet ।* (l.4)
sthāpayenmantramūlena tattadaṅgāni vinyaset ॥
ghaṭādbījaṃ hṛdi nyasya tattadbhirabhiṣecayet ।
parivāraghaṭāṃścaiva tattatpīṭhe*'bhiṣecayet ॥* (l.5)
brahmāṅgairabhiṣicyāthā(tha) hṛdā gandhādibhiryajet ।
haviṣā taṃ samā(ma)bhyarcya saptamāṃ sahṛddeya(yā)t ॥
parivārabaliṃ caiva *tadardheṇaiva dāpayet ।* (p.141a, l.1)
pratiṣṭhāvāhanaṃ hyeva utsavaṃ cādhunocyate ॥

raurave –
muhūrte liṅgamuddhṛtyā(tya) garbhagehaṃ samāviśet । (l.2)
ācāryaṃ suprasannātmā mūlamantramudāharan ॥
liṅgaṃ sadāśive cintye sthāpaye dvārādimukham ।
adhiṣṭhānasamāptau tu purato dvāra*bandhataḥ ॥* (l.3)
mūle'ṣṭakāyāṃ pūrve vā liṅgasthāpanamārabhet ।
nandyāvartaśilāṃ yodhya(jya) paritaḥ śaṅkarādiṣu ॥
kalkenaivāṣṭa*bandhenā(na) bandhaye(yet) sudṛḍhaṃ yathā ।* (l.4)
manonmanyāstu mantreṇa yojayet piṇḍikāṃ yathā ॥
śilālābhe'ṣṭakāgarbhaṃ sudhāpiṇḍikāṃ kuru । (l.5)
sthalaṃ ca samamatā nītvā tataḥ śilpivisarjanam ॥
puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā gavyābhiṣecanaṃ kuru ।
gandhodakena saṃsnāpya *puṣpa(ṣpaṃ) mūrdhni vinyaset ॥* (p.141b, f.158a, pūrṇāhuti(tiḥ), l.1)
पञ्चासनं च संकल्प्य समभ्यर्च्य यथाविधिः ।
पश्चादग्नि(ग्निं) समभ्यर्च्य कलशान् स्थापिता(तान्) *न्यसेत् ॥* <<(l.2)
jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛścaiva homayet ।
sviṣṭamagneti mūle pūrṇāhutiṃ samācaret ॥ (l.3)
kumbhānuddhṛtya śirasā bāhyadhāmapradakṣiṇam ।
kṛtvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ liṅgāgre sthāpayet *kramāt ॥* (l.4)
ṣaḍadhvā saṃnyase(set) liṅge sūkṣmadehasya gehakam ।
kumbhāddevaṃ samādāya mūlaliṅgasya madhyame ॥ (l.5)
hṛdā mūlaṃ samuccārya dhyānayuktasamanvitam ।
yojayet sūkṣmadehe tu mayūrārdhe mayūravat ॥
manonmanī ca vardhanyāṃ *piṇḍikāyāṃ ca yojayet ।* (p.142a, l.1)
anantādiśikhaṇḍyānta(ntaṃ) yojayāvratakaṃ nyaset ॥
tatkumbhasthodakai(kaiḥ/kaṃ) snāpya arcanoktaṃ *samarcayet ।* (l.2)
parivāraghaṭādbījaṃ parito viṣṭare nyaset ॥
haviṣaṃ dāpayetpaścāt devān udvāsayet *kramāt ।* (l.3)
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyā(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।
sumuhūrte sulagne tu mantranyāsaṃ ca kārayet ॥
bālāṃ ce? *snānavedyāṃ tu mantranyāsaṃ samācaret ।* (l.4)
devyāgre tu ghaṭānya(nnya)sya kumbhān(bhāt)bījaṃ tu taddhṛdi ॥
vinyaset *anukumbhebhyaḥ pīṭhasyoparito marut ।* (l.5)
tattvamūrtyādivinyāsaṃ pūrvavat kārayettataḥ ॥
tattatkumbhodakaiścaiva *tattaddeśe'bhiṣecayet ।* (p.142b, f.159a, l.1)
snapanaṃ kārayedante naivedyaṃ cotsavaṃ kuru ॥
kṛtvānekavidhaistotrairamuṃ *ślokaṃ paṭhettataḥ ।* (l.2)
yāvaśca(cca)ndrasūryaśca tāvattiṣṭhati medinīm ॥
tāvaśru(cchru)tvā devī sthātavyamahi sannidhau । (l.3)
saṃpūjya deśikādīścā(dīṃśca) dakṣiṇā dāpayettataḥ ॥
sumuhūrte sulagne ca mantranyāsaṃ samācaret ।
calaṃ ce(cet) *snānavedyāṃ tu mantranyāso japeddvijā(jāḥ) ॥* (l.4)
devāgre tu ghaṭā(ṭān) nyasya kumbho bījaṃ(kumbhādbījaṃ) tu taddhṛdi ।
vinyasedanyakumbhebhyaḥ pīṭhasya parito mantra.... ॥
tattatkumbhodakaiścaiva tattaddeśebhiṣecat(ṣecayet) ।
tattvamūrtyādivinyāsaṃ pūrvavat kārayettataḥ ॥ (p.143a, l.1)
snapanaṃ kārayedante naivedyaṃ cotsavaṃ nyaset ।

kāmike –
prabhāte snā(snānaṃ) kurvantu ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ saha । (l.2)
muhūrtānnāsikāpūrve sarva(rvā)todyasamākulam ॥
rātriśeṣaṃ huneddhīmān ṣoḍaśāhutiṃ(ti)saṅkhyayā ।
ghṛtamime ghṛtaṃ hutvā *devasyatvā gulaṃ hunet ॥* (l.3)
brahmajajñānamantreṇa śeṣadravyāṇi homayet ।
dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā pūrṇāti(huti)mathācaret ॥ (l.4)
jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca ra...rāṣṭrabhṛśca hunettataḥ ।
śayanāddematthāpya(ddevamutthāpya) sna(snā)navedyupari nyaset ॥
maṇḍapaṃ *bhūṣayeddhīmān snānārthaṃ saṅkalpitam ।* (l.5)
āsanaṃ śvabhramadhye tu kalpayetpadmasaṃjñakam ॥
tanmadhye sthāpayeddevaṃ *prāṅmukhaṃ hṛdayena tu ।* (p.143b, f.160a, l.1)
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ tvaṃ brahmāṅgaṃ tato nyaset ॥
pījayitvā hṛdā mantrī tattatsthāpanamārabhet । (l.2)
śivakumbhaṃ samādāya śivabījaṃ hṛdi nyaset ॥
gaurībījaṃ nyasetpīṭhe karṇadeśaṃ sāśra(śri)tam ।
vidyeśvarānyabhi*sthāpya kumbhatoyai(yaiḥ) svanāmataḥ ॥* (l.3)
snapanaṃ kārayedante pa(pū)rvoktavidhinā sahā(ha) ।
maṇḍape sthāpayeddevam *arcayitvā yathāvidhiḥ ।* (l.4)
naivedyaṃ dāpayeddhīmān tāmbūlaṃ ca nidāpayet ॥
utsavaṃ kārayetpaścāt yathāvittā*nusārataḥ ।* (l.5)
evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyāḥ sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ॥

dīpte –
sumuhūrte sulagne tu ropayet vedimadhyame । (p.144a, l.1)
śannodeveti mantreṇa śāyayet śayanopari ॥
kumbhasthāpanaṃ vakṣye kuryāt umāmaheśavat । (l.2)
prabhāte sumuhūrte tu sthāpanaṃ tu samārabhet ॥
sthāpako mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ vidhinā snānamācaret ।
aṅgoṅga*nityayitvā? tu pūjayedātmadaivatam ॥* (l.3)
uddhṛtyā śayanāṃ(nād) bimbaṃ vāsanādīn visarjayet ।
bimbaṃ saṃpūjayetpaścāt *vahnikuṇḍeṣu saṃyajet ॥* (l.4)
sthāpakādīni pūjyātha pūrvoktavidhinā sahā ।
jayādirabhyādānāśca rāṣṭramāṃśca hunet *kramāt ॥* (l.5)
ayaṃ someti mantreṇa hune(net) pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ ।
uddhṛtya śayanā(nāt) bimbaṃ snānākhyaṃ vinyaseddhṛtam ॥
pādādimūrdhi*paryantaṃ kuryādghṛtaśirorpaṇam ।* (p.144b, f.161a, l.1)
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ kārayedvidhinā saha ॥
kalaśādiku(kaṃ ta)toddhṛtya sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam । (l.2)
mandiraṃ tu paribhrāmya bimbāgre sthaṇḍilopari ॥
sthāpayeddhṛdayenaiva pradhānādikrameṇa tu ।
muhūrte *samanuprāpte guru(ra)ve sannivedayet ॥* (l.3)
prāṇapa(pra)tiṣṭhāyogaṃ tu jñātvā saṃsthānakaṃ kuru ।
ghaṭādbījaṃ samādāya *śūlyaṃśe hṛdi vinyaset ॥* (l.4)
tābhyāṃ saṃsthāpayeddevaṃ gandhapuṣpādinārcayet ।
lokeśaṃ bījamādāya pīṭhopari *pratanyaset? ॥* (l.5)
tattadghaṭodakenaiva tatta(ttat) sthāneṣu yojayet ।
snapanaṃ kārayedante pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyayā ॥ (p.145a, l.1)
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya prākṛtaṃ haviṣaṃ dadet ।
utsavaṃ kārayetpaścāt bhūtāhaṃ vā guṇāhakam ॥
a(ā)yudhasthāpanaṃ *hyevaṃ kārayeddeśikottamaḥ ।* (l.2)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
prabhāte sumuhūrte tu pūrṇāhutissumantrakam ।
ācāryaṃ pūjayet *sarvaṃ vastrahemāṅgulīyakam ॥* (l.3)
śayanādbimbamutthāpya snānavedyāmadhāpayet ।
ghṛtaṃ śirorpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śiva*kumbhaṃ tu sannidhau ॥* (l.4)
kalasthaṃ yathā devaṃ pratimāyāṃ tu yojayet ।
susnāpya kalaśairaiva gandhapuṣpādināvya(pya) vai ॥ (l.5)
prabhūtahaviṣaṃ dadyāt mūlamantreṇa deśikam(kaḥ) ।
prārambhavyotsavaṃ tatra bhṛtaṃ vittānusārataḥ ॥
tadutsavavidhau sarvamiheśāste *tu cohi(di)tam ।* (p.145b, f.162a, l.1)

suprabhede –
sumuhūrte samanuprāptena puraṃ na vilambitam ।
pradakṣiṇamathānīya prāsādadvāradeśikaḥ ॥ (l.2)
bimbamarghyādibhiḥ pūjya śanairgarbhaṃ praviśya ca ।
bhadrapīṭhe sthitaṃ kṛtvā paścādyantreṇa yantritaḥ ॥
bimbannai(mbādyaiḥ) *praguṇī?kṛtya sthāpayenmūlamuccaran ।* (l.3)
maṇḍalaṃ daivamāvāhyābiṃkṣisnai?kyaṃ nayet punaḥ ॥
sthāpanaṃ *sannidhānaṃ ca sannirodhanaṃ tathaiva ca ।* (l.4)
sarvāṃśca hṛdayamantreṇa kṛtvā mudrā pradarśayet ॥

kāraṇe –
pādyaṃ dadyāttu sadyena *vāmenā(na) candanaṃ dadeta ।* (l.5)
aghoreṇa dadairghyaṃ? ca gandhādyamitareṇa tu ॥
śāṅkareṇa dadet puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ varmeṇa dāpayet । (p.146a, l.1)
netramantreṇa nardīpam ārātriṃ śikhayā dadet ॥

bhīsaṃhitāyām –
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca *dhūpakam ।* (l.2)
dīpaṃ naivedyatāmbūlaṃ mukhavāsaṃ ca darpaṇam ॥
tattadbrahmaṣaḍaṅgaiśca dāpayettu krameṇa tu ।
pādyamācamanaṃ *cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam ॥* (l.3)
dīpaṃ naivedyatāmbūlaṃ pāyasaṃ bhakṣaṇānvitam ।
vastramābharaṃ(raṇaṃ) chatramañjaraṃ(naṃ) *cāmaraṃ tathā ॥* (l.4)
yuktāyuktaṃ tu yatkiñcit sarvaṃ vā hṛdayāṇunā ।
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam ॥
dīpaṃ *naivedyatāmbūlaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ vastrameva ca ।* (l.5)
añjanaṃ cāmaraṃ chatraṃ darpaṇaṃ padakaṃ tathā ॥
hṛdayena pradātavyam astraṃ cāditimantrataḥ । (p.146b, f.163a, prāyaścittam, l.1)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
pādyaṃ sadyena datvā tu vāmenācamanīyakam ।
aghoreṇārghyaṃ datvā tu astreṇaivābhiṣecayet ॥ (l.2)
gandhamīśānamantreṇa puṣpaṃ vai hṛdayena tu ।
dhūpaṃ tu śirasā datvā dīpaṃ netreṇa dāpayet ॥
naivedyaṃ *hṛdayenaiva namaskārāstramantrataḥ ।* (l.3)

raurave –
pūjayedāvṛdīṃ(tīṃ) paścāt dhūpadīpāvasānake ।
indrādīśca *tadastrāṇi tadbāhye samyagācaret ॥* (l.4)
ekena vā yaye(je)ttābhyāṃ tribhirvā caraṇairyutam ।
॥ kāmike – ? iti pratiṣṭhādīpikāyāṃ sthāpanavidhi ekādaśapaṭalaḥ? ॥

॥ prāyaścittam ॥
pratiṣṭhākālahīne tu mūlenaiva *śatāhutiḥ ।* (p.147a, l.1)
kriyāhīne tathā śāstraṃ mūlenaiva śatāhutiḥ ॥
kriyāyāṃ mantrahīne tu śikhāyāṃ tu śatā(taṃ) hunet ।
dravya*hīne tu vighneśaṃ hasasrāhutiḥ varmaṇā ॥* (l.2)
kriyātiriktaṃ sarvāṃ tu nyūne mūlaṃ śataṃ japet । - iti suprabhede ॥ (l.3)

(utsavam -)
pratiṣṭhāvāhanaṃ hyemutsavaṃ cādhunocyate ।
siṃhasya utsavaṃ kṛtvā garuḍaṃ vā vilekhayet ॥
pañcāyudha*samāyuktaṃ cakramūrtisamanvitam ।* (l.4)
sandhidevān samāhūya sarvaṃ ku(kā)ryaṃ mahotsavam ।
śūlaṃ kapālaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ *cāpaṃ tathaiva ca ॥* (l.5)
mohinyāyudhapañcaite pañcamūrtādhidaivatam ।
yāge tu vedikāyāṃ tu sthāye mohanī*vardhanam ॥* (p.147b, f.164a, l.1)
ahanyādīnavardhanyā paritasthāpayedbudhaḥ ।
tattanmantraṃ nyaseddehe haviṣāntaṃ samarcayet ॥
tasyai*ndrāgnīṃ tu saṅkalpya padmakuṇḍe tu moha(homa)yet ।* (l.2)
samidhājyaṃ caruṃ lājān sarṣapāstilamudgakān ॥
bastodhi?*daraṃ ca māṃsaṃ ca apūpaṃ ca kulastakam ।* (l.3)
sadyādyastrakamantreṇa mohinyāṃ tu śatāhuti(tiḥ) ॥
pañcāśat pañcaviṃśatyā pratidravyaṃ tu *moha(homa)yet ।* (l.4)
momā(homā)nte tu baliṃ dadyāt sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
yo(vo)riṣīṇi(ṇī)ti mardī ca pālinī piṅgalī tathā ।
māṣamī?*vikṛtimādadī brahmaṇī sañcidevatā? ॥* (l.5)
madhyā....śāvasāne tu baliṃ dadyāttu sandhiṣu ।
śuddhānnaṃ māsa(ṣa)saṃyuktaṃ *baliṃ dadyāt svamantrataḥ ॥* (p.148a, l.1)
navāhaṃ vātha saptāhaṃ pañcāhaṃ vā samācaret ।
baliṃ tejotsavaṃ kuryāt devī mohinī kṛto? । (l.2)
yānaṃ ca pariveṣayuktyā śeṣaṃ samācaret ॥
evaṃ dvitridinaṃ vāpi vanayātrā samācaret ।
anyatsarvaṃ ca kartavyamutsavasya mayoktavat ॥ (l.3)

raurave –
utsavaṃ kārayet paścāt paścādekāhaṃ tu vā ।
saptāhaṃ vātha kurvīta pūrvoktavidhinā saha ॥

dīpte – (l.4)
navāhe vātha saptāhe pañcāhe tridine'thavā ।
ekāhe vātha kartavyamutsavaṃ vidhipūrvakam ॥
navāhe vātha saptāhe *dhvajārohaṇamārabhet ।* (l.5)
spatahastadhvajapaṭe mayūraṃ vā gajaṃ tu vā ॥
lekhayeccitramārgeṇa kramukaṃ veṇukaṃ tathā । (p.148b, f.165a, l.1)
prā(prāsā)dasya samaṃ dīrghaṃ sthāpayennīlayāgrake ।
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vātha sthāpayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥
bheryo.....tataḥ *kuryāt utsavaṃ punarārabhet ।* (l.2)
yogeśasthāpanaṃ kuryāt samantreṇaiva homayet ॥
sāyaṃ prāta(taḥ) baliṃ datvā ante *cotsavamārabhet ।* (l.3)
śa(ca)krananni(ndi)kumārāntaṃ saptarātrādhi........... ॥
gajāni śaktisaṃyuktaṃ navarātre prajāpate । (l.4)
kramādhīnāstu śeṣā heti vyāntaṃ tu pūrvake ॥

cūrṇotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tīrthakarmaṃ *samācaret ॥* (l.5)

bhīmasaṃhitāyām –
utsavaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ prāgupta(kta)vidhinā budhaḥ ।

॥ iti sakalāgamasaṅgrahe pratiṣṭhotsavavidhidvā(rdvā)daśapaṭalaḥ ॥

(dakṣiṇā -)

	dakṣiṇā triguṇaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ cottamaṃ bhavet । tasmāddaśāṃśahotrāṇā(ṇāṃ) madhyāṃ(dhyā) triguṇaṃ tadardhataḥ । (l.2) japānā hotṛva(bha)ktasya vai jutipurā? bhavet । sakalasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ saṃprakīrtitaḥ । (c.1) bhayi(bhai)ravapratiṣṭhā । ravipratiṣṭhā । nāgapratiṣṭhā । (c.2) bhaktapratiṣṭhā । vimānapratiṣṭhā । maṇḍapapratiṣṭhā । (c.3) āsthānamaṇḍapam । rathārohaṇam । vighneśapratiṣṭhā । gaurīkavacam । (c.4) paṭumaṇḍapam । aṣṭabandhanam । kālīpratiṣṭhā । gaṅga(gā)stuti । (c.5) kālīpratiṣṭhā । (rep) savyamūrtipratiṣṭhā । jīrṇoddhāraṇam । śivapratiṣṭhā । (p.160a blank, p.160b, f.176a, nityapūjai(jā), l.1)

atha śivapūjāvidhirucyate –
brāhme muhūrte samutthāya । śucirvyāpya । śucirvā yathāsaṃbhavaśaucaṃ kṛtvā । (l.2) uttarābhimukho bhūtvā । upaviśyā(śya) । lalāṭe bhasma dhṛtvā । dvādaśānte śivaṃ dhyātvā । manasi śivamantraṃ japan (l.3) dhyāya śivagapuri? kīrtayet । gṛhānnirgatya । grāmān prācyāmudīcyām anyatra vā gṛhe । maitrapade vā (l.4) śiro veṣṭanam । dakṣakarṇasthānita-yajñasūtri nirjanadeśe karṇakavarjite divodaṅmukhaḥ rātrau dakṣiṇamukhāḥ (l.5) ardharātrau nairi(ṛ)tyabhimukhaḥ । bhītyādiyuktaścedyāthāmukho vā upaviśya । bhūmisthān visṛjet । 

devatā*ri(ṛ)ṣayaścaiva gaṇanāgāstathaiva ca ।* (p.161a, l.1)
uttiṣṭha sarvataścaiva bahirbhūmiṃ karomyaham ॥

	iti manasā śrāvya । śucisthāne (l.2) manojñe ayājñāṅgairanādraistṛṇairmamimāścādyā tatra vācayan । viṇmūtrādīn visṛjya । tṛṇaloṣṭādīnāmṛtkhaṇḍena (l.3) pakvaṃ puna(naḥ) gudaṃ pramṛjya । ūrdhvāṃśaṃ vyapohya । adho bhāge mṛdaṃ dakṣahastena saṃgṛhya । śucisthāne nidhāya । (l.4) tṛṇādikaṃ saṃśodhya । ādīkṛtya āmalakīphalamātraṃ vāmakare nikṣipya savyena jalaṃ nikṣipya liṅge mūtraśaucaṃ (l.5) kṛtvā savyaṃ davidhā prakṣālayet । pañcāpāne । vāmakare daśa । ubhayoruhastayoḥ saptavāraṃ mṛttikayā sajalena kṣālayet । (p.161b, l.1) yadvā āmaṇibandhapramāṇe jalamadhye mṛdamādāyā(ya) śaucaṃ kuryāt । yāvanmātraṃ manaḥ śuciḥ tāvat śaucaṃ vidhāya । (l.2) śocaśeṣamṛttoyādīn niśśeṣaṃ saṃkṣālya । anyadeśaṃ praviśyā(śya) । ājale āmaṇibandhāt vāmahastena (l.3) dakṣiṇapādaṃ pāṇi mṛttoyaiśca vāmapādaṃ vāmapāṇinā saṃkṣālya dvādaśagaṇḍūṣaṃ vāmabhāge bhūtkṛtya? (l.4) paścāddantadhāvanaṃ kurvīta । pratipat parvaṃ ṣaṣṭya..... bhānuvāre saṃkrāntau ekādaśyāṃ janmarkṣe ca na kartavyam । (l.5) anyadine kartavyam । tadyathā । 

āyurbalaṃ yaśo varcaḥ prajāṃ puśu vasūni ca । 
brahmaṃ mānaṃ ca medhāṃ ca tvanne dehi vanaspate ॥

	iti dantakāṣṭha(p.162a, l.1)māmantrya jambvādikāṣṭhaārdraṃ cvacam aparvaṃ kanikānāhaṃ dvādaśāṅgulaṃ saṃgṛhya । prakṣālya agreṇa pūrvābhimukhopaviśya (l.2) ūrdhvapaṅktimārabhya prādakṣiṇyena dantān antarbahiḥ śuddhiṃ kṛtvā vajyepalālepanapallavena vā dantaśuddhiṃ kṛtvā iṣṭikā(l.3)hastapādukairvastreṇāṅgulīyairna kāryaṃ paścāt dvādaśagaṇḍūṣaṃ kārayet ।

ācamanavidhirucyate – (l.4)
vāmapādaṃ sthale dakṣiṇapādaṃ jale kṛtvā utkuṭikāsanaḥ pūrvābhimukhaḥ pāṇiṃ gokarṇavat kṛtvā muktāṅguṣṭhaṃ (l.5) kaniṣṭhiko jānumadhyakaradvayo nirmalajale maṣamagnaṃ gṛhītvā । kṛ(u)ṣṇīṣaṃ trirācamya । paścāt praṇavenācamya aṅguṣṭha(p.162b, f.178a?, l.1)mūlena kapola(laṃ) dviḥ pramṛjya । aṅgulinā mūlena oṣṭhe dvi mārjya savyapāṇimabhyukṣya aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ savyavāma(l.2)krameṇa cakṣuṣoḥ śrotrayoḥ madhyamāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ bāhvoḥ caturbhiraṅgulibhiḥ aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa nābhiṃ (l.3) sarvāṅgulyagreṇa śiraḥ jalopetaṃ spṛśet । paścāt oṃ hāṃ śivatattvāya svādhā । oṃ hiṃ hṛdayaṃ vidyātattvāya svadhā । oṃ huṃ (l.4) ātmatattvāya svadhā । ityācamya । oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । iti pūrvava-daṅgāni spṛśet । ācamanavidhiḥ । prāṇānāyamya (l.5) saṃkalpya ।

atikrūramahākāya kalpāntadahanopamā ।

(snānavidhiḥ -)
	
	...marhasīti vijñāpya । nadyāṃ śivagaṅgādyanyatame tīrthe jale pañcavāraṃ (p.163a, l.1) mṛdamādāya । tīre visṛjya । āpo vā ityabhimantrya । gaṅgātīrthānyākṛṣya nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya । nadyāṃ prati śrotaso mukhaḥ । (l.2) taṭāke vā raverabhimukha snāyāt । taduktam - nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ śroto'bhimukhamāsthitaḥ । ...tuskarā-bhimukhasnāyāttaṭāke (l.3) vāmanusmaran । paścāddevari(ṛ)ṣipitṛtarpaṇaṃ snānāṅgaṃ kārayediti । mali?snānavidhiḥ । snānārthamācamya (l.4) astreṇa mṛdaṃ khātvā । ūrdhvāṃśaṃ vyapohya । hṛdā saṃgrāhya । hṛnmantreṇa khātamāpūrya śucisthāne (l.5) mṛdaṃ vinyasya । śirasā jalena sicya । tṛṇādikaṃ śikhayā saṃśodhya । kavacenā(na) tridhā kṛtvā । pūrvastha-mṛdamastreṇa (p.163b, f.179a, l.1) dakṣiṇasthaṃ brahmabhiḥ । uttarasthaṃ śivamantreṇa mantrya । astrajaptamṛdaṃ jale daśa-dikṣu nikṣipya mūlāllabdhaṃ jalamadhye (l.2) nikṣipya bhujabhramaṇaparyantaṃ śivatīrthaṃ smṛtvā । nārācamudrayā bindusthavahnināstreṇa bhujabhramaṇaparyantaṃ daṇḍaṃ (l.3) bhasmībhūtaṃ sañcintya nārācamudrayā binduprasṛtāmṛtenāpūrya gaṅgāditīrthāni vauṣaḍantahṛdākṛṣya (l.4) udbhavayā jale vinyasya । nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya brahmajaptamṛdaṃ grāhya । śiro.....hṛdguhyapādāntam । sarvāṅge(l.5)ṣvīśānādibhiḥ mṛddūrvāgomayaiścālipya nābhipramāṇajale sthitvā śivaṃ hṛdi dhyāyan । ṣaṇmukhīkaraṇena (p.164a, l.1) saṃnimajya saṃhitāmantrān trivāraṃ paṭhan paścāduttīryāñjalinā sūryasoma-svarūpahastābhyāṃ jalāni vauṣaḍanta(l.2)saṃhitāmantrai(ntraiḥ) śirasyabhiṣicya kavacenātmānaṃ pariṣecayediti । taduktam ācāryaiḥ mṛtsnayā dehaśuddhi(ddhiḥ) syāt (l.3) mantrajaptena vāriṇā । ātmaśuddhistu tenaiva dhyānena vai bhāvanā । iti snānavidhiḥ ॥

punastatvairācamya (l.4) sūryamaṇḍale astraṃ dhyātvā ।

caturbhujaṃ caturvaktraṃ pradīptāgnisamaprabham ।
varadaṃ ca tathā śaktimabhayaṃ śūlameva ca ॥ (l.5)
bibhrāṇamastrarājānaṃ dhyāyet sūryasya maṇḍale ।

	astreṇa trivāraṃ prokṣya astreṇa svāhāntenārghyatrayaṃ datvā śastreṇa (p.164b, f.180a, l.1) daśavāraṃ japitvā paścāt । śivaśastrāya vidmahe kalānalāya dhīmahi tanno śastrapracodayāditi visarjanārghyaṃ datvā । astraṃ hṛdi (l.2) dhyāyan tīre uttīrya prāñjaliṃ triprakāramātmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā namaskṛtya tīrthe vinyastamantrān hṛdi saṃyojya (l.3) tīre ūrdhvajñurdakṣiṇāmukha(śam) upaviśya । khaṇḍavastreṇa mūrdhnādisammārjya । ārdravasanaṃ vāmahastena ūrdhvāt saṃgṛhya (l.4) visṛjya śuddhavastramuttarīyaṃ ca dhṛtvā tyaktavastraṃ śucīkṛtya caturguṇīkṛtya apasavyopavītena pitṛtīrthena ni....yet । (l.5) ye ye cāsmatkule jātā putrāgotrajā mṛtāḥ te gṛhṇantu mayā dattaṃ vastraniṣpīḍanodaka-miti niṣpīḍyā(ḍya) (p.165a, l.1) ācamet । dhū(dhṛ)tvā sikatāṃśukaṃ bhasmalepanaṃ na hi yasya vaitajjanma bhastrikātulyaṃ tasmādyothoktamācamet । (l.2)

atha āgneyasnānavidhiḥ –
mūlena subhasmaṃ saṃgrāhya vāmahastatale nikṣipya aṅguṣṭhaanāmikābhyāṃ tilamānamasurāṃśam (l.3) astreṇa saṃtyajya nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ sakalīkṛtya । saṃhitābhirabhimantrya । vaiṣaḍantamūlenāmṛtīkṛtya madhyāṅgulyagreṇa (l.4) jalaṃ saṃsicya kavacena prādakṣiṇyena sammadya? (sammṛjya) īśānena samuddhīlya mukhaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa kaṇṭhamādinābhyanta(l.5)maghoreṇa nābhe jānvantaṃ vāmena jānunaḥ pādāntaṃ samāṅgeṣu sadyenoddhūlya śiro lalāṭahṛdā nābhijānvo (p.165b, f.181a, l.1) bāhukoparimaṇibandhapiṭharapṛṣṭhapārśve eṣu sthāneṣvīśānādimantreṇa tripuṇḍraṃ dhṛtvā tatkara.....lya(l.2) vauṣaḍamūlena śirasyabhiṣecayediti । 

bhasmadhāraṇavidhiḥ ॥
ācamya prāṇānāyamya prātaḥ ...........naṃ kariṣya (l.3) iti saṃkalpya jalayantraṃ trikoṇamadhye hrīṃkāraṃ koṇāgre praṇavaṃ nyaset । daṇḍeṣu vyāhṛdiścaiva praṇavena (l.4) ca veṣṭayet । tanmadhye nādamālikhya gāyatriyā darśayedbudhaḥ aṅkuśaṃ dhenumudrāṃ ca amṛtaṃ śukhameva ca । cakraṃ ca gāruḍaṃ caiva (l.5) gālinīṃ ca pradarśayet । āpo veti jalaṃ saṃspṛśya । āpohiṣṭheti mantrasya sindhūdvīpaṛṣiḥ gāyatrīcchandaḥ āpo devatā । (p.166a, l.1) āṃ bījaṃ paṃ śaktiḥ amṛtaṃ kīlakam । prokṣaṇārthe viniyogaḥ । āpohiṣṭheti navaprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā । sūryaścetya(l.2)nuvākasya agniṛṣiḥ gāyatrīcchandaḥ śrī sūyo(ryo) devatā । asmin manovākkāyakarmakṛtapāpapuruṣadahanārthamapāṃ (l.3) prāśane vini-yogaḥ । dadhikrāviṇṇeti mantrasya vāmadevaṛṣiḥ anuṣṭup chandaḥ । aśvasudharmadadhikrāpā devatā । (l.4) ātmani prokṣaṇam । asya pradā(dhā)namantrasya viśvāmitrari(ṛ)ṣiḥ ṇi? citragāyatrīśchandaḥ savitā devatā । ṇiṃ bījam । yaṃ śaktiḥ । (l.5) prāṇaṃ yāt kīlakam । śrībhagavatsamārādhanārthe mama bhogamokṣā tevarya?pradāne viniyogaḥ । oṃ tatsavitu brahmātmane (p.166b, l.1) hṛdayāya namaḥ । vareṇyaṃ viṣṇvātmane śirase svāhā । bhargodevasya rudrātmane śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । dhīmahi sarvārmane (l.2) kavacāya hum । dhiyoyonaḥ anantātmane netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । pracodayāt prakāśātmane astrāya phaṭ । bhūrbhuvasvaro(l.3)miti digbandhaḥ । 

dhyānam -
mūlādhāre hutavahakalāmiśrayantaṃ kalābhiḥ ।
brahmasthāne paramagagane tatsaviturvareṇyam ॥ (l.4)
bhargo devasya śaśikalāyutāṃ sīma...hityekarūpaṃ dhiyoyonaḥ paramamamṛtam ।
codayantaṃ purastāt । 
vedasāraṃ *paraṃ jotimūlabhūtaṃ parātparam ॥* (l.5)
hṛdi....(sthaṃ) sarvalokasthaṃ maṇḍalāntaṃ vyavasthitam । 
vajrāyudhaṃ mahendrasya sakṛtsandhānakāraṇam ॥ (p.167a, l.1)
cintayetparamātmānamāpaūrdhvaṃ vinikṣipet ।

	gāyatriyā trivāraṃ arghyaṃ datvā । prāyaścittārghyaṃ datvā । asāvādityo(l.2)brahmeti ātmānaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । ācamya । ādityādinavagrahāt keśavādidvādaśānāmaiśca tarpayitvācamya । (l.3) brahmāṅgaṇamiti ......(samarpya) । tīre śucisthāne bhūtaśuddhikṛdrucirāsanārthaṃ deśikaṃ kūrmāsanopari (l.4) pañcāsanaṃ saṃkalpya । prathamaṃ darbhāsanam । dvitīyaṃ kūrmāsanam । tṛtīyaṃ carmajam । caturthaṃ vyāghracarma । pañcamaṃ ratnakambalaṃ vinyasya । sarvapūjārhakamityabhidhīyate । 

taduktaṃ śrīmadupabhedakāraṇe –
āsanam -
darbhāsanaṃ tu prathamaṃ dvitīyaṃ kacchapā*sanam ।* (p.167b, f.183a, l.1)
tṛtīyaṃ carmajaṃ vidyāt caturthaṃ vyāghracarma ca ॥
ratnakambalavastraṃ tu pañcamaṃ parikīrtitam ।
caturaśrā*yatāśraṃ ca saubhāgyaṃ dārukāsanam ॥* (l.2)
darbhāsanena sānnidhyaṃ kūrmāsanaṃ ripukṣayam ।
vastrāsane vyādhināśakaṃ *........duḥkhamocanam ॥* (l.3)
kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhirmukti(ktiḥ) syā(syād)vyāghracarmaṇi ।
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā rūcirā*sanasaṃsthitaḥ ॥* (l.4)
dvayorjānvo(nvoḥ) ca kṛtvā tu pādāgre caiva saṃpuṭam ।
īṣannāsāgramuṣṭistu dehadīrghaṃ ca tatra vai ॥ (l.5)
rucirāsanamākhyātaṃ sarvapūjārhakaṃ viduḥ ।

ṣaḍādhāraṃ saṃkalpya -
ṣaṭśatāni gaṇeśasya ṣaṭsahasraṃ *prajāpateḥ ।* (p.168a, l.1)
ṣaṭsahasraṃ gadāpāṇe ṣaṭsahasraṃ pinākine ॥
ātmanastu sahasraṃ syāt paramātmā sahasrakam ।
sahasraṃ tu *guro(roḥ)nityamityevaṃ ajapākramāt ॥* (l.2)

ajapāgāyatrī ॥
mūlādhāramaṇḍape aruṇaprabhe caturdalapadme sthitāya (l.3) ....gaṇādhipataye ajapā nāma gāyatrī saṅkhyayā vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ saṃ(sa)sso'haṃ paramātmane aharniśaṃ (l.4) nivedayāmi । āsīnamaṇḍape anekavidyutprabhe । ṣa(ṣaḍ)dalapadme sthitāya । śrī brahmaṇe ajapānāṃ gāyatrīṃ sahasraṣaṭsaṅkhyayā (l.5) baṃ bhaṃ maṃ haṃsassohaṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ paramātmane aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi । maṇipūrakamaṇḍape । anekavidyutprabhe । (p.168b, f.184a, l.1) kṣīravarṇaprabhe ca daśadalapadme sthitāya śrī mahāviṣṇave ajapānāṅgāya triṣaṭsahasrasaṅkhyābaṃ hamsasso'haṃ ḍhaṃ (l.2) hamsasso'haṃ paṃ hamsasso'haṃ phaṃ staṃ(ha)msasso'haṃ paramātmane aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi । anāhatamaṇḍape । marataka(gada)prabhe dvādaśa(l.3)dalapadme sthitāya śrī mahārudrāya ajapānāṃ gāyatrī ṣaṭsahasrasaṅkhyājapaṃ(pa)kam । hamsasso'ham । khaṃ hamsasso'haṃ (l.4) gaṃ hamsasso'ham । ghaṃ hamsasso'ham । ṅaṃ hamsasso'ham । caṃ hamsasso'ham । chaṃ hamsasso'ham । jaṃ hamsasso'ham । (l.5) jhaṃ hamsasso'ham । ñaṃ hamsasso'ham । ṭaṃ hamsasso'ham । ṭhaṃ hamsa-sso'gam । paramātmane । aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi । viśuddhimaṇḍape । (p.169a, l.1) sindūraprabhe । ṣauḍaśadalapadme sthitāya śrī maheśvarāya ajapānāṅgāya trisahasrasaṅkhyājapam aṃ hamsasso'ham । āṃ (l.2) hamsasso'ham । iṃ hamsasso'ham । īṃ hamsasso'ham । uṃ hamsasso'ham । ūṃ hamsasso'ham । ṛṃ hamsasso'ham । ṝṃ hamsa(l.3)sso'ham । ḷm hamsasso'ham । ḹm hamsasso'ham । eṃ hamsasso'ham । aiṃ hamsasso'gam । oṃ hamsasso'gam । auṃ hamsasso'ham । aṃ (l.4) hamsasso'ham । aḥ hamsasso'ham । paramātmane aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi । 

ājñāmaṇḍape । sphaṭikaprabhe । dvidala(l.5)padmasthitāya । sadāśivamūrtaye ajapānāṅgāya trisahasrasaṅkhyājapaṃ laṃ hamsasso'gam । kṣaṃ hamsasso'haṃ paramātmane (p.169b, f.185a, l.1) aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi । binduvyāpine sahasradalapadme sthitāya gurave nityamevam ajapānāmagāyatrī sahasrasaṅkhyājapaṃ (l.2) praṇavātmane hamsasso'haṃ paramātmane aharniśaṃ nivedayāmi ityevamajapāgāyatrī japet । hamsohaṃ (l.3) hamsāya vidmahe so'hamsasso'haṃsa-so'haṃsadhīmahī hamsasso'haṃ pracodayāt । astamanasūryodayamārabhya adyadina(l.4)sūryodayaparyantam ahorātrocchvāsaniśvāsarūpavyāpārasaṅkhyayā ṣaṭśatādhikam ekaviṃśatisahasram ajapānāmagāya (l.5) trijapaṃ mūlā-dhārādisaptamaṇḍapavāsinībhyovighneśvarādidevatābhyo yathāyogaṃ vibhajya samarpayiṣye । ajapāgāyatrī(p.170a, l.1)mantrasya haṃsari(ṛ)ṣiḥ ....... gāyatrīśchandaḥ । paramahiṃ(haṃ)so devatā । haṃ bījaṃ saḥ śaktiḥ so'haṃ kīlakam । paramahaṃsaprītyarthe (l.2) jape viniyogaḥ । haṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । haṃ sīṃ śirase svāhā । haṃ hiṃ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । haṃ saiṃ kavacāya huṃ staṃ (l.3) sauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । haṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ । 

dhyānam –
agniṣṭomagurudvayaṃ praṇakaṃ binduṃ triṇetrojvalaṃ 
bhāsva*drudramukhaṃ śivāṅghriyugalaṃ pāśvarthasūryānalam ।* (l.4)
udyadbhāskarakoṭikoṭisadṛśaṃ hamsaṃ jagadvyāpinaṃ 
śabdabrahma*mayaṃ hṛdambujakuṭīnīḍe sadā saṃsmaret ॥* (l.5)
ajapākhyāṃ ca gāyatrīmātmānaṃprati kārayet ।
saśivassarvakṛcchuddhaḥ muktimanveti *sarvadā ॥* (p.170b, l.1)

taduktam –
nityaśśuddho buddhamuktasvabhāvaḥ satyaḥ sūkṣmaḥ san ...vilaścādvitīyaḥ ānandāsthiryaḥ paraḥ sohamasmapratyakdhātuṃ (l.2) nātra saṃśītirasti । asya śrī praṇavamahāmantrasya amitagautamari(ṛ)ṣiḥ । gātrīśchandaḥ । parabrahmadevatā । akāro bījam ukāraḥ śaktiḥ । (l.3) makāraṃ kīlakam । paramātmā kṣetraṃ parabrahmatattvaṃ parabrahmavācyam abhilaṣitārthasiddhe jape viniyogaḥ । aṅganyāsaṃ (l.4) karanyāsam । ṣaṭśuddhiḥ praṇavairvidhāya । oṃ bhūḥ abvagniātmane sarvajñānaśaktyātmane aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ bhuvaḥ prajāpatyā(l.5)tmanena nityatṛptaiśvaryai śaktyātmane tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ suvaḥ sūryātmane anādibodhaśaktyātmane madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ bhūrbhuva(p.171a, l.1)ssuvaḥ brahmātmane svatantrasarvaśaktyātmane anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ bhāsvatiluptavidyāśaktyātmane kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ ...spadati anantastejaḥ (l.2) śaktyātmane karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ । aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntam aṅgulitrayaparvasu bāhudvayasandhitrayeṣu pādadvisandhitrayeṣu (l.3) iḍāpiṅgalāsuṣumnāsu mūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhānamaṇipūrakaanāhata-viśuddhyājñāsu aṃ namaḥ uṃ namaḥ maṃ namaḥ । athavā (l.4) akāraukāramakārabindunādebhyo namaḥ । iti vā nyāsaṃ kṛtvā ।

kriyādeśe(darśe) –
akāraṃ mūladeśe tu svādhiṣṭhāne tayornamaḥ । (l.5)
varṇaṃ maṇipūrake tu ukārasya da(a)nāhate ॥
viśuddhau nādamityuktam ājñāyāṃ daṇḍamīritam ।

	iti nyāsaṃ kṛtvā । ājñāyāṃ dvādaśānte (p.171b, l.1) sarvanyāsaṃ kuryāt । oṃ aṃ namaḥ ...........dvayoḥ । .....................payoḥ । kaṭyoḥ pāṇyoḥ gulphayornyāsaṃ kuryāt । .....(l.2)..dātmavācāmapi drumādyaṃ vedyaṃ svasaṃvedaguṇena santaḥ । ātmānamāta.....................kaniṣṭhani ṣaṭkukṣīpīṭhasaṃsthaṃ ....(l.3)...tadupari vila-vatsomasūtraṃ sabinduṃ tanmadhye nādasasthaṃ praṇavamayamṛdaṃ pi(bi)ndunādau punaśchā + ................(l.4)....... dinaṃ tārakaṃ vyomasaṃsthaṃ sarvādhāraṃ maheśaṃ kasṛdapi manasā dhyāyate yaḥ sa ..........(l.5)....muktaḥ । 
jīvahṛtpadmamadhyasthaṃ sarvātmānamanāmayam ।
.................................ktaḥ sa ucyate ॥

praṇavanyāsavidhiḥ –

mūlādhāre –
vaṃśakṣa......(p.172a, l.1)saṃ caturdalapadmavāsine siddhilakṣmīvallabhāsametāya gaṇapataye amsassohaṃ hamsasvāhā ।

svādhiṣṭhāne – 
laṃ haṃ maṃ yaṃ raṃ (l.2) laṃ ṣaḍdalapadmanivāsine sāvitrīgāyatrīsametāya brahmaṇe hamsassohaṃ hamsasvāhā ।

maṇipūrake –
ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ (l.3) taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ paṃ phaṃ daśadalapadmanivāsine dharālakṣmīsametāya viṣṇave hamsassohaṃ ham(msa)svāhā (l.4) anāteḥ kaṃ khaṃ gaṃ ghaṃ ḍhaṃ caṃ chaṃ jaṃ jhaṃ ñaṃ ṭaṃ ṭhaṃ dvādaśadalapadmanivāsine kauryā maṇikarṇikāsametāya (l.5)....(para?)meśvarāya hamsasso'haṃ hamsasvāhā ।

viśuddhau –
aṃ āṃ iṃ īṃ uṃ ūṃ ṛm ṝm ḷm ḹm eṃ aiṃ oṃ auṃ (p.172b, f.188a, l.1) aṃ aḥ ṣoḍaśadalapadmanivāsine unmanā viśvādīnisametāya maheśvarāya hamsasso'haṃ hamsasvāhā । (l.2)

ājñāyām –
laṃ kṣaṃ dvidilapadmanivāsine ābrahmarandhrāgraṃ manonmanī vāmādiśaktisametāya sadāśivāya śrī (l.3) gurave hamsasso'haṃ hamsasvāhā ।

antarmātṛkavidhiḥ –
āsanamantrasya merupṛṣṭhari(ṛ)ṣiḥ sutalaṃ chandaḥ kūrmo (l.4) devatā laṃ bījaṃ māyā śaktiḥ huṃ kīlakaṃ sakalakarma-siddhyarthamāsane viniyogaḥ । anantāsanāya namaḥ । hṛdaye – kūrmāsanāya namaḥ । (l.5) śirase svāhā । yogāsanāya namaḥ । śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । vīrāsanāya kavacāya hum । padmāsanāya netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । vimalā(p.173a, l.1)sanāya astrāya phaṭ । oṃ bhūrbhuvasvaromiti digbandhaḥ । ugrabhūtapiśācādyā ete sarvavirodhitaḥ । eteṣāmavirodhena brahmakarma (l.2) samācaret । apasarpantu ye bhūtā ye bhūtā + jñayā । 

pṛthvī tvayādhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtāḥ । 
tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ devī pavitraṃ *kuru śāsanam ॥* (l.3)
caturaśramālikhet pūjāmalaṅkārameva ca । 
pṛṣṭhe manmathabījāni catuṣkoṇaṃ tu tārakam ॥
aṣṭavargaṃ *tu saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) kūrmayantraṃ praśasyate ।* (l.4)
madhye huṃ phaṭ kriyāsārdhaṃ japaṃ koṭiphalaṃ bhavet ॥

	mantram – oṃ namo bhagavate (l.5) kūrmarūpāya viṣṇave anantāya huṃ phaṭ svāhā । pūrvavat ṣaḍaṅgadhyānaṃ kṛtvā । prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt । tadyathā – dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭha.....(p.173b, l.1)...bhyāṃ dakṣiṇanāsāṃ pidhāya । pā....(vauṣaḍa)ntapraṇavena daśa kṛtvāpūrya nāsikādvayaṃ pidhāya daśa kṛtvā praṇavena .......(l.2).........daśa kṛtvā praṇavena recayet । evaṃ triḥ prāṇānāyamya । dakṣakarṇe dakṣāṅguṣṭhena saṃspṛśya vāmapā(l.3).......kṣi(nikṣi)pet । praṇavasya ri(ṛ)ṣiḥ brahmā devīgāyatrīcchandaḥ paramātmā devatā । aṃ bījam । uṃ śaktiḥ । maṃ kīlakam । mama sa(l.4).......dyarthe prāṇāyāme viniyogaḥ । aṃ brahmātmane hṛdayāya namaḥ । uṃ viṣṇvātmane śirase svāhā । maṃ rudrātmane (l.5) .........vauṣaṭ । aṃ brahmātmane kavacāya hum । uṃ viṣṇvātmane netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । maṃ rudrātmane astrāya phaṭ । saptavyāhṛtīṃ (p.174a, l.1) .........saha daśa kṛtvā । ......tya । ....vādibhiri(ṛ)ṣiśchando devatā । asya । āyātu varadā devīti japtvā । sāvitrī ri(ṛ)ṣiśchando devatā । (l.2)......maṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā ....... aṣṭāviṃśati kṛtvā । upasthānaṃ kariṣya iti । uttame śikhare devītyuktvā । mitrasyatve (l.3) ........ imamme varuṇeti sāyam । prātaḥ pūrvābhimukhaḥ । madhyāhne sūryābhimukhaḥ । sāye paścimābhimukhaḥ । (l.4) ......dakṣiṇā kṛtya abhivandanam । daṇḍavat bhūmau namaskṛtya । vaidikaṃ karma pūrvaṃ nirvartya । paścācchaivīṃ sandhyā(l.5)............. uttarābhimukhaḥ samācamya śaivasandhyāṃ kārayet । tatprakāraḥ । añjalihastaḥ ।
prātaḥ śuklo*pavītī ... raktavastravibhūṣaṇaṇā ।* (p.174b, f.190, l.1)
raktā bālā caturvaktrā caturhastāṣṭalocanā ।
hamsapadmāsanā svayasṛk...pā(l.2)............ ॥
....kuṇḍīdharā vāme tu kṛṣṇasārājinānvite । iti hṛdaye dhyātvā । 

madhyāhne । 
vaiṣṇavī *garuḍa...........śukaparicchadā ।* (l.3)
ekāsyā vanamālāḍhyā yauvanasthā dvilocanā ॥
kṛṣṇājinā ca vāmādau *śaṅkhacakragadābhayā ।* (l.4) iti bindau dhyātvā । 

sāye । 
raudrī prauḍhā vṛṣābjasthā kṛṣṇā kṛṣṇa.....padā ।
ekavaktrā *triṇetrā ca jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitā ॥* (l.5)
kṛṣṇājinopavītāḍhyā vyāghracarmāmbarapriyā ।
śūlākṣamālinīṃ savye *vāme sābhayaśaktikā ॥* (p.175a, l.1)

	iti brahmarandhre dhyātvā । paścāt svadehaṃ sūryamaṇḍale praviṣṭāṃ bhāvayet । jalaṃ (l.2) nirīkṣaṇādibhiścaturbhiḥ samskṛtya bindāpaṃ(pama)ṅkuśamudrayā vauṣaḍantaṃ hṛdā tīrthānyākṛṣya udbhavayā jale vinyasya (l.3) digbandhanādibhiramṛtīkṛtya tajjalaṃ hastābhyāṃ saṃgṛhya vauṣaḍasaṃhitāmantraiḥ sva śirasi mārjayet । paścāda(dda)kṣa(l.4)hastena ja........ṇi tale nikṣipya saṃhitayābhimantrya ।  vāmahastāṅguli-randhrasrāvitajalena (l.5) dakṣaha..........vauṣaḍantasaṃhitāmantraiḥ sva śirasi mārjayet । paścāt vāmahastaśeṣitajalaṃ dakṣahaste saṃgṛhya (p.175b, f.191a, l.1) nāsā sa.....nī(nīya) dharmarūpaṃ sitaṃ dhyātvā । pūrakeṇa iḍayākṛṣya kumbhakenāstraṃ trivāraṃ sarvāṅgeṣu vyāpta(l.2)pāpaṃ tat jyo[tsnayā]śca(sta)masitarūpaṃ dakṣarecakena dakṣahastena srāvitaṃ bhāvya bhrūvikṣepasahitaṃ krodhakaḥ sva (l.3) dakṣiṇe vajraśilāyāṃ huṃ phaḍantāstreṇa visṛjya । paścādācamya । vakṣyamāṇavat sakalīkṛtya kavacenātmānaṃ (l.4) pariṣicya sūryamaṇḍale sadāśivaṃ dhyātvā svāhāntaṃ śivamantreṇa arghyaṃ datvā upaviśya ।
oṃ tanmaheśāya vidmahe mahādevāya (l.5) dhīmahi tanno śivapracodayāditi daśavāraṃ japtvā paścāt sūryamaṇḍale praviṣṭaṃ sva dehaṃ bhānurūpaṃ vibhāvya । (p.176a, l.1) bahirnirgatya । 

gāyatrīṃ hṛdi sañcintya śuklāṃ śuklopavītinīm । 
tiryaprasṛtadakṣāṅghrīṃ vāmārghya(rghyā)bjadale sthitām ॥ (l.2)
ekavaktrāṃ triṇetrāṃ ca jaḍā(ṭā)khaṇḍendumaṇḍitām ।
tripuṇḍravalibhirdīptā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
kṛṣṇājinottarāsaṅgāṃ śuklavastra*vibhūṣitām ।* (l.3)
sākṣasūtraṃ varadaṃ savye vāme padmābhayānvitām ॥

	iti dhyātvā । gāyatryā visarjanāsya datvā । sūryamaṇḍale (l.4) praviṣṭāṅgāścatrīṃ vibhāvayet । tripradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mūlamantraṃ daśavāraṃ japitvā ācamya sakalīkṛtya mantrāṇāṃ (l.5) dīpanāya tarpayet । aṅgulimukhena śivādīśādyastrāntai(ntaiḥ) dvādaśamantrairlakṣmīsarasvatībrāhmīmāheśvarī (p.176b, f.192, l.1) vaiṣṇavīumāgaurī- manonmanītyaṣṭabhirmantraiḥ svāhāpadaprāntaistarpayet । nivītī । aṅgulīnāṃ parvasandhibhiḥ atriviśvā(l.2)mitrapulastya pralabhaṃ kṛtuvasiṣṭhamarīciśceti saptabhirnāmabhirntraiḥ .....ntena tarpayet । aṅguṣṭhasattanīviti (l.3) kaniṣṭhāmūladeśābhyāṃ sanakasanandanaatrisanātana-sanatkumārakavilapratu pañcaśikhā manuṣyeśvarā iti daśa nāmabhi(bhiḥ) (l.4) vauṣaḍantaṃ tarpayet । hastayugmā tathopavīti hastābhyāṃ mūlenākāśānilatejo jalaprathāti pañcabhūtāni (l.5) vauṣaḍantaṃ tarpayet । prācīnāvītī । savyajānuṃ bhūmau kṛtvā dakṣāṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhye īśasadāśiva(p.177a)śāntasomavān । aṅgiraḥ sa kavyavāhanaḥ । agniṣvāttā ityabhimantraiḥ praṇavahṛdbījacuturthyantai(ntaiḥ) svadhā nama iti tarpayet । (l.2) paścāt svasūtroktapitrādīn caturviṃśatistarpayet । agnidagdhāśca jīvā ye yepyadagdhāḥ kule mama । bhūmau dattena toyena (l.3) tatra ptā(tṛptā)yāntu parāṃ gatimiti santarpya nivītivarmaṇāñjalinā trirapā mamayitvā? huṃ phaḍantāstreṇa tīre tryudakaṃ santarpya । (l.4) ācamya । sakalīkṛtya । sūryamantreṇa trivāraṃ śirasi prokṣya trivāraṃ santarpya daśa kṛtvāvartya trivāraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya upasthāya (l.5) namaskṛtya ...ṣṭe mantrān sakalīkṛtapūrvaṃ japtvā ālayaṃ praveśayet । ....śaivasandhyāvandanaṃ kārayediti । (p.177b, l.1) tatpa[ra]mālayaṃ praviśya । pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā namaskṛtya devaṃ prārthya sūryapūjāmārabhet । prāṇānāyamya sūryamantraiḥ (l.2) sakalīkṛtya taireva mantrairarghyaṃ saṃkalpya tenaiva dravyāṇya(nnya)sya ...... bhānurūpaṃ vibhāvya । ātmānamabhyarcya । (l.3) paścimadvāradakṣiṇe – oṃ daṇḍine namaḥ । vāme piṅgalāya namaḥ । vāyavyāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । āgneyyāṃ gurubhyo (l.4) namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । cakrārcanaṃ cet । pūrvābhimukhaḥ । beraṃ cet nairi(ṛ)te sthitvā । uttarābhimukho ra(a)rghyodakena (l.5) beraṃ saṃprokṣya ।

aśvadhvajāya vidmahe padmahastāya dhīmahi tanno bhānupracodayāt ।

	iti abhyarcya । arghyaṃ datvā । nirmālyaṃ vyapohya । (p.178a, l.1) īśāne – caṇḍeśāya samarpya yathā vibhavapadamadhyerabhiṣicya āsanamarcayet । madhye āgneyādi-koṇeṣu prabhūtā(l.2)sanāya namaḥ । vimalāyai namaḥ । oṃ rasāya namaḥ । oṃ ārādhyāya namaḥ । paramasukhāya namaḥ । padmāsanāya namaḥ । pūrvādyaṣṭadaleṣu – dīptāyai (l.3) namaḥ । sūkṣmāyai namaḥ । jayāyai namaḥ । indrāyai namaḥ । vimalāyai namaḥ । amokhā(ghā)yai namaḥ । vidyute namaḥ । oṃ (l.4) madhye sarvatomukhyai namaḥ । padmamudrāyai namaḥ । visphuramudrāyai namaḥ । iti padmavisphuramudrāṃ pradarśya । arkāsanāya (l.5) namaḥ । khaṣolkāya sūryamūrtaye namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । 

dhyāyet –
dvibhujaṃ pañcavaktraṃ ca raktavarṇasvarūpakam ।
karaṇḍamakuṭopetaṃ *sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥* (p.178b, l.1)
ullāsavaktranayanaṃ .....maṇḍalamadhyagam ।
uṣāśca dakṣiṇe pārśve pratyuṣāṃ cottare nyaset ॥ (l.2)
aruṇaṃ tvagrato rūpaṃ paṅkajaṃ dhṛtavigraham ।
saptāśvarathamadhyasthaṃ bhāskaraṃ bhāvayettathā ॥

	sūryāya namaḥ iti (l.3) mūlenāvāhya । sthāpanādyarghapradānāntaṃ kṛtvā । īśānamūrdhādisarvāṇi vinyasya । hṛdayādiṣaḍaṅgāni vinyasya । (l.4) kālakaṇṭhabimbamudrān pradarśya । tālatraya-digbandhanādibhiḥ samskṛtya pādyācamanārghyaṃ datvā । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpairabhyarcya (l.5) kṣīrādibhiḥ saṃsnāpya vastreṇodvartya alaṅkṛtya raktacandanapuṣpadāmādibhirvibhūṣya । ....varaṇamarcayet । vāyunairi(ṛ)ta(p.179a, l.1)śāṃkarā..............caturdikṣu hṛdayādima..... .....somāṅgārakabudhabṛhaspatiśukraśanaiścararāhuketunāmabhiḥ । (l.2) pūrvādīśānāntamabhyarcya tadbāhye indrādīn abhyarcya hṛdayādicaturṇāṃ dhenumudrā, astrasya trāsāni, netrasya goviṣāṇaṃ, (l.3) grahāṇām añjalimudrāṃ pradarśya । pādyādikaṃ datvā । ...grahaṃ vā paraṇabhūnuṃ? dhūpadīpairabhyarcya svayaṃ gajahastavat (l.4) gulānnapānīyatāmbūlādīni nivedya nārājya aruṇaṃ bhānumudvāsya aiśānyāṃ pūrvaparyuṣitapuṣpaiśca te..... (l.5) caṇḍeśvaramāvāhya pādyādikaṃ datvā । sūryopubhuktānnaṃ nivedya visṛjediti sūryapūjā ।

	pādau prakṣālyācamya (p.179b, broken folio – in the beginning & in the end. in the middle small portion is available, l.1) ...stena dakṣahastatalapṛṣṭhamekavāraṃ dakṣahastena......(l.2)....... śaktaye vauṣaḍiti aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ saṃspṛṣṭe karatala........(l.3)..........oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye namaḥ । iti mūrtiṃ vinyasya ........(l.4).......iti aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ karatalayoḥ vinyasya । oṃ hāṃ netra............(l.5)......aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ mūlaṃ karatalayoḥ vinyasya .............(p.180a, broken like 179b, l.1).......vauṣaḍiti madhyamāditribhiraṅgulibhiḥ.........(l.2)........oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śirase svāhā । ............
