(l.3) aṃ mūrdhni āṃ lalāṭe iṃ dakṣiṇanetre īṃ vāmanetre ..............(l.4).....yāṃ ṝm vāmanāsikāyām ḷm dakṣiṇakapole ḹm vāmaka[pole] ................(l.5) auṃ ........nte aṃ śikhāyāṃ aḥ jihvāgre । dakṣiṇabā..........(p.182a, secon half of the folio broken, l.1)dapañca........di । vāmapādapañcasandhiṣu tādi । dakṣiṇapa..........(l.2)yaṃ hṛdayeṣu । dakṣiṇadormūle raṃ vāmadormūle । ja......................(l.3) saṃ vāmabāhau haṃ dakṣiṇapāde laṃ vāmapāde ..................(l.4) śivāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye namaḥ । iti ma..........(l.5)ṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ mukhe oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । aṅgu............(p.182b, second half of the folio broken, l.1) aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ nābhau oṃ hiṃ vāmadeva-guhyāya...........(l.2)ye namaḥ । iti daṇḍabhaṅgīṃ nyasya । pūrvavanmudrayā sarvamarcya pū..........(l.3) muṇḍabhaṅgīṃ nyasya । aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ pūrvavat vidhāya ...............(l.4).....madhyamādibhistribhiraṅgulibhiḥ netrayoḥ oṃ hauṃ ................(l.5)ṣṭhābhyāṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । hṛdi aṅguṣṭhā..........(p.183a, second half of the folio broken, l.1)........... śikhāyām । aṅguṣṭhamadhyamānāmikākaniṣṭhika............(l.2)......nāntaṃ vāmahastamadhaḥ dakṣiṇa-hastamūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā ..........(l.3)...........bindusthāneṣu astrāyaphaḍiti triḥ santāḍya aṅgu............(l.4)....... rā(nārā)camudrayā kavacena trivāramavakuṇṭhya vauṣaḍantamūle ............. (l.4) ṣaḍantaśaktimastreṇa pādādimūrdhāntaṃ mahāmudrayā pari..........(lp.183b, l.1) ....nnābhau prakalpayet । lalāṭe ceśvaraṃ dhyātvā varadaṃ sarvatomukham ityantaryajanakrameṇa jyotirliṅgaṃ hṛdabje sañcintya (l.2) arghyādikaṃ kalpayet । astreṇa pātratrayaṃ prakṣālya yantradhā(sā?)reṣu vinyasya vauṣaḍahṛdādigandhodakena pātrā(l.3)ṇyāpūryā(rya) ...... dvayam ekapātre vākṣatadūrvākuśā-grāṇi vinyasya praṇavena saptadhā'bhimantrya amṛtīkṛtya ācamanāya (l.4) tatpūtre phalatrayakarpūratuṭipatrapāṭalīsiddhārthośīrayutaṃ vinyasya namontasahitayābhyarcya । amṛtīkṛtya tatpātre arghyā (l.5).......kṣīrakuśākṣatataṇḍulasumanasatilayavasiddhārthān vinyasya । āsanamūrtipañcabra(brahma)netravidyādehamūlamantranetrahīnaṃ (p.184a, l.1) .... namontenābhyarcya mūlena saptavāramabhimantrya astreṇa tālatrayadigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā kavacenāvakuṇṭhya (l.2) vaiṣa.....mūlena dhainumudrayā amṛtīkṛtyā(tya) vauṣaḍantaśaktimantreṇa mahāmudrayā paramīkṛtya । dravyāṇa(ṇā)mabhāve akṣatatoya (l.3) ........ kuśāgrāṇi vinyasya praṇavena sadyena puruṣeṇa īśānenābhyarcya tenaivābhimantrya amṛtīkuryāditi (l.4) sāmānyārghyapādyācamanārghyavidhiḥ ।

(mantraśuddhiḥ -)
	mūlena candanaṃ lalāṭe dhṛtvā puṣpaṃ śirasi vinyasya ātmānamabhyarcya puṣpāñjalibhūtvā śivamantraṃ (l.5) layāntaṃ trivāramuccāryā(rya) gaurībījaṃ pañcabrahmāṅgāni sakṛduccārayediti mantraśuddhiḥ ।

(pañcāmṛtavidhiḥ -)
	bhūmimastreṇārghyavāriṇā (p.184b, f.201a, l.1) saṃprokṣya bhūmidevimabhyarcya nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya sthalaśuddhiṃ vidadhyāt । hastamātreṇa prāgudagraiścatuḥ (l.2) sūtrairnavakoṣṭhamāsphālya śālibhisthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā darbhaiḥ paristīrya madhyakoṣṭhe oṃ hāṃ śivatattvakoṣṭhāya namaḥ । pūrve (l.3) oṃ sadāśivatattvakoṣṭhāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe oṃ vidyātattvakoṣṭhāya namaḥ । uttare oṃ puruṣatattvakoṣṭhāya namaḥ । paścime kālatattvakoṣṭhāya namaḥ । (l.4) .....hane prakṛti namaḥ । āgneyyāṃ - pṛthvītattva + namaḥ । nairi(ṛ)te – appu + namaḥ । vāyau teja + namaḥ । madhyādipātreṣu oṃ supratiṣṭha(l.5)pātrāya namaḥ । oṃ suśānta + namaḥ । oṃ tejorūpa + namaḥ । oṃ ratnodaka + namaḥ । oṃ amṛtātma + namaḥ । oṃ avyakta + namaḥ । oṃ niyatipātrāya (p.185a, l.1) namaḥ । kālapātrāya namaḥ । kalāpātrāya namaḥ । cut । āpyāyasveti kṣīramāpūrya । dadhikrāviṇṇeti pūrve dadhi । śukramasīti dakṣiṇe (l.2) ghṛtam । tatsavituriti uttare gomūtram । gandhadvāreti paścime gomayam । īśāne devasyatveti kuśodakam । agnimīleti (l.3) āgneyyāṃ vi(pi?)ṣṭam(ṣṭaram)? । naiṛte iṣṭhe tvetyāmalakam । agnaāyāhīti vāyave rajanīm । kṣīre rudraṃ dadhni maheśaṃ ghṛte sadā(l.4)śivam । gomūtre brahmāṇam । gomaye viṣṇum । kuśodake manonmanīm । piṣṭe umām āmalake śrīdevīm । rajanyāṃ (l.5) sarasvatīm । ityevam ityevam adhidevatāṃ saṃpūjya । kṣīrādhipari(ṛ)ṣiṃ pūjayet । bhāradvāra(ja)kāśyapaviśvāmitragautamātreya-bhārga(p.185b, f.202a, l.1)vāgastyakauśikaparāśaradhāritān । saṃbhāvya īśānādisadyāntaiḥ kṣīrādīni ekadvitricatuḥpañcagāyatryā kuśodakaṃ (l.2) ṣaḍvāraṃ piṣṭaṃ hṛdayaśiraśikhākavacena sakṛtsakṛdabhimantrya gomayaṃ gojale । gojalaṃ ghṛte । ghṛtaṃ dadhini । dadhi (l.3) kṣīre । kuśodakaṃ ca gokṣīre sadyādīśānāntai(taiḥ) gāyatryā ca pūrya । kavacena mathitvā mūlaṃ yathāśaktiṃ japtvā । (l.4) amṛtīkṛtya । dhūpadīpairabhyarcya nivedayediti । pūrvoktaprakāreṇa sthaṇḍile koṣṭhapātrārcanādikaṃ kṛtvā । (l.5) madhye kṣīraṃ । pūrve dadhi । dakṣiṇe ghṛtam । uttare kṣaudram । paścime śarkarā । ikṣusāraṃ vā īśāne (p.186a, l.1) gandhodakam । āgneyādikoṇeṣu । kadalīphanasaphalam āmbraphalāni vinyasya abhimantrya dhūpadīpādikaṃ dade(l.2)diti । pañcāmṛtavidhiḥ ॥

(snapanavidhiḥ -)
tatpūrve snapanā...ekapañcanavabhirvā । ekakalaśasya pādeśamātram ekakoṣṭhaṃ (l.3) nirmāya śālyādibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā aṣṭadalapadmaṃ vilikhya tanmadhye sūtravastrasvarṇadarbhāgandhāmbupūritacūtapallava(l.4)nālikerakūrcamālyādibhira-laṅkṛtya kumbhe sāsanaṃ sadāśivamāvāhya garbhāvaraṇaṃ saṃpūjya dhūpadīpaṃ (l.5) datvā tenaivābhiṣecayediti snapanavidhiḥ ॥

	pūrvoktavat sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā pūrvādidikṣu caturdala(p.186b, l.1)padmaṃ vilikhya kumbhavardhanīcatuṣkavaśāt alaṅkṛtān galitāmbhasā pūritān madhye karṇikāyāṃ śivakumbham । (l.2) taduttare – uttaranālaṃ pūrvādidaleṣu catuṣkalaśān vinyasya । madhyakumbhe sadāśivam । vardhanyāṃ manonmanī । (l.3) pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu tatpuruṣā-ghoravāmadevasadyojātamantrairāvāhya vidikṣu (l.4) agniyā(gnyā)dikoṇeṣu kalaśānvinyasya puruṣādibhirvā hṛdayādibhirvā saṃpūjyābhiṣecayediti । yāvadaśraṃ tathā (l.5) paṭhettāvasnapanamiṣyate । tatprakāraḥ vṛttaliṅgasya prādeśamātrāntare prāgudagrai(graiḥ) dvi dvi sūtraṃ nirmāya tanmadhye (p.187a śaṅkuṃ sthāpya prādeśamātreṇa remaining place blank, p.187b, f.204a, l.1) udumbare ।

dvinetrau pītavarṇau ca bhadrakāyau bhayaṅkarau ।
kṛtāñjalipuṭopetau dhyāye tāṃ tu prajāpate ॥ (l.2) oṃ bhuvaṅgāya namaḥ । adhodumbare – 

dvibhujau ca dvinetrau ca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitau ।
raktākṣaraktavarṇaṃ ca tadvarṇā*mbarabhūṣitau ॥* (l.3)
dakṣiṇe tu śiro nyasya pādau cottaradiksthitau । oṃ pataṅgāya namaḥ । ūrdhvodumbara(l.4)mukhadakṣiṇe –

niviṣṭaṃ śyāmaṃ gaṇapatimaśri(śra)ye dadhānam ।
vāme laḍḍukaparaśuṃ karābjayugme dattaṃ ca ॥ 

	svaka(l.5)maparatra cākṣamālām । oṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । ūrdhvodumbaramukhavāmato niviṣṭāṃ vande'haṃ dhavalaruciṃ (p.188a, l.1) sarasvatiṃ ca vāme pustakamatha dakṣiṇe । akṣamālāṃ bibhrāṇāṃ karayugale varābhayau ca । oṃ hāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । (l.2)

tanmadhyataḥ kamalamadhyasukhopaviṣṭāṃ 
	hastidvayoddhṛtavaṭāmbukṛtābhiṣekām । 
sarve vāraṇajñasahitaḥ vahatīṃ ca (l.3) 
	lakṣmīṃ vāme tu bilvaphalapadmadharāṃ supītām ॥ -  oṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । 

śūlākṣamāle dadhataṃ karābhyāṃ *vāmetarābhyāṃ jaṭilaṃ triṇetram ।* (l.4)
dvārda(dvāra)dakṣiṇasthāṃ samavāpya śākhāṃ raktaṃ(tnaṃ) sthitaṃ nandimāśrayāmi ॥  -  	oṃ nandine (l.5) namaḥ । 

taduttare tatra niviṣṭamūrtiṃ gaṅgāṃ ca śuklāṃ makarādhirūḍhām । 
hastadvaye dakṣiṇavāmasaṃsthe nīlotpalaṃ *pūrṇaghaṭaṃ vahantīm ॥* (p.188b, f.205a, broken folio at the end, l.1) oṃ hāṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ । 

tadvāmaśākhāsthitakuṣṇamūrtiṃ nāgopavīta........... । (l.2)
...pālaśūlānvitavāmasavyakaraṃ mahākālamahaṃ prapede ॥  -  	oṃ mahākālāya namaḥ । 

yamunāṃ dakṣa.......māṃ kūrmasthitāṃ bhaje । 
vāme dakṣiṇahastasthapūrṇakumbhā sitotpalam ॥  -  	oṃ yamunā......... । 

....*mbare ।* (l.4) 
caturvaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ pradīptāgnisamaprabham । 
śivasyābhimukhaṃ cākṣaśūlābhayavarānvitam ॥
jaṭā.........................*rdhendukṛtaśekharam ।* (l.5)
dvāramantarmukhaṃ jñeyaṃ dvārapālā(lāḥ) bahirmukhāḥ ॥
	oṃ hāṃ astradvārapālāya namaḥ । ............(p.189a, broken folio at the end, l.1)mīpaṃ vṛṣabhe । oṃ hāṃ vṛṣabhāya namaḥ ।
ekavaktraṃ caturbāhurudareṇa kha(ka)rāgrataḥ ।
vṛṣabhaṃ śvetavarṇa....................*mahābalam ॥* (l.2)
cutuṣpādaṃ perūpa? agnirūpaṃ tu śri(śṛ)ṅgakam ।
ādityaṃ candranetraṃ ca mukhaṃ rudrasta........... ॥
.......*ṣṇuśca rudraśca sarvadevamayaṃ śubham ।* (l.3)
sthitaṃ vā śayanaṃ vāpi yuge pādamihocyate ॥
saṃpra..................*kapādabahi(hiḥ) sthitam ।* (l.4)
dharmadevakṛti syānvā(dvā)hanaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ tathā ॥
muṣṭigrīvasamāyuktaṃ dhyāye................... । (l.5)
	....hāravṛṣabhamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ vṛṣabhāya namaḥ ।
tīkṣṇaśri(śṛ)ṅgāya vidmahe vedapādāya dhīmahī ।
tanno vṛṣa................................... ॥ (p.189b, f.206a, l.1)

	.....puṣpadhūpadīpādibhiḥ saṃpūjya rakṣāmeṣāṃ vijñāpya । nyastamūlena cakṣuṣordhvaṃ vilokya divyān oṃ haḥ astrāya(l.2)phaḍiti jvalannārācamudrayāstreṇa puṣpaṃ kṣepayitvā antarikṣān oṃ śliṃ paśa huṃ phaṭ pāśupatāstreṇa (l.3) dakṣiṇapārṣṇighātatrayeṇa bhūmiṣṭhān trividhānvighnānudvāsya । astreṇa ghaṇṭāṃ vādayan । vāmaśākhāṃ samāśrayan । (l.4) hṛdayena pracchi(ccha)nnapaṭamudvāsya astradvārapālān antaḥ praveśārthaṃ vijñāpya । dakṣiṇapādapurassaram antaḥ praviśya । (l.5) dehalyām astramabhyarcya । nīlāturaṅgamadhiruhya virājamānāṃ nīlā(lāṃ)śukābharaṇamālyavilepanāḍhyāṃ nīlāpaṭena bhuvanāni (p.190a, l.1) tirodhanānāṃ khaḍgāyudhāṃ bhagavatīṃ parivādayāmi? pracchi(ccha)nnapaṭe oṃ sthāṇave namaḥ । ardhamaṇḍapasthitāṃ bhogaśaktiṃ (l.2) pādyādidhyānapūrvamāsanamūrtimūlena gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya dhūpadīpau datvā tato garbhagṛhadvāramāśritya dehalyāṃ (l.3) astramabhyarcya dakṣiṇe bhīmarudramarcayet ।

caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca varadābhayasaṃyutam ।
gadācāmarasaṃyuktaṃ bhīmarudraṃ *samarcayet ॥* (l.4)  -  oṃ hāṃ bhīmarudrāya namaḥ ।

niśācarāntakṛt devadeveśayajanārthakam । 
śuddhadvārasya madhye tu *gacchadhvaṃ hi prasīda me ॥* (l.5)  -  iti vijñāpya ।

nairi(ṛ)tyām –
hamsasthaṃ tundilaṃ kūrcaṃ gauraṃ vande yuktamāsyaiścaturbhiḥ । (p.190b, f.207a, l.1)
hastadvandve kuṇḍikāṃ sṛksametāṃ vāme'nyasmin sākṣamālāṃ sṛvaṃ ca ॥

	oṃ vāstvadhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । paścime – (l.2) oṃ gaṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । vāyavye – oṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । uttare - mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । gomukhe - vāmadevāya namaḥ । (l.3) īśāne – saptagurubhyo namaḥ । garbhadvāradakṣiṇavāmayorūrdhvādhaḥ – jñānaśaktiṃ kriyāśaktiṃ saṃpūjya । (l.4) gurvājñayā śivamarcayet । evaṃ garbhāvaraṇasthitān devān yathākrameṇa dhyānapūrvaāsanamūrtimūlena gandhapuṣpādinā(l.5)bhyarcya । dhūpadīpau datvā । paścājjaladroṇīmastreṇa prakṣālya dhūpayitvā । jaladroṇyā .....dijalaṃ (p.191a, l.1) paurāṇyakṛ..........toyamāhṛtya vastragālitena nānā vi....ṣu ghaṭeṣvāryā? ātodyaḥ ...... prādakṣiṇyena (l.2) garbhagehāgneyasthitadroṇyāmāpūrya mūlabrahmāṅgairabhimantrya । pāṭalotpalapadmaca......kaṃ sugandhīni (l.3) elālavaṅgakarpūracandanāgaru-kallola.......dīn snānatoye nikṣipya vāsasi .... nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ (l.4) saṃskṛtya praṣṭho'ṣṭhaudare brahmaviṣṇurudradhiṣṭhitam । bindāvaṅkuśamudrayā tīrthānyākṛṣya saṃhitayābhyarcyā(rcya) (l.5) īkṣaṇādidigbandhanāvakuṇṭhanāmṛtīkaraṇa-paramīkaraṇaiḥ kṛtvā abhiṣekadravyāṇi saṃskuryāt । (p.191b, l.1) .......udarepṛṣṭhe rudraviṣṇubrahmādhiṣṭhitamabhyarcya śivasya dakṣe āgneyyāmuttarābhimukhāṃ hastamā(l.2).....cet āsīnaḥ ūrdhvaṃ cet sthitvā devaṃ yaṣṭvā arghyodakena astreṇa liṅgaṃ saṃprokṣya śiva(l.3).....īśānādirarghyaṃ datvā ....śanādibhiḥ daṇḍabhaṅgī muṇḍabhaṅgī nyasya īśānādibhirabhyarcya । (l.4) ..... dhūpadīpaṃ datvā mūrtikāmaścet sadyādibhiḥ aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ navapuṣpaṃ saṃgṛhya (l.5) tarja..... īśāne nirmālyaṃ saṃgṛhyā(hya) hṛdā navapuṣpamāropyala caṇḍeśāya aiśānyāṃ vā caṇḍa(p.192a, l.1)mūlaliṅga.............. tailodgatayantredbhavaśuddhatailairgavyā śiromantre (l.2) pīṭhāntaṃ saṃsicya abhiṣicya dakṣahastena gaḍḍukamādāya jalaṃ saṃgṛhya vāmahastatale nikṣipya (l.3) susiktena gaḍḍukaṃ saṃgṛhya patākapā? jalairanucaladbhiḥ astreṇa liṅgapīṭhaṃ (l.4) pāśu[patā]streṇa samantāt sama[bhi]ṣicya .......... pṛthak pṛthak hṛdayaśiraśśikhāmantraiḥ madhye (l.5) hṛda...kuśo śirādikūrcakavāsinā liṅgaṃ pīṭhaṃ bahuśaḥ samantāt samyagāśodhya toyenā(p.192b, f.209a, l.1)bhiṣicya sāmānyārghyeṇāstreṇābhiṣicya savyadakṣahastena muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā oṃ hauṃ hrīṃ śivaśaktibhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.2) aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa liṅgaṃ pīṭhaṃ muṣṭyā spṛśediti liṅgaśuddhiḥ ।

atha pīṭhapūjā – 
	tato devasya pīṭhādho(l.3)bhāge madhyataḥ oṃ yogapīṭhāsanāya namaḥ । kūrmaśilāyām । kūrmaśilāyai namaḥ । tadupari ādhāraśilāyām । oṃ (l.4) ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । ratnādhāre – oṃ anantāya namaḥ । tadupari oṃ anantāya namaḥ(rep) । āgneyādikoṇeṣu pādarasān । (l.5) dharmāya namaḥ । jñānāya namaḥ । vairāgyāya namaḥ । aiśvaryāya namaḥ । pūrvādidikṣu phalakāsu – adharmāya namaḥ । ajñānāya (p.193a, l.1) namaḥ । avairāgyāya namaḥ । anaiśvaryāya namaḥ । nairi(ṛ)te – adhacchadanāya namaḥ । īśāne – ūrdhvacchadanāya namaḥ । (l.2) pūrvādidikṣu – kṛtayugāya namaḥ । tretāyugāya namaḥ । dvāparayugāya namaḥ । kaliyugāya namaḥ । avyaktāya namaḥ । niyataye namaḥ । (l.3) kālāya namaḥ । kalāya namaḥ । madhyapāśe – keśavāya namaḥ । padmāsanāya namaḥ । adhobhāganmadhye – kandāya namaḥ । bījāya namaḥ । (l.4) aṅkurāya namaḥ । nālāya namaḥ । kaṇṭakebhyo namaḥ । dalebhyo namaḥ । kesarebhyo namaḥ । karṇikāyai namaḥ । bījebhyo namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ padmāya namaḥ । praṇavahṛdbījacaturthyantaiḥ svanāmabhiḥ saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ padmamudrāyai namaḥ । iti svāyambhuvadevikaliṅge (p.193b, f.210a, l.1) piṅgāntaṃ vyāpakaṃ sañcintya mānuṣaṃ cet ādhāraśilāyāṃ pañcāsanaṃ calaṃ cet pīṭhe sarvāsanamarcayet । (l.2) āvaraṇamapi tadvat । iha piṇḍiko-rdhvakarṇikābāhye pūrvādyaṣṭadaleṣu vāmā-jyeṣṭhā-raudrī-kālī-kalavikaraṇī(l.3)balavikaraṇī balapramathanī sarvabhūtadamanī karṇikāvyāptaṃ madhye manonmanyai namaḥ । iti praṇava(l.4)hṛdbījacaturthyantaiḥ strīliṅgaṅgāntairarcayet । patrāgre pūrve oṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya namaḥ । oṃ sūryamaṇḍalādhipataye (l.5) āgneye brahmaṇe namaḥ । kesare dakṣiṇe somamaṇḍalāya namaḥ । somamaṇḍalādhipataye nairi(ṛ)te (p.194a, l.1) viṣṇave namaḥ । paścime karṇikāyām । vahnimaṇḍalāya namaḥ । vahnimaṇḍalādhipataye vāyavye rudrāya namaḥ । (l.2) uttare karṇikordhve śaktimaṇḍalāya namaḥ । śaktimaṇḍalādhipataye īśāne sadāśivāya namaḥ । pañcāsanavyāpakam । (l.3) dhārikā...śaktibījama...ntapaṃ kajakūrmalaṃ? puṇyabodhavirāgabhūtipadaṃ vilomacatuṣṭayaṃ (l.4) gātraka...danadvayaṃ kamalakesarakarṇikāśaktimaṇḍasaṃyutaṃ mahantormi śivāsanam । oṃ śivāsanāya namaḥ । (l.5) puṣpairañjalimāpūrya vidvannibhāmanalastambhanibhāṃ śivamūrtiṃ sañcintya । oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye nama iti mantraṃ (p.194b, l.1) vauṣaḍantamuccārya puṣpāñjaliṃ bhrūparyanaṃ nītvā liṅgāgrahe āvāhya । daṇḍabhaṅgī muṇḍabhaṅgī kalābhyāṃ ...ṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ ca (l.2) nyastvā sadāśivaṃ dhyātvā ।

sadākhyaṃ sarvadeveśaṃ pratyakṣaṃ sarvatomukham ।
kṣityādikuṭilāprāntamantrasihmāsanasthitam ॥
sahasradala*padmasthaṃ karṇikāmadhyavāsinam ।* (l.3)
dviraṣṭavatsarākāraṃ jaṭācūḍavibhūṣitam ॥
nāgopavītinaṃ śāntaṃ jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitam । (l.4)
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ sadyojātaṃ prakīrtitam ॥
abhayaṃ śūlaparaśuṃ vajrakhaḍgaṃ ca dakṣiṇe ।
vāmabhāge'nalaṃ *ghaṇṭānāgaṃ pāśāṅkuśaṃ tathā ॥* (l.5)
babhruścandrāṃśu śi(śa)śi saumyaśuklāmbarānvitam ।
hārakaṅkaṇakeyūraṃ *mudrikābhiralaṅkṛtam ॥* (p.195a, l.1)
sañcintya mūrterupari śaktimātravijṛmbhaṇam ।
liṅgākāropamardena bhāvane liṅgatāṃ tyajet ॥ (l.2)
evaṃ vidyātmakaṃ dehaṃ nyasedārohagocare ।
gomukhīṃ dakṣiṇe pārśve dhyāyedvidvānmanīm? ॥
caturbhujāṃ *triṇetrāṃ ca dvipā...pīṭhasaṃsthitām ।* (l.3)
varadābhayasaṃyuktāṃ padmotpaladharānvitām ॥
śivavāmāṅgabhāge *tu ropitāṃ vāmanonmanīm ।* (l.4)

	evaṃ dhyātvā puṣpāñjaliṃ kṛtvā । oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ vidyādehāya (l.5) nama iti sadāśivāntamuccārya añjaliṃ kāntakaṃ nītvā । liṅgaṃ rohe āvāhya madhyādipañcadaśasu netreṣu netramantreṇa (p.195b, f.212a, l.1) netramudrāṃ pradarśya । paścādañjalimāpūrya । hṛddeśe nidhāya । prāsādaṃ daśākṣaraṃ vā uccārya pūrya kumbhakenopadiṣṭa(l.2)mārgeṇa layāntamuccārya hṛdayasthaṃ bindumadhyasthaṃ dvādaśāntasthe saṃyojyā(jya) । vidyunnibhaṃ vibhāvya añjaliṃ dvādaśāntaṃ (l.3) nītvā । patatpataṅgapratimāmiva bhrūmadhye viśrāntatārakākāra(raṃ) sañcintya । añjaliṃ hṛddeśe nidhāya । (l.4) recakena hastapuṣpagataṃ pūrṇacandrākāraṃ dhyātvā । sadyojātena sadāśivasya ...mārgeṇa hṛdayakamale praviṣṭaṃ(ṣṭhaṃ) (l.5) vibhāvya āvāhya vāmadevena sthāpya aghoreṇa sannidhīkṛtya puruṣeṇa nirudhya īśānena svāgatam (p.196a, l.1) īśādipañcamūrdhāsu svāhāntena mṛgyā?bajalapuṣpeṇa viśeṣārghyeṇa nirodhanārghyaṃ datvā pañcādañjalihastaḥ (l.2) svāminsarvajagannātheti prārthyāstreṇa kālakaṇṭhīṃ mūlena liṅgamudrāṃ pañcamukhīṃ sadāśivaśaktimudrā(l.3)mañjalimudrāṃ pradarśya । sadāśivasya hṛdayādisthāneṣu hṛdayādimantrān netrahīnaṃ vinyasya tālatrayadigbandhanā(l.4)vakuṇḍa(ṇṭha)nairamṛtīkṛtya paramīkaraṇenāṅgaśivayorabhinnaṃ vibhāvayediti āvāhanavidhiḥ । (l.5)

namontaṃ sadye na(da)kṣavāmapādayoḥ pādyaṃ svadhāntaṃ puruṣeṇa puruṣādīśāntaṃ vaktreṣvācamanīyam । (p.196b, f.213a, l.1) īśānādipañcamūrdhasvīśānādimantreṇa svāhāntenārghyaṃ pūrvārcitapuṣpaṃ puṣpeṇa visṛjya । namontamūlena (l.2) gandhaṃ dūrvākṣatapuṣpāṇi vauṣaḍantamūlena dadyāt । dhūpadīpaṃ datvā yathālābhaṃ nivedya । pīṭhapūjāpūrvaṃ (l.3) malasnānaṃ tadūrdhve vidhisnānāya mahāsnānaṃ pañcagavyābhiṣekasthiraistvāvāhanordhvataḥ । calaliṅgaṃ cet (l.4) bhāgiśuddhyantaṃ pīṭhapūjāpūrve pañcagavyābhiṣekam । 

kṣīradadhighṛtaṃ kṣaudram ikṣusāraṃ krameṇa tu । (l.5)
likucādikadalyādiphalapañcāmṛtaṃ tathā ॥
jaladhūpāntaraiḥ kāryo abhiṣeko bhave sati ।
pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgaistu *krameṇaivābhiṣecayet ॥* (p.197a, l.1)
evaṃ kṛtvābhiṣekaṃ tu gandhodakaṃ tu paścimam ।
dhūpadīpaṃ tato *datvā mantrānnaṃ tu nivedayet ॥* (l.2)
snapanairabhiṣicyātha dhārāsahasrakārcanam ।
sahasradhārāpātraṃ tu śakti*maṇḍalamarcayet ॥* (l.3)
sa ca nānmā(mnā) sahasreṇa śivanāmnaiva cārcayet ।
tatkāle śaṅkhatoyena abhiṣekaṃ *viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.4)
śaṅkhamūle dharā devī kukṣo viṣṇava(vā)dhidevatam ।
agre tu brahmadevatyaṃ satīrthāni tatra vai ॥ (l.5)
rekhāyāṃ ravidaivatyaṃ pṛṣṭhe vai somadaivatam ।
nandikālau pārśvadeśe saṃpūjya(jyā)bhiṣecayet ॥ (p.197b, f.214a, pañcāvaraṇam, l.1)
viśeṣārghyodakenaiva abhiṣekaṃ samācaret ।
pādyāditritayaṃ datvā vastreṇodvartanaṃ kuru ॥ (l.2)
vastramābharaṇaṃ gandhaṃ puṣpamālyādyalaṅkṛtam ।
paścātsaṃpūjayeddevaṃ bhogāṅgāni krameṇa tu ॥ (l.3)

āvaraṇapūjā –
brahmādyāvaraṇaṃ śambhoḥ pūjayāmi tavājñayā ।
pūjayedadya tu jñātajambhorāvaraṇaṃ yajet ॥
brahmā pañcāṅga*vidyāṅgavidyeśāṃśca gaṇeśvarān ।* (l.4)
dikpālakāni cāstrāṇi sarveṣāṃ mānamucyate ॥
caturbhujāstriṇetrāśca *sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ।* (l.5)
namaskārakarākārāḥ sarve akṣamālābjadharakā ॥

	iti pañcāvaraṇadevatān dhyātvā sadāśivasya (p.198a, l.1) vaktrebhyo'ṅgebhyo pādadeśayoścāṅkuśa-mudrayā dīpāddīpavadākṛṣya । liṅgapīṭhe karṇikāyāṃ (l.2) īśāne īśānam । pūrve tatpuruṣam । dakṣiṇe'?ghoram । uttare vāmadevam । paścime sadyojātam । āgneyyāṃ hṛdayam । aiśānyāṃ (l.3) śiraḥ । nairi(ṛ)tyāṃ śikhā । vāyavyāṃ kavacam । īśāne śiram । madhye netram । pūrvādidikṣu – astramiti ।

(dvitīyāvaraṇam / garbhāvaraṇam)
garbhāvaraṇam – tat (l.4) bāhye । pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottaraīśānāgneyanairi(ṛ)tavāyuve(yavye)ṣu । anantasūkṣmaśivottamekanetrakarudra(l.5)trimūrtiśrīkaṇṭhaśikaṇṭhana iti dvitīyāvaraṇam ।

(tṛtīyāvaraṇam -)
pīṭhakaṇṭhe – uttarādivāyvāntam । umācaṇḍeśavṛṣabhanandimahākāla(p.198b, f.215a, l.1)gaṇeśabhṛṅgīśamahāsena । iti tṛtīyāvaraṇam ।

(caturthāvaraṇam -)
pīṭhopāne tu pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu – īśānanairi(ṛ)te ca – indrāgni(l.2)ya(yama)niṛtivaruṇavāyukuberaīśānabrahmaviṣṇu । iti caturthāvaraṇam ।

ādhāraśilāyām – pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu – (l.3) īśānanaiṛte ca – vajraśaktidaṇḍakhaḍgapāśāṅkuśagadātriśūlapadmacakra-nāmānīti praṇavahṛdbījacaturthaiḥ । (l.4) sva sva nāmabhiḥ sva sva bījaiśca pṛthak pṛthak āvāhanādipādyādibhiḥ gandhapuṣpairabhyarcya । dhenupadmatriśūlamakarasṛgiti (l.5) pañcāvaraṇadevānāṃ pañcamudrāmañjalimudrāṃ pradarśya । aṣṭapuṣpamarcayet । liṅgāsane oṃ śivāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ haṃ (p.199a, l.1) hāṃ haṃ śivamūrtaye nama iti mūrtau īśānādimūrdhādipādānteṣu oṃ aṃ īśānamūrdhāya namaḥ । āṃ tatpuruṣa(l.2)vaktrāya namaḥ । uṃ ghorahṛdayayāya namaḥ । uṃ(ūṃ) vāmadevaguhyāya namaḥ । vyoṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ ā i u vyomavyāpine (l.3) oṃ iti mūlamantreṇa śivaṃ hṛdi saṃpūjya devyāśca āsanamūrtipañcabrahmāṇi jraṃ jra iṃ kṣaṃ manonmanyai nama ityabhyarcya (l.4) pādyādi datvā । dhūpaṃ pradarśya ghaṇṭāmastreṇa saṃprokṣya । astreṇa saṃpūjya । nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya (l.5) vāmena saṃgṛhya । nābherūrdhvapradeśe'streṇādyante dhvaniseyuktamadhye nādenātāḍya । dhūpapātramastreṇa (p.199b, f.216a, l.1) saṃprokṣya । nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ samskṛtya । sadhūme'gnaye nama ityabhyarcya digbandhanādikaṃ kṛtvā muṣṭimudrāṃ pradṛśyā(śya) । (l.2) śivasya īśānādipañcaghrāṇeṣu sadyojātena svāhāntena sāṅgāya sāvaraṇāya manonmanyā vināyakasya (l.3) dhūpaṃ pradarśyācamanīyaṃ datvā । hastaṃ prakṣālya । saghṛtadīptadīpapātre nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ (l.4) samskṛtya । jvālāyāmagnaye namaḥ ityabhyarcya । digbandhanādikaṃ kṛtvā । śūlamudrāṃ pradarśya । śivasya īśānādipañca(l.5)pañcadaśanetreṇa netramantreṇa svāhāntena sāṅgāya sāvaraṇāya manonmanyā vināyakasya dīpaṃ datvā । ācamanīyaṃ datvā । (p.200a, l.1) hastaṃ prakṣālya । sopadaṃśāntaṃ sarvaṃ pacanālayāt sthalikācchādanasahitaṃ chatracāmaravādyaiḥ saha vardanī dhārā(l.2)nugataṃ śivāgre maṇḍape mārjanaprokṣitapiṣṭarṇairalaṅkṛte maṇḍale tripādyupavinyasya । tatpūrve śuddha(l.3)vastreṇa gadalīpatreṇa vā nānālikāsahitaṃ nānāvidhānaṃ vinyasya । nānāvidhāpūpaiḥ saha lehyādibhiryuktaṃ (l.4) ājyenābhighāritamanaṃ(mannaṃ) ca gālitavāsitapānīyaṃ ca saugandhīśilāsahitatāmbūlaṃ ca vinyasya nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ (l.5) samskṛtya । kavacena pariṣicya । digbandhanādibhiramṛtīkṛtya saṃpūjya । ahamahīkṛtyannasūktaṃ japitvā । (p.200b, f.217a, l.1) āpo janayatācamanaṃ datvā । mṛgyābajalapuṣpaṃ puruṣādīśāntavaktreṣu svāhāntamūlena pañcavāraṃ trivāramekavāraṃ (l.2) vā naivedyaṃ datvā । sāṅgāya sāvaraṇāya manonmanyāsanāyakasya । nivedya lehyādinānāvidhopahārān (l.3) pānīyaṃ ca nivedya । ardhaghaṭikāvasānaṃ śivaṃ bhuñjayanniti śivadhyānasahitamavasāya । uttarāpoja(ca)nāyā(l.4)camanīyaṃ datvā । candanacarcitakarahastodvartanāya prakṣālya । gaṇḍūṣārthamācamanīyaṃ datvā । tāmbūlaṃ nivedyā(l.5)camanīyaṃ dadyāt । paścācchivopabhuktānnamālādikaṃ caṇḍeśāya dadyāt । anyaparivārāṇāṃ snānakāle (p.201a, l.1) snānam । naivedyānte nivedayediti naivedyā(dya)vidhiḥ ॥

(balividhiḥ -)
atha bala(lā)rthaṃ śuddhānnaṃ sthalikācchādanasahita(l.2)māhṛtya । ācāryaḥ śiṣyo vā sāmānyārghyakaraḥ । dvāradeśaṃ prakṣālya dvāraṃ pūjoktavat devān bhūmau madhye dakṣe (l.3) vāme saṃyojya । pādyādikaṃ datvā । bhuvaṅgapataṅgayorgaṇapatisarasvatimahālakṣmī nandimahākālagaṅgā(l.4)yamunotkalapatanavimalasubāhārhaḥ । astrāya dvārapālāya nama iti praṇavahṛtbījacaturthyantai(taiḥ) sva sva nāmabhiḥ (l.5) svāhāpada-prānterjalamiśreṇāntena baliṃ datvā । bihirniga(rga)tya vṛṣā dīpaparivārān pūrvādīśāntaṃ vṛṣāgnimātarā (p.201b, f.218a, l.1) vighnaskandhajyeṣṭhādurgākṣetrapālā iti indrādilokapālān vā prādakṣiṇyena svāhāntena baliṃ datvā mahāpīṭhe (l.2) karṇikāyāṃ avighnaṃ pūrvādidaleṣu āmodāya pramodāya pramukhāya durmukhāya īśānādidalāgreṣu (l.3) vighnakartre rājasāya tāmasāya vedyāmindrādilokapālān namontenābhyarcya madhye nīla(l.4)rūdrasahitaṃ svāhāntena baliṃ datvā yathākrameṇa visṛjediti balividhiḥ ॥

(nityāgnividhiḥ / agnikāryam -)
agnikāryaṃ karomīti (l.5) vijñāpya । labdhānujñaḥ sāmānyārghyakaraḥ prādakṣiṇyena pacanālaye anyāyatanadeśe uttarābhimukhopaviśya । kuṇḍanirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ (p.202a, l.1) samskṛtya । rekhācatuṣṭayaṃ kṛtvā । oṃ hāṃ kalāmayāya kuṇḍāya namaḥ । iti kuṇḍaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ hāṃ vāgī (l.2) vāgīśvarāsanāya namaḥ । iti viṣṭaraṃ datvā । tau dhyātvā-vāhya । saṃpūjya । saṃsṛṣṭaṃ vibhāvya । śuddhapāvakamāhṛtya kravyādaṃśaṃ (l.3) naiṛte saṃtyajya । nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ samskṛtya bhūtāgniṃ saṃhāriṇyā vahnibījena saṃhṛtya । baindavodaravahninā...me(l.4)kīkṛtyā(tya) recakena pradīpa-kalikopamam agnibījena pātrāgnau saṃpuṣpaṃ vinyasya । digbandhanādibhiramṛtīkṛtya (l.5) kuṇḍaṃ bhrāmya mūlenātmasaṃmukhaṃ yonimārgeṇa samūhya paridhānasaṃpratīṃ vibhāvya । śaucācamanādikaṃ datvā । (p.202b, f.219, l.1) oṃ hrūṃ garbhāgnaye namaḥ । garbhādhānādisamskāra(rā)ya sadyādīśānyairabhyarcya । gavyamājyaṃ sṛvaṃ nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya । (l.2) mūlena pañcādyāhutirjuhuyāt । pūrvādidikṣu । pañcadarbhairastreṇa paristīrya । pūrvādidikṣu mekhalāyāṃ brahmāṇaṃ (l.3) śaṅkaraṃ viṣṇumanantaṃ cābhyarcya । agniṃ dhyātvā । śivāgnistvamiti nāmaṃ kṛtvā । ājyapātre sṛvābhyāmājya-mādāya । (l.4) vauṣaḍantena pūrṇāṃ juhuyāt । vāgīśvarau namaskṛtya vasṛjya । śivāgnistvamityājñāṃ srāvya । agniṃ (l.5) hṛtpadme sadāśivaṃ manonmaniṃ pañcāvaraṇasahitamāvāhya । dhūpadīpairabhyarcya । samidhādidravyaiḥ pañcaviṃśati (p.203a, l.1) ṣoḍaśasaṅkhyākaṃ vāmanonmanyāḥ tadarthaṃ pañcāvaraṇadevānāmekaikaṃ hutvā dhūpadīpairabhyarcya pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā (l.2) aghoreṇa prāyaścittārthaṃ yāthālābhaṃ vā hutvā । parāṅmukhārghyaṃ datvā । visijya । brahmādibhyo baliṃ datvā । ........jya (l.3) paristaraṇaṃ dagdhvā bhasma dhārya । vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā । agniṃ visṛjya । bāhye vāyavyādibhyassvāhā । samayabhedibhyaḥ svāhā । itthaṃ (l.4) baliṃ datvā । pādau pāṇi prakṣālyācamya । sakalīkṛtya । sāmānyārghyaṃ punaḥ saṃkalpya । śivāntikaṃ gatvā । arghyodakaṃ (l.5) culukodakenādāya śivāya śivāya pūjāhomaphalaṃ gṛhāṇa svāheti śivasya varadahaste nivedayet । homājyābhāve? (p.203b, caṇḍeśvara......., l.1) homārthaṃ śivasaṃhitājapaṃ kuryāt । iti nityāgnividhiḥ ॥

(caṇḍeśvarapūjā -)
atha caṇḍeśvarapūjā । caṇḍaśasya anantādipadmāntaṃ (l.2) ṣaḍutthāsanaṃ parikalpya । caṇḍāsanāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ caṇḍamurtaye huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । iti mūrtiṃ saṃpūjya । (l.3) 

dhyānam –
raudrāgneyaṃ(ya)prabhaṃ caṇḍaṃ kajjalāmabhayānanam ।
śūlaṃ ṭaṅkadharaṃ raudraṃ caturvaktraṃ caturbhujam ॥ (l.4)
mukhodgīrṇaṃ mahājvaraṃ raktadvādaśalocanam ।
jaṭāmakuṭakhaṇḍendumaṇḍitaṃ maṇikaṅkaṇam ॥
vyālayajñopavītaṃ ca *kamaṇḍalvakṣasūtriṇam ।* (l.5)
śvetapadmāsanāsīnaṃ bhaktibahvātināśanam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । śivanirmālyādi dāpayet । iti (p.204a, l.1) caṇḍeśvarapūjā ॥

	śivāya nityaṃ pavitraṃ dadyāt । dūrvā kuśāgrākṣatabilvapatrasahitaṃ nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya (l.2) daśākṣareṇa vā oṃ hiṃ haṃ hāṃ śivatattvavidyātattvamātmatattvasarvatattvādhipataye śivāya sāṅgāya sāvaraṇāya namaḥ । pavitragrahāṇa (l.3) svāheti śive samarpya । vijñāpayet ॥

(nīrājanavidhiḥ -)
atha nīrājanavidhirucyate – śivāgramaṇḍape mārjana(l.4)prokṣaṇayute tripādisthalikāsahitamadhye vinyasya । tanmadhye ghaṭaṃ nīrājanapātraṃ pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu aṣṭapātrāṇi (l.5) vinyasya । āgneyādikoṇeṣu nimbapatra-sarṣapa-kārpāsabīja-lavaṇāni vinyasya । madhyādidikṣu pātreṣu (p.204b, l.1) karpūragarbhapatitasaghṛtadīpānāropya navadīpapātraṃ cet navaśaktayaḥ । pañcapātreṣu pañcabrahmāṇi । tripātre (l.2) tritattvam । ekapātre śivam । dvaye'śivanītyadhidevatāṃ saṃpūjya । śivāyāṣṭapuṣpādyādikaṃ datvā । ghaṇṭāravasahitaṃ dhūpa(l.3)dīpācamanīyaṃ datvā । hastaṃ prakṣālya । pañcāvartidhūpadīpe pañcabrahmāṇike dīpe sarasvatī nāgadīpe (l.4) anantaḥ bhaje । gaṇeśaṃ vyāghre śāstāraṃ sthūle? । prajāpatiḥ aśve । meghanādaḥ siṃhe । hariḥ śūle । (l.5) astraṃ patra...pe । śriyaṃ vṛṣe । vṛṣaṃ puruṣāmṛge । puruṣaṃ kukkuṭe । skandaṃ kacchape । viṣṇuṃ sṛkdīpe । trimūrtiḥ (p.205a, l.1) bhaktadīpe । śaktidīpe ādiśaktirityadhidevatāṃ saṃpūjya ।

taduktaṃ kiraṇe –
prathamaṃ ketudīpaṃ syāt *dvitīyaṃ nāgadīpakam ।* (l.2)
gajadīpaṃ tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ tu caturthaṃ vyāghradīpakam ॥
pañcamaṃ hamsadīpaṃ syāt ṣaṣṭha(ṣṭhaṃ) vai vājidīpakam । (l.3)
saptamaṃ sihmadīpaṃ syāt śūlamaṣṭamamucyate ॥
navamaṃ patradīpaṃ syāt daśamaṃ vṛṣadīpakam ।
ekādaśaṃ *puṅmṛ(puṃmṛ)gaṃ syāt dvādaśaṃ kukkuṭaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.4)
trayodaśaṃ kacchapaṃ syāt sṛkdīpaṃ syāccaturdaśam ।
pañcādaśaṃ *bhaktadīpaṃ śaktidīpaṃ tu ṣoḍaśa ॥* (l.5)

	pratyekaṃ nirīkṣaṇādikaṃ kṛtvā śūlamudrāṃ pradatvā praṇavākāreṇa (p.205b, l.1) haṃsasvastikapadmākāreṇa vā pratyekaṃ pratyekaṃ paribhrāmya pṛthagācamanaṃ datvā ghaṇṭānādaṃ (l.2) mocayet । pāścādārātrikapātre āvāhanakrameṇa bindunābhivarti?māvāhyā(hya) । digbandhanādi pādyādi dhūpadīpaṃ (l.3) datvā । ghaṭādidīpān pratyekaṃ netramantreṇa paribhrāmya । nimbapātrādīn astreṇa paribhrāmya । pṛthagācamanaṃ (l.4) datvā । paścādvā sitaṃ bhasitaṃ nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ samskṛtya saṃhitayābhimantrya amṛtīkṛtya । svalpam aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ bhasma(l.5)mādāya । śivasyopari astreṇa triḥ paribhrāmya । dīpapātre visṛjya । hastaṃ prakṣālya । aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ (p.206a, l.1) bhasmamādāya śivasya mūrdhādipādānteṣvīśānādimantreṇa samarpya । manonmanyā vināyakasya samarpyā(rpya) svasmin (l.2) hṛnmantreṇa dhārya hastau prakṣālya darpaṇavyajanachatratālavṛntacāmarāṇi nirīkṣaṇādibhiramṛtīkṛtya । darpaṇe śaktiḥ । (l.3) vyajane raviḥ । chatre induḥ । tālavṛnte śaityavāyuḥ । cāmare mātariśvā । evamadhidevatāṃ saṃpūjya । pañca(l.4)brahmamantreṇa pratyekaṃ śivāya sandarśyā(rśya) । samarpya । pṛthagācamanaṃ datvā । śivamāsanamūrtigītavādyaiśca śivaṃ santuṣṭaṃ (l.5) vibhāvya paścāt puṣpāñjaliṃ datvā japaṃ karomīti prārthya । gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa prāsādaṃ daśākṣara(p.206b, f.223a, second half of the folio broken, l.1)maṣṭottaraśataṃ pañcāśat pañcaviṃśatirvā manonmanārthaṃ ............ (l.4)..........kṣatagolakāliṅgeṣu pāśupatamāvāhya pādyādikaṃ datvā .......... (l.5) ..........āsanamantrasamskṛte vastrapuṣpādibhiralaṅkṛte baliberaṃ ca .......... (l.2)..........hanādyupacāraṃ kṛtvā dhūpadīpahavibhiḥ sarveṣāṃ datvā kṣaṇika........(l.3).........yitvā kṣaṇikādi........ śiṣyaśirasi vāhayitvā yānakrameṇa dhva..............(l.4).........ktagaṇikādyairgānādibhiḥ sārdhaṃ prathamaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ yānakrame............(l.5)...........dyādiphalatāmbūladhūpadīpaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak bharatoktanṛttagītavā..............(p.207b, f.224a, stotram, l.1) antaḥ praviśya gopuradvāre pariveṣakramaṃ nītvā sarve prakṣātāṅghrayaḥ devaṃ devīṃ svasthānaṃ prāpaiyya (l.2) śramanirharaṇo-pahārādyairti(rnī)rājyaśuddhanṛtta(ttaṃ) kārayet । tatprākāraḥ ।

	śivāgre nṛttamaṇḍape (l.3) raṅgapūjārthaṃ gomayena maṇḍalamādya tatra raṅgādhipaṃ nandikeśaṃ prātarmadhyāhnasamayeṣu brahmaviṣṇurudrā(l.4)nāvāhya upahārādyai(dyaiḥ) dhūpadīpairabhyarcya śuddhakule jātā lakṣaṇayutā snātā rudrākṣabhasmadhāritā (l.5) kāṣāyavasanā hemakaṇṭhabhūṣaṇabhūṣitā rudragaṇikā puṣpāñjaliṃ dhū(dhṛ)tvā । mandārapuṣpaparikīrṇavicitra(p.208a, l.1)gandhamattālibṛndamadhuradhvanigītanṛttaṃ nānāvidhāṅgakaraṇairabhivartamānā puṣpāñjalirbhavatu me kamalāsanāya । (l.2) bhāvānubandhena saro(rvo)ttamena nṛttena nṛttāṅgasaṃbhavena । 

āmodamattairalibhirvṛkūrcan *puṣpāñjaliṃ syāt puruṣottamāya ।* (l.3)

nānāprasūtanicayairnṛparāṣṭravṛddhyai puṣpāñjalirbhavatu me parameśvarāya ॥ (l.5)

stotrapāṭhaḥ ।
surādhipa namaskṛtya śaśāṅkakṛtakheraram ।
kapālamālinaṃ deva(vaṃ) praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ (p.208b, f.225a, l.1)
kāleśvaraṃ mahākālaṃ kālāñjananivāsinam ।
kālakṛt kālavettāraṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥
sarvakṛt *sarvavettāraṃ sarvajñamaparājitam ।* (l.2)
sa bhūtātmabhūtasthaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 3 ॥
yo'sṛjat sarvabhūtāni *brahmādīṃśca surāsurān ।* (l.3)
tamahaṃ sarvakartāraṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 4 ॥
varaṃ vareṇyaṃ viradaṃ variṣṭaṃ *varavāhanam ।* (l.4)
varamālādharaṃ devaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 5 ॥
prītimatprītidātāraṃ supriyaṃ priya*darśanam ।* (l.5)
umāpriyaṃ sadākāntaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 6 ॥
dharmādharma yaddevaṃ sukhaduḥkhaphalapradam । (p.209a, l.1)
bandhamokṣapradātāraṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 7 ॥
manobuddhyahaṅkāraṃ tanmātre hīyagocaram ।
pradhāna*puruṣeśānaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 8 ॥* (l.2)
yaṃ dhyāyanti mahātmāno munayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ ।
tamokāraparaṃ sūkṣmaṃ *praṇato'smi sadāśivam ॥ 9 ॥* (l.3)
sūkṣmaṃ sarvagataṃ nityaṃ sarvavidyeśvareśvaram ।
sarvatattvāśrayaṃ devaṃ praṇato'smi *sadāśivam ॥ 10 ॥* (l.4)
bhūmirāpo'gniranilakhamātmā candrābhāskarau ।
tasmai paramadevāya praṇato'smi *sadāśivam ॥ 11 ॥* (l.5)
bhaktyā saṃpūjya deveśaṃ yaḥ paṭhatyagratastapam ।
stotrametadasau yāti śāntaṃ tatparamapadam ॥ (p.109b, f.226a, l.1)

godānam –
gavāmaṅgeṣu tiṣṭhanti bhuvanāni caturdaśa ।
yasmāttasmācchivamemasya iha loke paratra cā(ca) । (l.2)
tilabhūtilāḥ pāpaharaṃ nityaṃ viṣṇordehasamudbhavā ॥
tiladānādasaṃhyamema pāpaṃ nāśayate *śivā ।* (l.3)
ājyaṃ tu kāmadhenusamudbhūtaṃ sarvatra tu kṣa(kṣī)rasaṃsthitam ॥
devānāmājyamāhāraṃ ataḥ śāntiṃ *prayaccha me ।* (l.4)

vastram –
śuddhavastrayugaṃ yasmāt ādityasya ca vallabham ।
tasmādasya pradānena ataḥ śāntiṃ prayaccha me ॥ (l.5)

(śāstāmantram -)
śyāmavarṇaśarīrasuyauvanaṃ tuṅgabhṛṅgasahasramu...kundalaṃ yogaveṣṭikapadmasukhāsanaṃ vāmadakṣiṇa(l.2)puṣkalapūrṇayoḥ ।
oṃ hāṃ hariharaśāstāya namaḥ । mūlamantram । oṃ sāṃ sīṃ sūṃ samalavarayūṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ krīṃ (l.3) ca śam । hariharamahāśāstāya namaḥ ।
pūrṇādevīmūlamantram – oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ namaḥ । puṣkalādevīmūlamantram – (l.4) oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ namaḥ । iti mantreṇa pūjayet । hariharaśarīrajanmaṃ maratakamaṇimekhalaiśca – jagati (l.5) tadeva śāstā sakalajagatsiddhamohinīmūrti । oṃ hari-śāstāya namaḥ । haraśāstāya namaḥ । maha(hā)śāstāya namaḥ । (p.219b, f.236, l.1) divyaśāstāya namaḥ । giri-śāstāya namaḥ । mahāśāstāya namaḥ । ārabhyaśāstāya namaḥ । kuṅkumaśāstāya namaḥ । varṇaśāstāya namaḥ । (l.2) śāntaśāstāya namaḥ । śārataśāstāya namaḥ । candrakāntaśāstāya namaḥ । kāntaśāstāya namaḥ । dhavalaśāstāya namaḥ । candrābhi(l.3)śāstāya namaḥ । rāmānanaśāstāya namaḥ । candrakotpalaśāstāya namaḥ । kāntakuṇḍalaśāstāya namaḥ । vīṇādiśāstāya namaḥ । (l.4) postakādiśāstāya namaḥ । akṣasūtraśāstāya namaḥ । vadanaśāstāya namaḥ । vyākhā(khyā)naśāstāya namaḥ । mudrādiśāstāya namaḥ । (l.5) karādiśāstāya namaḥ । vibhrāṇaśāstāya namaḥ । kalādiśāstāya namaḥ । sādākhyaśāstāya namaḥ । hṛdayaśāstāya namaḥ । śāstaṃ tu (p.220a, l.1) vāksiddhaye namaḥ । gajaśāstāya namaḥ । aśvaśāstāya namaḥ । ajādiśāstāya namaḥ । kukkuṭādiśāstāya namaḥ । 

yuddhe vidyāprasaṅge ca *surate krayavikraye ।* (l.2)
āhāre vihāre ca tyaktalajā(jjā) sukhī bhavet ।
haridraṃ lavaṇaṃ dhānyaṃ marīci kramukaṃ gulam ॥
punaḥ pāka*viśeṣaṇa na doṣāya kadācana ।* (l.3)
mūrdhnā lalāṭahṛnnābhijānudvau ṣaṭ ca hastayoḥ ॥
pri(pṛ)ṣṭhaṃ pārśvadvayaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ ṣoḍaśa*sthānamīritam ।* (l.4)
mūrdhni trayaṅgulaṃ caiva lalāṭe dvādaśāṅgulam ॥
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ tu hṛdaye nābhau ekāṅgulaṃ bhavet ।
bāhumūle'ṅgulaṃ ṣaṣṭhyā *bāhumadhyekamaṅgulam ॥* (l.5)
maṇibandhe ca jāhnau ca kaṇṭhe pṛṣṭe(ṣṭhe) ca pārśvake ।
yekamāvāṅgulaṃ proktaṃ tripuṇḍrasya pramāṇakam ॥
ācārya *dakṣiṇe haste madhyamāṅgulimadhyame ।* (l.6)
parvamānaṃ ca dīrghaṃ syānāhaṃ mātrāṅgulaṃ bhavet ॥ (p.220b, f.237a, c.1) 

	kṛttikānakṣatravrataudyāpanakārikaikku(க்கு) vipā(mā) 1, pradhānakumbhaṃ devīkumalu 3, சுத்துக்குமலு 6, ஆக வேதிகை 1. ṣaṭkoṇakuṇḍam 1, (c.2) மண்டபம் சிங்காரம் பண்ணி ācāryā sādhakau upanemakamāka(ga) இருந்து ஸ்நாநம் பண்ண உபவாஸியா-யிருந்து முந்த saṅkalpam பின்பு அந்தகர்தாவு இரட்சாபந்தன(ம்) வலதுகையில் ஸ்த்ரீகளுக்கு இடதுகை ....ண்டுபொழுணு (c.3) யோனாலு வேதிலகயுகும்பங்களு மரித்துக்காளும் ஸாதகரும் ப்ரத்யேகம் ஆசார்யா ஒருதரே பொ(போ)தும் கும்பங்களுக்கு எல்லாம் ப்ரதிமைத்தகடுண்டு ப்ரதாநத்தகடு னாலு-ஜாம்மும் பூசை (p.221a, c.1) பண்ணனு பின்பு கும்பபூசை ஓமம் னாலுசாமமும் பண்ணுவது நெதபடி(நித்யபடி) னாலுசாமமும் பூசை பண்ணி ப்ரபாதே எல்லோரும் ஸ்நாநம் பண்ணி வந்து பூசை பண்ணி பூர்ணாஹுதி பண்ணி கும்ப(c.2)தேவதையை அஹி...வயில ப(வ)ச்சு அத்தை ...மாலிலே யோசி(ஜி)த்து செய்து கர்தாவும் ஸ்நாநம் இந்த கும்பங்களெல்லாம் அபிஷேக(கம்) பண்ணிவிச்சு பின்னும் வாகனவஸ்த்ராலங்காரம் பண்ணிக்கொண்டு வந்திருந்து ஆசார்யருக்கு ப்ரதாநதேவி யிவர்கள் (c.3) வஸ்த்ரம் ப்ரதிமை இதுஹதாநம் பின் புரிதுக்கள் அவாவாகளிது அவாவாகளுக்கு தாநம் ஆஜ்யத-நிரீக்ஷணதானம் பார்பவதி பரமேஶ்வரபூஜை ஆசார்யருக்கு பூரிதானு(னம்) செய்யலு அப்பால (p.221b, f.238a, c.1) ப்ராஹ்மணபோசனு மஹேஶ்வரபூஜை செய்யலு maṇḍapālaṅkāraṃ puṇyāhavācanaṃ kautukabandhanam anujñārthaṃ pratimāśuddhi kumbhapūjai (c.2) karbhāvidhānapūjai homavidhānaṃ naivedyaṃ pratiyāmapūjai(jā) sahasranāmārcanai puṣpāñjali 6 pradakṣiṇaṃ 6 namaskāraṃ prabhāte snānaṃ punaḥ pūjā homaṃ pūrṇāhuti kumbhadevatā-udyāpanaṃ kuḍumbasahita-kartā(c.3)snānaṃ kumbhajalaṃ vastradhāraṇaṃ bhasmadhāraṇaṃ dānaṃ pradi(ti)mādānaṃ bhūridānaṃ pārvatīparameśvarapūjai(jā) brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ māheśvarapūjai(jā) (p.222a blank, p.222b, f.239a, puṣpavidhi, l.1) குடமல்லி, யிருவாச்சி – பிச்சி, வெண்டாமரை, கொங்குதிருவாத்தி, கொன்றை, கொக்குறகு, மந்தாரை, (l.2) முல்லை, மல்லிகை, புன்னை, குருந்துமகிழம் ஞ சணாத்தி கொன்றுத்தி செந்தாழை கந்தாமுடி கெதகை (l.3) குருக்கத்தி நீலோரபலம் குரவகம்பாதிரை மந்தாரை பனினீர்குவளை கருநெய்தல்லரி. ஆடாபவள, (l.4) மல்லிகை, பெருவாகை, செண்பகம், அடுக்கலரி, வெள்ளெருக்கு, அகில், நெல்லி, வன்னி, பொன்னுவாரை, பட்டிகை, யயாந(l.5)தக்கரை நொச்சிப்பூக்கள் அடுக்குமல்லிகை, நந்தியாவட்டம், வெள்ளடுக்கலரி, செவ்வந்தி, விடதிந்தநது, யலவெண்ணொச்சி, (p.223a, l.1) சங்கலரி, மாத(து)ளை, அனங்கன் மல்லிகை, மதத்தை படா கிளுவை, நாயுருவி, பெதரி, மாந்தளிர், மருது, பட்டலரி, சூரியகாந்தம், (l.2) பாரிசாதம், வருக்கை, நவிங்கம், கஸ்தூரி, பருமணமருக்கொழுந்து, படிகசெந்தாமரை, வல்லிகாந்தகம், செம்பருத்தி, மாவிலிங்கை, வில்லு, (l.3) பகாவினா, எள்ளு, மலர்செங்கீரை எனும் பிச்சைபங்குணி, மாசிப்பச்சை-யுடன் மருவுமது, பச்சைதுளசி, தூறுவை, நாவலுர்பலம், (l.4) ஞபிழலின்பு, உம்பாசெழுமலர் கொன்றை, செங்கழுநீர் மந்தாரை, உறுவிபடா விஷ்ணுக்ராந்தி, யொங்குகுங்குமவலரி செவ...ந்த(l.5)மகரந்தை, யுமைபாகாபத பூசை செய்யுத்த மொததம்மான பத்திறாடபங்களை ஓதுமாகமப்படி யிதே. (p.223b, l.1)

gosage? snāpayeddevaṃ saṃśuddhādbhiḥ saśaktikam ॥

vicitramamburaṅgatvā? sarvā kalpaiśca *bhūṣayet ॥* (l.2)
naṭeśāgre viśeṣeṇa sthālibhi(bhiḥ) sthaṇḍilaṃ sudhīḥ ।

utpalasya ca kandāni juhuyāt pūrvavatsudhī ।

campakotpalahrīberasamukhāmbujapāṭalī ।

mālādyai*rbhūṣayeddevaṃ yathāśakti saśaktim ।* (l.6)
taruṇādgaṇaspandhi vicitrāmutpalaprabhām ॥ (p.227b, f.244a, l.1)
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ datvā mantrahavirdadet ।
tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettatrā(tra) mahiṣāṃ cānulepakṛt ॥
sarvātmanādhare pāde *hastayorapyaraktakam ।* (l.2)
hemamājyaṃ tilaṃ vastraṃ dhānyarāśiṃ ca taṇḍulam ॥
ṣaḍaṅgenābhimantryādau devamālokayeddhṛdā । (l.3)
payasvaniṃ savatsā gāṃ dhenu saṃprokṣya badyat(badhyāt) ॥
pacchaccha(pracchanna)paṭamāvarjya nṛtteśaṃ gāṃ pradarśayet ।
hṛdābhimantritāṃ *badhvā dūrvāṃ tasyai pradāpayet ॥* (l.4)
hemamājyaṃ tilaṃ vastraṃ dhānyarāśiṃ ca taṇḍulam ।
śraddhāyuktā savatsāṃ gāṃ *deśikāyāṃ pradāpayet ॥* (l.5)
tasyā(syā)gre muravaṃ yaṣṭvā ghoṣayeśca mahāram ।
gurupūrvaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ nāmā dakṣaṃda?......... ॥ (p.228a, l.1)
.......rathe vā śibikāyāṃ vā tannaṭeśvaram ।
āropya śaktimanyasmin ekasminvāpa niśchalam ॥
sarva(rvā)dyasamāyuktaṃ sarvā*laṅkārasaṃyutam ।* (l.2)
gehaprakārake vāstuprakāre cātha cobhayoḥ ।
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gāyadbhiḥ gaṇikāgaṇaiḥ ॥ (l.3)
paritaḥ vāgrato vāpi nṛtyadbhiḥ gaṇikāgaṇaiḥ ।
gandharvādyaiśca gāyadbhiḥ madbhaktaiḥ sarvamaṅgalaiḥ ॥ (l.4)
hṛṣṭatuṣṭagaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ brahmaghoṣasamanvitam ।
pradīpairvivadhaiśchatraiścāmaro rakṣapiñcākaiḥ ॥ (l.5)
mauktikaiśca vitānādyairdhvajairnānāvidhairapi ।
pradakṣiṇe tataḥ kṛtvā viśedāsthānamaṇḍapam ॥
praṇatā ye tu *nṛtteśaṃ sarvapāpaṃ vyapohya ca ।* (p.228b, f.245a, l.1)
śivasamīpyamāyānti śivenasaha modate ॥
pariveṣaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pādyādīnya*pradāpayet ।* (l.2)
śālitaṇḍulakaṃ kṣuṇṇaṃ kṣīrāmbugulasammitam ॥
pakvaṃ ca kakaṇavat citrarathe sthalirereyat । (l.3)
atyacchakhaṇḍaiḥ saṃproktaṃ nālikeraphalodgataiḥ ॥
kadalīpanasāmbrādyaiḥ saṃvṛttaṃ veṣṭitaṃ tataḥ ।
nivedyā*camanaṃ datvā tāmbūla(laṃ) nivedayet ॥* (l.4)
dīnā viśiṣṭanṛttādyaiḥ vyapohya rajanīmukhe ।
naṭaṃśaṃ snāpayet proktaṃ *pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ ॥* (l.5)
prabhūtahaviṣaṃ datvā tāmbūlaṃ ca pradāpayet ।
dinaviṣṭanṛttādyai(dyaiḥ) vyapohya rajanī....... ॥ (p.229a, l.1)
naṭeśam arcayedagre muravādīni ghoṣayet ।
sāyarakṣāṃ tataḥ kuryāt nṛttagītasamāyutam ॥
prabhūtahaviṣaṃ datvā *tālavādyai(dyaiḥ) praharṣayet ।* (l.2)
pūrvavat samalaṅkatyā(tya) candanāgarukuṅkumaiḥ ॥
karpūragulikairvāpi viṣṭai*rmuktāphalaistu vā ।* (l.3)
limpedalaktakaṃ cāpi pūrvavat suvicitrataḥ ॥
dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā dhūpadīpagaṇo*jjvalam ।* (l.4)
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarva(rvā)todyasamanvitam ॥
praviśedālayaṃ paścāt snapanaṃ kārayet sudhiḥ(dhīḥ) ।
haviṣyantā yajet *paścāt niyataṃ rajanīmukhe ॥* (l.5)
pādyādibhiḥ samabhyarcya bhūṣayet bhūṣaṇārhakaiḥ ।
nṛtyantaṃ tamanusmṛtya muravādimahāravaiḥ ॥ (p.229b, f.246a, ṣaṣṭhivināyakam, l.1)
ghoṣayedatha pādyādi dāpayedarcayettataḥ ।
dhūpadīpāvasāne tu sāyarakṣāṃ samācaret ॥
evaṃ yaḥ kuruto(te) *martyo yaja nityaṃ naṭeśvaram ।* (l.2)
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo'śvabhṛ muktā ivoragām ॥
॥ iti bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ naṭeśvarotsava*paṭalaḥ ॥* (l.3)

(vighneśvarayajanam -)
atha vighneśvarayajanaṃ śruṇudhvaṃ tatprabhañjanā(na) ।
ma(mā)rghaśīrṣamāse tu śatabhiḥ ṣaṣṭisaṃyute ॥
ṣaṣṭhi*yukte viyukte vā taddine ca samācaret ।* (l.4)
vighneśaṃ pūjayeddhīmān gandhapuṣpādinā sahā(ha) ॥
naivedyaṃ dāpayi*tvātha phalabhakṣyādisaṃyutam ।* (l.5)
dhūpadīpaṃ tato datvā nīrāñjanamathācaret ॥
evaṃ nītvā divākāle rātrau pūjā*vasānake ।* (p.230a, l.1)
atha vāstunivedyānte bhakteśvaramathārcayet ॥
tataḥ ārabhya candrāntaṃ bhakteśastotramācaret । (l.2)
tadrātau pūjā vaghana(vighna)rājasya cotsavaṃ tu samācaret ॥
apare divase dhīmān pracedhikā?vasānake ।
athava(vā) kāla*sandhyānte bhaktotsavamathācaret ॥* (l.3)
rātrau ceya(da)rdhayāmānte bhaktotsavamathācaret ।
evaṃ dine dine *kuryātta.......viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.4)
ye(e)vaṃ yaḥ kurute martya(rtyaḥ) sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ।
vighneśayajanaṃ proktam *ārdrāvratavidhiṃ śruṇu ।* (l.5)
॥ iti sūkṣmaśāstre vighneśayajanavidhiraṣṭādaśapaṭalaḥ ॥ (p.230b, f.247a, ārdrotsavam, l.1)

(ārdrotsavam -)
ārdrotsavavidhiṃ vakṣye śruṇudhvaṃ tatprabhañjana ।
maṅgalaṃ caiva saukhyaṃ ca saubhāgyaṃ caiva vaśyakam ॥
dhvajahoma*baliyānayuktamaṅgalameva ca ।* (l.2)
balihomasamāyuktaṃ berayānaṃ samācaret ॥
saukhyametattu kartavyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ ca tataḥ śruṇu । (l.3)
dhvajahomabi(ba)liṃ hutvā berayānaṃ samācaret ॥
hīnamadhyena cotkṛṣṭam utsavaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet । (l.4)
devotsavena kartavyaṃ bhaktānāmutsavaṃ śruṇu ॥
yajamāneśca vā kāle cotsavaṃ tu samācaret ।
dhvajahomabi(ba)liṃ *kṛtvā berayānekasandhiṣu ॥* (l.5)
ārdrādivṛścikāntaṃ ca māsanakṣatrakaṃ na hi ।
deve(vo)tsavena sārdhaṃ syā(syāt) anyaiścaiva na *kārayet ॥* (p.231a, l.1)
ārdramāse tu kartavyaṃ vighnanakṣatrakādiṣu ।
īśanakṣatramanyaiśca bhaktotsavamathācaret ॥
āṣāḍhasyānumāse *tu māsanakṣatramantyuṣi ।* (l.2)
tīrthamādau tu niścitya kriyāpūrvaṃ samācaret ॥
navāhe caiva saptāhe pañcāhe tryahe'pi vā । (l.3)
ekāhaṃ ekasandhiścet utsavaṃ tu samācaret ॥
taddinātpūrvarātrau tu rakṣābandhana(naṃ) samācaret ।
vāstuśuddhi(ddhiṃ)*tataḥ kṛtvā gavyena prokṣya bhūtale ॥* (l.4)
snānamaṇḍapālaṅkṛtya na(nā)nācitravicitritam ।
prokṣayetpuruṣeṇaiva *puṇyāhaṃ ca tataḥ kuru ॥* (l.5)
yānamūrtiṃ ca dāhvāne? harirāsakasaṃsthitam ।
āsanaṃ mūrtimāvāhyā(hya) snānadravyādisāda(dha)yet ॥
abhyaṅga*piṣṭacūrṇaṃ ca tintriṇīdarbhabhasmakam ।* (p.231b, f.248a, l.1)
pañacagavyābhiṣekaṃ tu nālīphalagulairyutam ॥
dadhikṣīreṣu madhunā kadalīphala(laṃ) *mardayet ।* (l.2)
śuddhatoyābhiṣekaṃ tu dravyanasnā(snānā)ntarāntare ॥
pañcakumbhābhiṣekaṃ tu pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet ।
vastrakhaṇḍena sammārjya *citravistrāmbarairyutam ॥* (l.3)
sṛklepāne ca bhūṣitvā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ।
rudravājāvasāne tu bhaktānāmutsavaṃ *nayet ॥* (l.4)

(bhaktotsavam -)
madhyānnapūjanānte tu tadante yānamācaret ।
rathe vā vāhane vāpi gaścake śibike'pi vā ॥
yānayāgasya kāle tu *dīpajvālā samāyutam ।* (l.5)
chatracāmarapiñchādyaisstotramaṅgalavācakaiḥ ॥
vedadhvanisamāyuktaṃ bhaktastotrapurāṇakam । (p.232a, l.1)
sarva(rvā)todyasamāyuktaṃ sarvabhaktena sārdhakam ॥
śanaiḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā praviśedālayaṃ prati ।
yātrārthasnapanaṃ paścāt bhūripānnaṃ(naṃ)? *nivedayet ॥* (l.2)
bhakṣyāpūpādikaṃ datvā tāmbūlaṃ tu nivedayet ।
tīrtharātradivase cūrṇotsavaṃ samācaret ॥
nadītaṭākatīre *vā tīrthakarmaṃ samācaret ।* (l.3)
ācāryaṃ pūjayetpūrvaṃ vastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ ॥
bhaktānāṃ paricārāṇāṃ vastradānaṃ tu *dāpayet ।* (l.4)
yajamānena kartavya(vyaṃ) dīrghamāyuśriyaṃ bhavet ॥
putrapautrasamṛddhyartham ante sāyujya tatpadam ।
bhaktānāmutsavaṃ *proktam ārdrāvratavidhiṃ śruṇu ॥* (l.5)

(ārdrāvratavidhiḥ -)
ārdrāvratavidhiṃ vakṣye śriṇudhvaṃ tatprabhañjana ।
āyuśrīpañcalabhate putrapautravivardhanam ॥ (p.232b, f.249a, l.1)
mārgaśīrṣamāse tu ārdrāyāṃ vratamācaret ।
ārdrāyāṃ pauṣasaṃyuktaṃ yuktamārdrāntameva ca ॥
viśeṣeṇa tu ārdrāyām uttamaṃ tu *pravakṣyate ।* (l.2)
ārdrāyām aṣṭanāḍī vā anyarkṣe vratamācaret ॥
aṣṭanāḍī vinā ri(ṛ)kṣaṃ pūrvāhe(hṇe) vratamācaret ।
dvādaśaṃ *daśahastaṃ vā navahastāṣṭahastakau ॥* (l.3)
ṣośa(ḍaśa)stambhasaṃyuktaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam ।
paṅktitrayasamāyuktaṃ paṅktimānamayogamayam ॥ (l.4)
vitānadhvajasaṃyuktaṃ muktādāmairalaṅkṛtam ।
stambhaveṣṭanasaṃyuktaṃ yugatoraṇasaṃyutam ॥
nānālīlāsamāyuktaṃ maṇḍapaṃ *parikalpayet ।* (l.5)
madhyame vedikāṃ kuryāt aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ ॥
tadalthe sthaṇḍilairbhūṣya tadarthaiśca tilairapi । (p.233a, l.1)
ācāryaḥ suprasannātmaṃ sakalīkṛtavigraham ॥
deveśaṃ pūjayet paścāt gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt ।
prabhūtā(ta)haviṣaṃ dadyāt vijñāpya vratamuttamam ॥ (l.2)
aṅkurāṇyarpayet paścāt puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ ।
snapanaṃ kārayettatrā(tra) yātra(trā)homasamanvitam ॥
vahnimadhye viśeṣeṇa *bhogaśaktiṃ prapūjayet ।* (l.3)
vāmādīnā(na)ṣṭaśaktīnāṃ snanātnādyaiḥ? samarcayet ॥
samit hṛdayamantreṇa kavacena ghṛtaṃ *hunet ।* (l.4)
caruṃ hutvā śikhenaiva vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyāttataḥ ॥
jayādirabhyādhānāṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛcca kramāddhunet । (l.5)
garbhagehaṃ praviśyātha bhogaśaktiṃ samarcayet ॥
snapanādyai(dyaiḥ) viśeṣeṇa sthāpayet parameśvarīm ।
navavastreṇa *saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) gandhapuṣpādibhiryajet ॥* (p.233b, f.250a, l.1)
maṇḍapeṣviṣṭadeśe vā sthaṇḍilaṃ kārayet tataḥ ।
aṣṭadroṇaistadardhairvā *āḍakairdroṇaśālibhim(bhiḥ) ॥* (l.2)
tadardhaistaṇḍulairbhūṣya tadardhaiśca tilairapi ।
darbhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ paristīrya tasyārdhe *lavaṇena tu ॥* (l.3)
ghāritrayaṃ tadardhaṃ vā droṇaṃ vāpi tadardhataḥ ।
padmamaṣṭadalaṃ kuryāt kesarāḍhyā sakarṇikam ॥
pañca*cūrṇairalaṅkṛtya kalpayet siṃhamāsanam ।* (l.4)
tasyordhve vastramāstīrya niśi cūrṇena pūrayet ॥
bhogaśaktiṃ samādāya ālayādi*pradakṣiṇam ।* (l.5)
nidhāya śītavādyādi dhūpadīpādisevitam ॥
brahmaghoṣasamāyuktaṃ sarvālaṅkāraśobhitam ।
siṃhāsane samaṃ *ropya kautukaṃ bandhayet sudhīḥ ॥* (p.234a, l.1)
sthalikāṃ taṇḍulaiḥ pūrya sasūtra(traṃ) madhyame nyaset ।
bhasmapātreṇa saṃyuktaṃ śaṅkhadhvanisamāyutam ॥ (l.2)
nānāvādyasamāyuktaṃ nānāstrīparisevitam ।
paricārakamāhūya soṣṇīṣaṃ sottarīyakam ॥
śirasā vāhayitvā tu svasti*sūktamanujñapan ।* (l.3)
dhāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
devyāgre sthāpayedva(dvi)dvān puṇyāhaṃ tatra vācayet । (l.4)
rakṣāsūtraṃ japadvidvān sāvitriyādikaṃ kramāt ॥
devīṃ saṃprokṣya vāmena oṃ namo brahmaṇena tu । (l.5)
dhūpaṃ vastrādimantreṇa vastraṃ caivottarīyam ॥
yāste rāketi mantreṇa gandhaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ ।
āyaṇakena mantreṇa *rakṣāsūtraṃ ca bandhayet ॥* (p.234b, f.251a, l.1)
bṛhassāmeti mantreṇa bhasmitaṃ dāpayettataḥ ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjyā(jya) pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet ॥
kadalīphala*saṃyuktam apūpaṃ nālikerakam ।* (l.2)
tāmbūlaṃ dāpayedvidvān gaurīgāyatrimantri(ntra)taḥ ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ viśeṣeṇa śūrīṃ *santoṣayet kuru ।* (l.3)
tataḥ prāsādaparitaḥ pūrvādīśānamaṇḍape ॥
niśicūrṇaṃ yā devīṃ prāsādāgrasya maṇḍape ।
vijayā ca *sakṛt cūrṇaṃ āgneyyabhavane nyaset ॥* (l.4)
jayantīmutsavaṃ yuktaṃ dakṣiṇe bhavate budhaḥ ।
parājitā ca lavaṇaṃ nairi(ṛ)tyāṃ diśi vinyaset ॥ (l.5)
dūrva(rvā)kṣatena saṃyuktaṃ rambhāyāṃ varuṇe gṛhe ।
vāyavyāṃ nilayoścaiva tintriṇīṃ ca tilottamaḥ ॥
bhasmitena tu *gaṅgāyām uttare bhavena budhaḥ ।* (p.235a, l.1)
sarasvatīṃ yajedīśe dūrvākṣatasamanvitam ॥
vardhanīṃ droṇapūrṇāṃ sūtravastreṇa veṣṭitam । (l.2)
vidhāya kūrcasaṃyuktaṃ nālikeraphalānvitam ॥
pūjayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ tattanmantreṇa buddhimān ।
paścānnityāgnisandhyāśeṣaṃ *samācaret ॥* (l.3)
tatkāle cātmaśuddhiṃ syā(syāt) pūjakaḥ saṃprayojayet ।
ārādhya devadeśaṃ haviṣyantaṃ śivārcanam ॥
devīṃ *saṃpūjya vidhivat gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt ।* (l.4)
naivedyaṃ dāpayedvidvān upadaṃśakasaṃyutam ॥
gulakhaṇḍasamāyuktam ājyayuktaṃ *dadhiplutam ।* (l.5)
nivedayettu gāyatryāḥ pānīyaṃ dāpayettataḥ ॥
jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantīśca parājitaḥ ।
rambhā tilottamā *gaṅgā bhāratīṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ॥* (p.235b, f.252a, l.1)
pūrvādīśānaparyantam arcayettāṃ krameṇa tu ।
tataḥ sandhyāvasāne tu devīṃ saṃsnāpya deśikaḥ ॥ (l.2)
sarvavastrairalaṅkṛtya sarvapuṣpopaśobhitam ।
sarvābharaṇairbhūṣya kadalīphalasaṃyutam ॥
dhūpaṃ dadyā*dviśeṣeṇa nīrājanamathācaret ।* (l.3)
rathe vā śibikāyāṃ vā gaplake? yānamācaret ॥
tālakāhalajhallarīmardalai*praṇavādibhiḥ anyaiḥ ।* (l.4)
nānāvidhairvādyai(dyaiḥ) bṛhadghoṣasamanvitam ॥
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt nānābhaktajanairyutam ।
nānā*cakrasamāyuktaṃ nānādhvajaiśca śobhitam ॥* (l.5)
mayūravyajanādīnāṃ cāmarādibhi(bhiḥ) sevitam ।
devīṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ nītvā śaṅkhadhvani*samanvitam ॥* (p.236a, l.1)
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ grahaṃ pratigrahaṃ prati ।
sthitvā sthitvā jayādinā yāṣvārakṣāntikaṃ kramāt ॥
saṃdṛśya dravyasaṃbhārānabhyarcya hṛdayena tu । (l.2)
evaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāttīrthasnānaṃ samāviśet ॥
tīre samatalaṃ kṛtvā prapāṃ kṛtvātisundaram ।
taraṅgaṃ vaṭṭavedāgai? vitānādyairviṣayet ॥ (l.3)
snapanaṃ kārayettatra jalakrīḍāṃ samācaret ।
gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcyā(rcya) siddhānnā parikalpayet ॥
praviśenmandiraṃ *paścā(ścāt) sarvāro(to)dyasamāyutam ।* (l.4)
evaṃ dine dine kuryāt ārdrāntaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ ॥
ārdrāvratadinātpūrve somanakṣatrake niśi । (l.5)
tīrthārthamaṅkuraṃ kuryāt kautukaṃ bandhayettataḥ ॥
snāpayeddevadeveśīṃ dvitīyadivase budhaḥ ।
vastrairābharaṇairmālyai *alaṅkṛtya viśeṣataḥ ॥* (p.236b, f.253, l.1)
pūrvairyānakrameṇaiva pradakṣiṇamathācaret ।
tīrthatīraṃ samāsādyā(dya) kutvā cūrṇotsavaṃ budhaḥ ॥ (l.2)
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu tīrthakarmaṃ samācaret ।
jale sūtraṃ visṛjyātha tataḥ snānaṃ samācaret ॥
devyāyā saha *yatkāle snānaṃ kurvīta yo naraḥ ।* (l.3)
saṃvatsarakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ snānenaiva vinaśyati ॥
jalāduttīrya deveśaṃ snāpayet *snapanādikramāt ।* (l.4)
vastrairāveṣṭya tāṃ devīmalaṅkṛtya viśeṣataḥ ॥
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ śīghraṃ gate niketane ।
prāsādasya *purobhāge kalpitodyānamadhyame ॥* (l.5)
maṇḍapaṃ kārayettatra ḍolasyārohaṇārthakam ।
vitānaiśca dhvajaiścaiva *karāgrairgātraveṣṭanaiḥ ॥* (p.237a, l.1)
muktā sṛk muktapuṣpaiśca puṣpadāmaiḥ prala.....sayet? ।
kramukaiścaiva tāmbūlai(laiḥ) nālikeraphalaiḥ *sahā(ha) ।* (l.2)

īśāne – (p.243b, f.260a, l.1)
pītaṃ pīnaṃ caturbāhuṃ brāhma(brahmā)ṇaṃ caturānanam ।
haṃsayānaṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ daṇḍākṣasṛkkamaṇḍulu(ṇḍala)m ॥  	iti dhyātvā । brahmāṇaṃ saṃpūjya । 

nairi(ṛ)te – 
hamsasthaṃ tuntilaṃ gauraṃ tanmaśrū caturānanam ।
dadhānaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme daṇḍa....sṛkkamaṇḍulu(ṇḍala)m ॥  iti dhyātvā । 
	oṃ hāṃ (l.3) vāstvā(stva)dhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । 

paścime – oṃ hāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ ।
dvārasya sammukhīṃ śyāmāṃ puṣparatnaukha(gha)varṣa(l.4)ṇīm ।
dhyātvāvāhya yajedvidvān smarenmaṇḍaparūpiṇīm ॥

	iti lakṣmīṃ saṃpūjya । tataḥ śivakumbhavardhanyau calāsanaṃ (l.5) saṃpūjayet । tadyathā kumbhādhaḥ – oṃ hāṃ vṛṣabhāsanāya namaḥ । iti calāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । kumbhe mūrtiṃ vinyasya(syā)vāhyā(hya) śivaṃ saṃpūjayet । (l.6) vardhanyā vyālāsanam । iti calāsanaṃ kṛtvā । pāśupadāstramūrtaye namaḥ । iti mūrtiṃ vinyasyā(sya) ।

dhyāyan -
caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca ślakṣṇa*piṅgordhvakeśadhṛk ।* (p.244a, l.1)
akṣābhavakaraṃ dakṣe vāme pāśadharānvitam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ ślīṃ paśūṃ phaṭ pāśupadāstrāya huṃ (l.2) phaṇṇamaḥ । ityāvāhyā(hya) । pādyādikaṃ datvā । oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ ślīṃ śirase huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ phaṃ śikhāyai huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । (l.3) oṃ śūṃ kavacāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ huṃ netrebhyo huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ huṃ phaṭ astrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । iti sāṅgaṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । (l.4) ....... lokapālān viṣṇvantān āvāhanāyudhasaṃyuktān dhyātvā -
prācyādidikṣu saṃpūjayet । oṃ hām indrāya namaḥ । (l.5)

aira(rā)vataṃ gajārūḍhaṃ svarṇavarṇaṃ kirīṭinam ।
sahasranayanaṃ cakraṃ vajrapāṇiṃ vibhāvayet ॥  	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hām indrāya namaḥ । 

saptārcitaṃ ca vibhrāṇam *akṣamālā kamaṇḍalum ।* (l.6)
jvālāmālākulaṃ raktaṃ śaktihastaṃ ca gāsanam? ॥  iti vibhāvya । agnayet ।

kṛtāntaṃ mahiṣārūḍhaṃ daṇḍahastaṃ bhayānakam । (p.244b, f.261, l.1)
kālapāśadharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhyāyeddakṣiṇadikpatim ॥   iti smaran । oṃ hāṃ yamāya namaḥ ।

raktanetraṃ śavārūḍhaṃ nīlotpaladalaprabham । (l.2)
kṛpāṇapāṇimasraukhaṃ(ghaṃ) bibuntaṃ rākṣaseśvaram ॥  	iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ niru(ṛṛ)taye namaḥ ।

nāgapāśadharaṃ hṛṣṭaṃ raktaukha(gha)dyutivigraham ।
śaśāntadhavalaṃ *dhyāye(yet) varaṇa(ṇaṃ) makarāsanam ॥* (l.3)  	oṃ hāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ ।

āpīnaṃ haritaśchāyāṃ viloladhvajadhāriṇam ।
prāṇabhūtaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ *hariṇasthaṃ samīraṇam ॥* (l.4)  iti dhyān(dhyāyan) । oṃ hāṃ vāyavye namaḥ ।

kuberaṃ manujāsīnaṃ sagarvaṃ kharvavigraham ।
svarṇacchāyāṃ gadāhastamuttarāśa*patiṃ smaran ॥* (l.5)  iti vibhāvyā(vya) । oṃ hāṃ kuberāya namaḥ । 

īśānaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ triśūlaṃ vyāladhāriṇam ।
śaracchandrāvadānaṃ ca candramaulīṃ *trilocanam ॥* (l.6)  evaṃ dhyātvā । oṃ hām īśānāya namaḥ ।

īśāne pīnaṃ pītaṃ caturbāhuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ caturānanam ।
haṃsayānaṃ ca vibhrāṇaṃ daṇḍākṣaṃ *sṛkkamalam ॥* (p.245a, l.1)  	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । 

nairi(ṛ)te – 
anantaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ phaṇaḥ śayyoparisthitam ।
vidyunnāgapratīkāśaṃ kūrmakūḍaṃ *kṛtāñjalim ॥* (l.2)  iti dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । 

bho bho śakṛttvayāssyadiśi vighnapraśāntaye ।
sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāvatsedhaṃ *śivājñayā ॥* (l.3)

	ityanena krameṇa sarvān lokapālān śivājñāṃ śrāvayet । kumbhāgragāmīmakhaṇḍitadhārāṃ vardhanīmastra(l.4)durgamanusmaran । mūlena bhrāmya । pūrvakumbhaṃ paścāt vardhanīṃ ca saṃsthāpya । sthirāsane kumbhavardhanyāṃ sthāpya । bhagalīṃ samāyogaṃ (l.5) vidhāya । kumbhasthe śive tatveśvaramūrtimūrtīśvarān vinyasya । tataḥ layāṅgaṃ bhogāṅgaṃ japāntaṃ saṃpūjya । nijadhāgaṃ jagandharaṃ (l.6) rakṣa bhaktānukampayeti vijñāpya । jñānakhaḍkhaṃ samarpya nirodhārghyaṃ saṃsthāpya । nivedya । vardhanyāsanavikirorthāstrairyāgagṛhaṃ (p.245b, f.262a, l.1) prasāritā śeṣavighnamaṇḍapaṃ vibhāvya । vāyavye – vighnanāyakamabhyarcayet ।

yat sureśādhvaro'smābhi(bhiḥ) pārśvānugrahahetave । (l.2)
pravartitaprayāntavyamavighnaṃ tava sannidhau ॥  iti vijñāpya । 

aiśānyāṃ gurumabhyarcya jaḍāmakuṭaśobhāḍhyam ।
bhasmapāṇḍaravigrahaṃ yogapaṭṭa*yutā brahmasūtra nābhisthalopari ॥* (l.3)
kṛtayogāghya(khya)mudrāṃ ca dhyātvā śāntaṃ guruṃ param ।

	evaṃ dhyātvābhyarcya । kumbhasthe śive labdhānujño(l.4)'rghyapātramādāya kuṇḍāntikaṃ kṛtvā । kuṇḍanābhiṃ punaḥ kurvan saumyāsyopaviśyā(śya) tatrānāmikāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ nyastamūlena (l.5) cakṣuṣā kuṇḍasya nirīkṣaṇaṃ śivamantreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ tāḍanaikuśaiḥ huṃ phaḍantāstreṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ vauṣaḍantakavacena khananāva(l.6)kīraṇapūraṇasamīkaraṇasevanakuṭṭanasammārjanasamālepanāni khaṭ(ḍ)gena kuśena vidhāya । kuṇḍamadhye pūrvā yāmyottarapaścimeṣu (p.246a, l.1) śāntyātītādikalāmantrān nyastvā kalāmayaṃ parikalpya trisūtryāmastreṇāveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) । oṃ hāṃ kalāmayāya kuṇḍāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcyā(rcya) । (l.2) prāgāyatamudagāyataṃ vā tiryak rekhāyā saha rekhācutuṣṭayan । ubhayatra triśūlākṛtinā kuśatrayeṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ pūrvottarāgrakuśadvayena (l.3) catuṣpathānyāsam astreṇa kuṇḍasyābhyantare samantādutthitaiḥ kuśaiḥ rakṣākapāṭaṃ kavacena vidhāya madhye kūrcarūpaṃ (l.4) viṣṭaram oṃ hāṃ vāgīśvarī vāgīśvarāya namaḥ । iti datvā । 

tasmin -
devīmāvāhayedvidvān vidhivat viśvamātaram ।
śyāmāṃ ri(ṛ)tumatīṃ dhyātvā *somālyabhrūṃ praṇāṃ* (l.5)
prasannavadanāṃ padmapatrābhanayanatrayām ।

	oṃ hāṃ vāgīśvaryai namaḥ । iti vāgīśvarīṃ tadvāgīśvaraṃ ca oṃ hāṃ vāgīśvarāya (l.6) namaḥ । iti gandhādibhirabhyarcya । alaṅkṛtya । araṇe sūryakānta-dvijagṛhāt svagṛhāt vahniṃ mṛtpātre tāmbrapātre vā nidhāya । āgneyyā aiśānyā(nyāṃ) (p.246b, f.263a, l.1) nairi(ṛ)tyāṃ vā nidhāya । agniśakalaṃ kuryādārthamastreṇa nairi(ṛ)te nissārya vīkṣaṇādibhiścaturbhiḥ samskāraiḥ viśodhya oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ (l.2) vanhi(hni)mūrtaye namaḥ । iti bhūtāgniṃ pūrakeṇa gṛhītvā hṛdayenā(na) kumbhakenābhyagninā bindvagninā saṃyojyā(jya) recakena nissārya । oṃ hrūṃ vanhi(hni)(l.3)caitanyāya namaḥ । iti pātrasthe agnau vinyasya । saṃhitayā saṃpūjyā(jya) । saṃrakṣyāvakuṇḍyā(ṇṭhya) । prāsādena vauṣaḍantenā(na) (l.4) dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtyādāya kuṇḍasyopari pradakṣiṇaṃ triḥ paribhrāmya । śivabījamiti dhyātvā । pitarāpīśadiggataśirasau (l.5) dhyāyan vāgīśvarīṃ garbhanāḍyāṃ vāgīśvareṇa kṣipyamāṇaṃ vibhāvya । bhūmiṣṭha-jānumūlenātmanaḥ sammukhaṃ kṣipet । tato (l.6) nābhideśe samūhya paridhānasya saṃpratīṃ vibhāvya । śaucācamanārthaṃ toyabinduṃ hṛdā datvā । kavacenāsadigdhanairāccādyā(p.247a, l.1)streṇa prajvālyā(lya) । oṃ hrūṃ garbhāgnaye namaḥ । iti garbhāgniṃ saṃpūjyā(jya) । garbharakṣārtham astrajaptasaptadarbhakaṅkaṇaṃ devīhaste badhvā । garbha(rbhā)dhānāya (l.2) sadyojātenābhyarcyā((rcya) । hṛdayenāhutitrayaṃ tirairdatvā । pumsavanārthaṃ tri(tṛ)tīye māsi vāmadevenābhyarcya । śirasāhutitrayaṃ datvā । jalabinduṃ nikṣipya । (l.3) sīmantonnayananimittaṃ ṣaṣṭhe māsi aghoreṇā-bhyarcya । śikhāya(yā)hutitrayaṃ datvā । śikhāyai vaktrāṅgakalpanāni niṣkṛtiṃ ca vidhāya । jananārthaṃ (l.4) daśame māsi puruṣeṇa(ṇā)-bhyarcyā(rcya) । kavacena tisrāhutirdatvā । jāte garbhamalāpahasnānārthaṃ vahniṃ darbhādyaiḥ saṃdhukṣya । devīhasta(l.5)darśakaṅkaṇamunmucya svarṇa-bandhanaṃ devyākṛtīṃ dhyātvā । hṛdābhyarcyā(rcya) sadyaḥ sūtakanivṛttaye kuṇḍamastrāmbunā saṃsicyā(cya) darbheṇa tāḍyāstreṇa (l.6) tāḍyā(ḍya) । kavacenābhyukṣya । vanhirakṣārthaṃ pūrvādimekhalāsyuttarapūrvāgrān darbhānastreṇāstīrya । teṣu paridhiviṣṭarān saṃsthāpya । (p.247b, f.264a, l.1) vakrāṇāṃ lālāpanodāyāgneḥ puṣṭaye ca mūlāgrayoḥ ghṛtāktāḥ pañcasamidhakhaṭge(ḍge)na hutvā । paridhiṣu pūrvapaścimadakṣiṇo (l.2)ttarādidikṣu oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ hām īśvarāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcyā(rcya) । viṣṭareṣu oṃ hāṃ (l.3) indrāya namaḥ । oṃ hām īśānāya namaḥ । aiśānyāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । nairi(ṛ)tyāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā-rghyayitvā । vighna(l.4)saṃkhātaṃ nivāryā(rya) । bālakaṃ pālayiśyatha iti śivājñāṃ śrāvayet । tataḥ sṛk sṛvau ca sakuśādūrdhvavadanā-dhomukhau (l.5) gri(gṛ)hītvāstreṇa saṃprokṣya kavacenāvakuṇḍyābhyukṣya agnau pratāpyā(pya) । bhrāmyā(mya) । kuśāgreṇa tadagraṃ saṃspṛśyā(śya) । punaḥ paribhrāmya madhye (p.248a, l.1) madhyaṃ saṃspṛśyā(śya) । punaḥ paribhrāmya । mūlena mūlaṃ saṃspṛśya । tayoḥ kuśa-spṛṣṭapradeśeṣu śivavidyātmākhyāṃ tattvatrayaṃ (l.2) rudraviṣṇubrahmāhmākhyādhipasamyutakramāt oṃ huṃ hīṃ hāṃ śambarai vinyasya । sruciśaktiṃ śraveśaṃ śambhuṃ nyastvā varmaṇātra sūtriveṣṭita(l.3)grīvātmanaḥ sarvadik...ge kuśopari mūlena saṃsthāpya । hṛdā saṃpūjayet । atha gavyamājyaṃ samādāya vīkṣaṇādibhirviśodhya (l.4) svadehaṃ brahmamayaṃ vicintyādāya kuṇḍasyordhvaṃ tridhā vaktryāgnigocaret dāpayitvā āgneyyāṃ saṃsthāpya (l.5) darbhāgreṇa ghṛtabindumādāya oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe svāheti hutvā । punaḥ viṣṇumayaṃ svadehaṃ dhyātvā aiśānyāma-dhiśritya kuśāgreṇājyabinduṃ (p.248b, f.265a, l.1) viṣṇave svāheti hutvā । punaḥ svamūrtiṃ rudramayīṃ dhyātvā । nābhau dhṛtvā rudrāya svāhetītyājyabinduṃ kuśāgreṇa juhuyāt (l.2) ityudvāsya prādeśamātraṃ darbhāgradvayanirmita-madhyagranthipavitrakam aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ gṛhītvā astreṇājyamagniṃ (l.3) tridhāplāvayet । tatvātmasammukhaṃ tridhā saṃplavanaṃ hṛdā kṛtvā । tatpavitragranthima(mu)nmucyāgnau kṣiptvā hṛdābhimantritadagdhadarbhonmukhaṃ (l.4) astreṇa ghṛtena kṣiptvā pavitrīkṛtyā(tya) । tadapyagnau vastreṇa kṣipet । dīptenānyena darbheṇa kavacena nirājyāgnau kṣiptvā (l.5) pavitrīkṛtyā(tya) tadapyagnau astreṇa kṣipet । dīptenāstreṇa sandīpya tadapyunmukhaṃ vahnau kṣipet । tataḥ (p.249a, l.1) prādeśamātraṃ darbhāgradvayanirmitaṃ granthi kuśadvayaṃ praṇavena ghṛtamadhye nyastvā । dakṣiṇavāmakrameṇa śuklakṛṣṇapakṣa-dvayaṃ (l.2) vibhāvyākṣiṇavāmamadhyabhāgeṣu piṅgaleḍāsuṣumnānāḍītrayaṃ ca saṃkalpya । savyabhagāt sraveṇājyamādāya oṃ hāṃ (l.3) agnaye svāhetyagniṃ dakṣiṇanetre vāmabhāgādājyamādāya oṃ hāṃ somāya svāhā । vāmanetramadhyamādāya oṃ hām agnaye (l.4) somāgnībhyāṃ svāhā । agnimadhyanetreṣu oṃ hāṃ agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhetyāgnivaktre ca svāheti kiñciddhṛtyā(tya) (l.5) śeṣamājyaṃ tadājyabhāgeṣu heti kṣipet । śuklapakṣe kṛṣṇapakṣe oṃ hāṃ somāya svāhā । oṃ hāṃ agnaye svāhā । (p.249b, f.266a, l.1) ...........  bhyāṃ svāhā । oṃ hām agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhā । agnervāmadakṣiṇamadhyanetreṣu vaktre ca pūrvavaddhṛtvājyaṃ (l.2) saṃhitayābhimantrya saṃrakṣyāvakuṇṭhyā(ṭhya) mūlena dhenumudrayā amṛtīkṛtyā(tya) । mūlena saṃpūjāyā(jya) । hṛdayenājyabindukṣepādanyājya(l.3)-śodhanaṃ kuryāt । tadanu । oṃ hāṃ sadyojātāya svāhā । oṃ hīṃ vāmadevāya svāhā । oṃ hum aghorāya svāhā । oṃ heṃ (l.4) tatpuruṣāya svāhā । oṃ hom īśānāya svāhā । ityekaikayā ghṛtā hutvā । agnivaktrāṇāmāghāram । oṃ haṃ hiṃ sadyojāta(l.5)vāmadevābhyāṃ svāhā । oṃ hiṃ huṃ vāmadevaaghorābhyāṃ svāhā । oṃ huṃ hem aghoratatpuruṣābhyāṃ svāhā । oṃ heṃ (p.250a, l.1) hoṃ tatpuruṣeśānābhyāṃ svāhā । iti vaktrasandhānam । oṃ haṃ hiṃ huṃ heṃ hoṃ sadyojātavāmadevāaghora(l.2)tatpuruṣeśānābhyāṃ svāhā । ityagnino vāyuṃ gatayanairi(ṛ)tamīśānaṃ gatayā ghṛtāhutyā vaktraikīkaraṇaṃ vidadhyāt । tato (l.3) nāmakaraṇārtham īśenābhyarcyāstreṇāhṛtitrayaṃ datvā । oṃ hāṃ śivāgnistvaṃ hutāśaneti nāmakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā hṛdayena (l.4) vāgīśīvāgīśāmabhyarcyā(rcya) । visṛjya । kuṇḍamānaṃ yathābhilāṣitaṃ vaktraṃ parikalpya madhyajihvayā ...ḍāntasamskārapūraṇā ta....vauṣaḍantamūlena pūrṇāṃ vidadhyāt । tadyathā sarpiṣā sravamāpūrya puṣkaropavyadhomukhaṃ sṛcā kṛtvā tadagre (p.250b, l.1) puṣpayā vāmadakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ śaṅkhavat saṃsthābhyāṃ punaḥ savyena saṃlagnau tā vupādāya sampādasamṛddhāya ūrdhvakāyo nābhyāṃ (l.2) tiryagvidhāya sṛgadaśratadṛṣṭi(ṣṭiḥ) vauṣaḍantamūlena mantramuccaran vāmastanāntaṃ sṛvamūlamānīyānudvagnau yavapramāṇadhārayā (l.3) samastamājyamagnau kṣipet । iti pūrṇāṃ vidhāyopaviśyātha carvārthamagniṃ vibhajya sthānāntare saṃrakṣya śivāgnirāpyāya(l.4)mānāya akārādyai(dyaiḥ) svarai(raiḥ) ṣoḍaśāṅgulānidhmān palāśān juhuyāt ।

sitapadmāsanāsīnaṃ pañcavaktraṃ caturbhujam ।
lambakūrcaṃ *brahmamū....jaḍāmaṇḍitaśekharam ॥* (l.5)
sṛkkamaṇḍaludaṇḍākṣaṃ mālāvyagrakarāmbujam । 

yadvā –
saraktatanutryakṣaśivāyudhabhujānvitam । (l.6)  itthamagniṃ dhyātvā । 

agne tvamaiśvara tejvī(jasvī) pāvanaṃ paramaṃ yataḥ ।
tasmātvadīyahṛtpadme saṃsthāpya tarpayāmyaham ॥

	ityātmaprārthane (p.251a, l.1) agne vigrahamadhyanāḍyāṃ praviśya । pū..... hṛdayapaṅkaje yogapīṭhaṃ vinyasya । śodhyārthvopetā mūrtidvayaṃ saṃkalpya । śivamāvāhyā(l.2)ṅgairavakuṇṭhyāvaraṇapūjāntaṃ karmāṇi nityavannivṛtyā(tya) । dhūpadīpaṃ datvā । śivavaktrāgnivaktrayoḥ sandhānaṃ kṛtvā । ni... mūla(l.3)mantramuccārya vahnisthaśivanāsāvinirgatajyotirūpaśikhā yāgasthaśivaśikhālīnāṃ raśmimātrāviyogenā(na) vicchinnāmubhayatrā(l.4)kūtā svadā(dhā) jyā(dhyā)yet । evaṃ kumbhasthaṇḍilavanhi(hni)sthena śivenātmanā saha nāḍīṃ sandhāya sadāspadaṃ dhyāyet । tataḥ samānaviśeṣa(l.5)tarpaṇāya pratyekaṃ mūlaṃ śatahomaṃ vidhāya brahmāṅgaiśca daśāṃśato hutvā । pūrṇāṃ vidhāya । upaviśya । śivādimantrān (l.6) svāhāntenaikaikāhutidānena santarpya punarājyenāhutitrayeṇa dīpayet । atra prayogaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śivāya svāhā । (p.251b, f.268a, l.1) oṃ hom īśānamūrdhāya svāhā । .... oṃ haṃ sadyojātāya svāhā । ityāditarpaṇe । oṃ hūṃ hauṃ śivāya huṃ phaṭ । oṃ hūṃ (l.2) hoṃ hūṃ īśānamūrdhā(rdhnā)ya huṃ phaṭ । .... oṃ hūṃ haṃ hūṃ sadyojātāya huṃ phaṭ । ityādi dīpane । tataḥ sthālīṃ tāmbramayīṃ vā (l.3) mṛṇmayīṃ vā prāṇādidoṣarahitamanupayuktā astreṇa vāriṇa(ṇā) prakṣālya । kavacenāvakuṇḍyā nirīkṣaṇādi-catuḥ saṃskāraiḥ (l.4) samskṛtya । indanādibhi(bhiḥ) āpaṃ lipya । dhapayitvā । śastrakavacābhi(bhiḥ) japtvā । trisūtri kuśābhyākaṇṭhe saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) । gomaye(l.5)nopaliptamaṇḍale 	ebhiḥ (l.5) samskṛtya saṃpūjya vastreṇācchādyā bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prakāraṃ tritayaṃ bahirvidadhyāt । piṇḍikāyā nālamārabhya – oṃ hāṃ śaktitattvāya namaḥ । (p.258b, f.274a, l.1) śaktitattvādhipataye śaktimataye namaḥ । ityādi pūrvavat । pṛthivyantatattvāni tattvā nāyakāśca vinyasya । trikhaṇḍadarśanārthaṃ (l.2) mālāpratisaraṃ datvā ।

nālabhāge – oṃ hūṃ(hāṃ) śivatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śivatattvādhipataye icchāyai namaḥ ।  śaktitattvāntam । pīṭha(l.3)kaṇṭhordhvabhāge – oṃ hīṃ vidyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hīṃ vidyātattvādhipāya jñānāyai namaḥ । 
sadāśivatattvāntam । pīṭhamūlādārabhya (l.4) kaṇṭhāntarabhāge – oṃ hām ātmatattvāya namaḥ । ā + ye kriyāyai namaḥ । māyātattvāntam । evaṃ vinyaset । yadvā kaṇṭhādhobhāge – (l.5) kaṇṭhe tadūrdhve ca tritatvaṃ vinyaset । tataḥ pratibhāgaṃ pūrvādidikṣu – oṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । kṣmāmūrtyādhipataye dhāriyai? (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ hām arkamūrtaye namaḥ । arkamūrtyādhipataye jyosnā(tsnā)yai namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ jalamūrtyāya namaḥ । ja + yai ugrāyai namaḥ । (p.259a, l.1) oṃ hām agnimūrtaye namaḥ । agnimūrtyādhipāyai dīptinyai namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । yajamānamūrtyādhipāyai cetanāyai (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipāyai pa(ba)lotkaṭāyai namaḥ । oṃ hām indumūrtaye namaḥ । indumūrtyadhipāyai dhātrai(tryai) (l.3) namaḥ । oṃ hām ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । ākāśa-mūrtyā(rtya)dhipāyai vanhai(hnyai) namaḥ । iti vinyasya । nirodhya । oṃ hrīṃ saṃ mahāgaurī (l.4) rudrādhite svāhā । śaktyantamuccāryā-vagāhyā(hya) saṃsthāpya । nirodhayet । evamādhāraśilāyāṃ pīṭhaṃ vinyaset । tatra viśeṣaḥ । (l.5) sadāśivatattvanyāsaṃ śabdacittasadeśāntaṃ trikhaṇḍaṃ śeṣaṃ pīṭhottara(re/raṃ) vinyasya । oṃ sūm ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । ityāvāhyā(hya) rodhayet । (l.6) eṣāṃ bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prākāra-tritayaṃ pratyekaṃ bahirvidadhyāt । paścāt sva sva diksthitalokapālānādhya(rādhya) rakṣārthamadhyeṣya kuṇḍā(p.259b, f.275a, l.1)ntikaṃ vrajet । tatra kuṇḍamadhye parameśvaramekhalāsu nivṛttyādikalācatuṣkaṃ nābhau kriyāśaktiṃ oṣṭhe nādaṃ vinyasya । ghaṭasthaṇḍila(l.2)vanhi(hni)......pena saha nāḍīsandhānaṃ kuryāt ।

anuditena viśantīṃ tāṃ nirgatya mudritena ca ॥ (l.3)
ātmane'nuditenaiva viśantī cintayedyathā ।

tatsandhānārtham āhūtitrayaṃ datvā । kuṇḍe – oṃ hāṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā-(l.4)hutitrayaṃ datvā । tattvatattvā-dhipāṃśca saṃpūjyāhutīnāṃ trayaṃ trayaṃ datvā । mūrtimūrtīśvarān saṃpūjayedyathā – oṃ hāṃ lūṃ kṣmā(l.5)mūrtaye namaḥ । kṣmāmūrtyādhipāya śarvāya namaḥ । oṃ hām agnimūrtaye namaḥ । agnimūrtyādhipāya paśupataye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śūṃ yajamānamūrtaye (l.6) yajamānamūrtyādhipāya ugrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ṣūm arkamūrtaye namaḥ । arkamūrtyādhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ṣūṃ jalamūrtaye (p.260a, l.1) namaḥ । jala-mūrtyādhipāya bhavāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyādipataye īśānāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śūm indumūrtaye (l.2) namaḥ । indumūrtyādhipāya mahādevāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hūṃ khaṃ mūrtaye namaḥ । khaṃ mūrtyādipataye bhīmāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhuti(l.3)trayeṇa santarpya । mūlabrahmāṅganivṛtyekaśataṃ sahasram ardhaṃ vā pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । guru liṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā śāntikumbha-(l.4)jalena prokṣya । mūlamantreṇa kuśamūlena brahmāṅgaṃ saṃspṛśya homasaṅkhyā(saṅkhyayā) japtvā śāntikumbhāmbhasā prokṣya । guruḥ (l.5) paścāt sannadhīṃ gatvā । varmaṇāvakuṇṭhya nirodhya tattvādhipānāmājñāṃ śrāvayet । ātmatattvādhipabrahman yāvaddoṣaiḥ samudthitaṃ (l.6) liṅgaṃ tāvattvayā vai sthātavyaṃ sannidhānataḥ iti । tataḥ guruḥ oṃ hām ātmavidyātattvābhyāṃ namaḥ ।  iti māyāntaṃ nādoccāreṇa (p.260b, f.276a, l.1) saṃpūjayet । oṃ lūṃ lūṃ rūṃ rūṃ śūṃ śūṃ ṣūṃ ṣūṃ pūṃ pūṃ yūṃ yūṃ sūṃ sūṃ hūṃ hūṃ bījāni mūrtitattvayośca mūrtīnāṃ (l.2) ca sandhānaṃ kuryāt । tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । saṃpūjya । sannidhānārthaṃ juhūyāt । tato vidyātattvamīśvaraṃ viṣṇuṃ saṃpūjya । sannidhāpya । (l.3) mūrtimūrtīśvarān saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । saṃhitāyāṃ ca pūrvavat juhuyāt । paścāt liṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā (l.4) pūrvavat śāntimumbhāmbhasā prokṣaṇapurassaraṃ kuśamadhyena mūlamantreṇa .......bhāgaṃ saṃpūjya । homasaṅkhyā(ṅkhyayā) japtvā । śānti(l.5)kumbhā-mbhasā prokṣya । hṛdasya nasī? avakuṇḍanaṃ ca vidhāya । viṣṇumājñāṃ śrāvayet । vidyātattvapade viṣṇo yāvaddoṣaḥ samutthitam । (l.6) liṅgaṃ tattvayātraiva sthātavyaṃ sannidhānata iti । tataḥ oṃ huṃ vidyātattvaśivatattvābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । mūrtīnāṃ ca mūrtipāśca (p.261a, l.1) paścimasandhānaṃ vidadhyāt । tataḥ kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā । virūpamagnāvapi? sandhānāṃ hutvā । śivatattvatadīśvaraṃ rudraṃ saṃpūjya । sannidhāpya । (l.2) mūlamantrasaṃhitāṃ ca pratyekaṃ śatādibhi(bhiḥ) santarpya । guruḥ liṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntijalena saṃprokṣya । mūlena kuśāgreṇa rudrabhāgaṃ (l.3) saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyā(ṅkhyayā) japtvā । saṃprokṣya । mūlena śāntyā(ya)mbhasā prokṣya । hṛdaya....kṛtvāvakuṇṭhya । nirudhya । rudramājñāṃ (l.4) śrāvayet । 

bho bho rudra śivājñā tvā śivatattvapate tvayā ।
yāvaddoṣānvita....ṅgaṃ stheyaṃ tāvadiha prabho ॥ iti ।

	evaṃ (l.5) piṇḍiśilayoḥ ātmavidyāśivatattvāni kriyājñānecchākhyām īsvarīṃ ca kṣmāmūrtyādikā trimūrtīśvaraṃ ca icchāgnau saṃpūjyāhutitrayeṇa santarpya । (l.6) oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । oṃ sūm ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । iti pratyekaṃ śataṃ śatapūrṇāntaṃ hutvā liṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntyā(ya)mbhasā (p.261b, f.277a, l.1) kṣyā(prokṣya) pūrvavat madhyāgre saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyā(ṅkhyayā) japtāvā । tatvayo(yoḥ) mūrtīnā(nāṃ) śuddhānāṃ mūrti paścimasandhānaṃ gatvā । gatyā japtvā (l.2) sandhāyā(ya) gatvā । hutvā । sarvaṃ pūrvavadācaret । tato nādi(ndi)doṣanirhaṇā(rharaṇā)rthaṃ śivenāghoreṇāsinā śataṃ śataṃ pūrṇā(l.3)sahitaṃ hutvā caruṃ datvā । pūrṇāṃ vidhāyā-camanatāmbūlaṃ datvā । śiva...... vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā । baliṃ datvā । śrotra...vadanaṃ (l.4) kṛtvā । sakalīkṛtyā(ya) । śivasamīpaṃ gatvā । śivasya dakṣiṇaśravaṇe bhaganna?...tato mohāt brahmādyāśraṃ? trayo'pi yat kṛta(l.5)karmamayananam । atha kaṃ bhaktavatsala । tatsarvamastu saṃpūrṇa(rṇaṃ) tvatprasādādi........re । oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya namo'stu te (l.6) karma pūrṇamapūrṇaṃ vā svaśaktyāpūrya grā(gṛ)hyatāmiti vijñāpya । jñānaśaktiṃ vinyasya । nirudhyāt । piṇḍikāyāṃ ca bhagavatīmadhā(tha) bhāgatraye'pi (p.162a, l.1) karmanivṛttitaṃ tvatprasādāt saṃpūrṇatāṃ yat । oṃ hrīṃ śaṅkarī pū........................vijñāpya । kriyāśaktiṃ vinyasya । (l.2) nirudhyāt । brahma-śilāyā(yāṃ) sarvakarma pūrya gṛhyatāṃ vijñāpya । oṃ sūṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । ityādhāraśakti(ktiṃ) nirudhyāt । (l.3) śaktitaḥ saptarātryā vā pañcarātryā vā trirātrakam ekarātramathā(tha) sadya(dyaḥ) kuryādato'dhivāsakam । hotavyaṃ pratyahaṃ sva sva mantrai(ntraiḥ) (l.4) homaśataṃ śatam । śivakumbhapūjāṃ ca śivakumbhapūjāṃ ca digbaliṃ ca nivedayet । pañcagavyādikaṃ pra....spaśet? । kriyākāṇḍe bahudivasapakṣeme (l.5) dhyāśana(dhyāna)mantrasvapediti pratiṣṭhādhivāsavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ।

	atha prātaḥ kṛtanityavidhikāryadvārapālabhūta(l.6)śuddhiantaryā(rya)janaviśeṣārghyadravyābhimantreṇa śivahastaśivabhāvanālokapālaśiva-kumbhavardhanīgaṇapatigurusthaṇḍila(p.262b, f.278a, l.1)pūjā(jāṃ) kṛtvā । liṅgam aṣṭapuṣpikā(ka)yābhyarcya । piṇḍikāyāṃ brahmaśilāyāṃ saṃpūjya । va(a)gnau pūrvavat śivaṃ saṃpūjya । mantra(l.2)tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā । piṇḍikāyāṃ mantrāṇāṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ vidadhyāt । adīkṣitastu yat kartā dīkṣaye mahodhyare carupākaṃ (l.3) kṛtvā nivedyā(dya) baliṃ vidhāya । hṛdayena liṅgamuddhṛtya snānavedikāyāṃ samāropya vajraṃ be(ba)ndhanakāle (l.4) sulagne manasā brahmaśilāratnāni veśanapiṇḍikāyojanaṃ vidhāya । tataḥ śāntikumbhasārdhena pañcagavyādibhiḥ śilpādi(l.5)sparśātya(dya)rthaṃ hṛda(de) saṃsthāpya । oṃ hrīṃ hauṃ kriyāśaktiśivābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti liṃ(liṅga)mudrayā pīṭhaliṅge babhukṣo? mumukṣostu – (l.6) oṃ hauṃ hrīṃ śivaśaktibhyāṃ namaḥ । iti liṃ(liṅga)pīṭhe saṃspṛśya । adhvaṣaṭkaṃ nyastvā ।

(kalādhvā -)
rudrabhāge – oṃ hauṃ śāntyātītāyai (p.263a, l.1) namaḥ । viṣṇubhāge – oṃ hyaiṃ śāntikalāyai namaḥ । brahmabhāge – oṃ hrūṃ vidyākalāyai namaḥ । oṃ hvīṃ pratiṣṭhākalāyai (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ nivṛttikalāyai namaḥ । iti kalādhvā ।

(tattvādhvā -)
rudrabhāge – oṃ hāṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śaktitattvāya namaḥ । viṣṇubhāge – (l.3) oṃ hāṃ sadāśivatattvāya namaḥ । īśvaratattvāya namaḥ । śuddhavidyātattvāya namaḥ । brahmabhāge – māyātattvāya namaḥ । śuddhatattvāya namaḥ । (l.4) niyatitattvāya namaḥ । kalātattvāya namaḥ । rāgatattvāya namaḥ । puruṣatattvāya namaḥ । prakṛtittvāya namaḥ । guṇatattvāya namaḥ । buddhitattvāya (l.5) namaḥ । ahaṅkāratattvāya namaḥ । manastattvāya namaḥ । śrotratattvāya namaḥ । tvaktattvāya namaḥ । cakṣu(cakṣus)tattvāya namaḥ । jihvātattvāya namaḥ । (l.6) ghrāṇatattvāya namaḥ । vāktattvāya namaḥ । pāṇitattvāya namaḥ । pādatattvāya namaḥ । pāyutattvāya namaḥ । upasthatattvāya namaḥ । śabdatattvāya namaḥ । (p.263b, f.279a, l.1) sparśatattvāya namaḥ । rūpatattvāya namaḥ । rasatattvāya namaḥ । gandhatattvāya namaḥ । ākāśatattvāya namaḥ । vāyutattvāya namaḥ । agnitattvāya namaḥ । (l.2) jalatattvāya namaḥ । pṛthvītattvāya namaḥ । iti tattvādhvā ।

(tattvabhuvanāni -)
rudrabhāge – oṃ hām anāśra(śri)tāya namaḥ । oṃ hām anāthāya namaḥ । oṃ hām anantāya namaḥ । (l.3) vyomarūpiṇyai namaḥ । vyomavyāpinyai namaḥ । iti tattvabhuvanāni ।

(bindukalābhuvanāni -)
oṃ hāṃ ūddhitāminyai namaḥ । mocikāyai namaḥ । recikāya(yai) namaḥ । (l.4) dīpikāyai namaḥ । indhikāyai namaḥ । nādakalā.....ni oṃ hāṃ śāntyātītāya namaḥ । śāntyai namaḥ । vidyāyai namaḥ । pratiṣṭhāyai (l.5) namaḥ । nivṛtyai namaḥ । iti bindukalābhuvanāni ।

(śuddhavidyātattvabhuvanāni -)
viṣṇubhāge – oṃ hāṃ sadāśivāya namaḥ । sadāśivatattvāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.6) śikhaṇḍine namaḥ । śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ । trimūrtaye namaḥ । ekarudrāya namaḥ । ekanetrāya namaḥ । śivottamāya namaḥ । sūkṣmāya namaḥ । anantāya (p.264a, l.1) namaḥ । īśvaratattvajīvanam । oṃ hāṃ manonmanyai namaḥ । sarvabhūtadamanyai namaḥ । balapramathanyai namaḥ । balavikaraṇyai namaḥ । (l.2) kalavikaraṇyai namaḥ । kālyai namaḥ । raudryai namaḥ । jyeṣṭhāyai namaḥ । vāmāyai namaḥ । śuddhavidyātattvabhuvanāni ।

brahmabhāge – (l.3) vidyākalāyām – oṃ hām aṅguṣṭhamātrāya namaḥ । bhuvaneśāya namaḥ । īśānāya namaḥ । ekekṣaṇāya namaḥ । ekapiṅgalāya (l.4) namaḥ । udbhavāya namaḥ । bhavāya namaḥ । vāmadevāya namaḥ । mahātejase namaḥ ।
